执行摘要Executive summary
各组织正在迅速部署人工智能（AI）智能体，既用于提高内部生产力，也用于承担面向客户的工作。 Organisations are rapidly deploying artificial intelligence (AI) agents, both for internal productivity and in customer-facing roles. 随着合作伙伴、客户和供应商也采取同样的做法，组织的智能体将日益跨越组织边界，与已知和未知的交互方开展互动。这些互动带来了新的安全与治理挑战：智能体之间的互动可能引发任何单一组织的控制措施都无法覆盖的失效。 As their partners, customers and suppliers do the same, an organisation's agents will increasingly interact across organisational boundaries, with counterparties both known and unknown .These interactions create new safety and governance challenges: failures can emerge from interactions between agents that no single organisation's controls can reach.
本报告提出了一个分析框架，旨在帮助实践者、政策制定者和研究人员在智能体互动超出内部部署范围、开始跨越组织边界时，分析风险、控制措施与治理问题。 This report introduces an analytical framework to help practitioners, policymakers, and researchers reason about risk, controls and governance as agent interactions go beyond internal deployments and start crossing organisational boundaries.
具体而言，本报告将当前最先进的研究与实践提炼为一个围绕 3 个部署层级构建的框架，各层级的区分依据是：对于相互交互的智能体，可以假定其至少具备何种程度的共同治理： Specifically, it distils the current state of the art into a framework built around 3 tiers of deployment , distinguished by the minimum level of common governance that can be assumed between interacting agents:
• 单一治理：由一个组织治理系统中的所有智能体，并可单方面对整个系统实施管控。 • Singular governance : one organisation governs every agent in the system and has unilateral reach over the whole.
• 联合治理：多个组织依据一套商定的规则，在共享环境中进行部署。 • Federated governance : multiple organisations deploy into a shared environment under an agreed set of rules. 各组织均不再能够单方面实施全面管控，激励不一致会引发新的失效。 Each loses unilateral reach, and new failures emerge from misaligned incentives.
• 开放环境：持续运行的智能体通过公共基础设施运作，不存在中央治理机构。 • Open environments : persistent agents operate through public infrastructure with no central governing authority. 即使存在治理，它也是以多中心的方式涌现的，而失效则会在群体规模上出现。 Governance, where it exists, emerges polycentrically, and failures appear at population scale.
核心观点是：可用的控制措施及能够实施这些措施的主体，取决于部署层级。 The overarching message is that the controls available, and who can action them, depend on the deployment tier. 每进入下一个层级，都会带来新的失效模式，而控制措施也逐步超出部署组织的管控范围，转向共享框架、公共基础设施和集体行动。 Each subsequent tier brings new Failure mode s, and the controls move progressively beyond the deploying organisation's reach, toward shared frameworks, public infrastructure and collective action. 本报告考察了选定的风险因素、失效模式和控制措施，涵盖智能体系统及其相关的关键治理实践，并逐一指出哪些主体具备采取行动的条件。 The report examines selected Risk factor s, failure modes and controls, spanning both agentic systems and key governance practices around them, and identifies in every case who is positioned to act.
• 对部署组织而言，该框架是一种风险分流与决策工具：确定现有或拟议系统所处的层级，识别适用于该层级风险的控制措施，决定是否部署以及在部署时采用哪些控制措施，持续开展治理，并认清单方面管控的边界。 • For deploying organisations, the framework is a triage and decision instrument: identify the tier an existing or proposed system operates at, the controls applicable to that tier's risks, decide whether to deploy and if so which controls to apply, govern continuously, and recognise where unilateral reach ends.
• 对政策制定者而言，本报告提供了一份指引：针对每项风险，指出谁能够采取行动；对于没有任何现有主体负责应对的风险，则描述其中的治理空白。 • For policymakers, the report offers a map: for each risk it identifies who can act, and for the risks that fall to no existing actor it characterises the gap.
• 对研究人员和标准制定机构而言，本报告揭示了控制措施所依赖的方法与基础设施方面的缺口，涵盖从多智能体评估到智能体身份标准的各个方面。 • For researchers and Standards bodies , it surfaces methodological and infrastructural gaps on which controls depend, from multi-agent Evaluation to agent identity standards.
大规模运行的多智能体 AI 系统的安全，将建立在所有这些群体的共同努力之上。 The safety of multi-agent AI systems operating at scale will be built on the combined efforts of all these communities. 本报告旨在为这些群体提供一个共同框架，以应对未来的挑战。 This report aims to give each of these communities a shared framework for the challenges ahead. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 8 > | industry .gov.au 8
1 引言1 Introduction
通用 AI 智能体已经到来。 General-purpose AI agents are here. 各组织已经部署基于大语言模型（LLMs）的智能体，用于处理文档、响应客户咨询、检索知识库，以及在生产代码库中开发代码。 Organisations already deploy agents based on large language models (LLMs) to process documents, service customer queries, navigate knowledge bases and develop code in production codebases. 随着 LLM 能力的提升以及配套协议和基础设施的成熟，智能体开始跨业务部门或跨组织开展交互，其中一些还在开放互联网上与未知的交互方互动。 As LLM capability grows and the surrounding protocols and infrastructure mature, agents are beginning to interact across business units or across organisations, and some are interacting with unknown counterparties on the open internet. 这些交互带来了诸多挑战，必须解决这些挑战，才能确保智能体安全、可靠地运行。 These interactions create challenges that must be addressed for their safe and reliable operation. 本报告考察了这一范围内的 3 个智能体交互层级，阐明了各层级的治理需求、风险和控制措施。 This report examines 3 tiers of agent interactions across this spectrum, setting out governance needs, risks and controls at each.
1.1 研究动机1.1 Motivation
各组织已经开始在生产环境中部署 AI 智能体，而部署的发展趋势指向由多个智能体构成的系统，这些智能体彼此互操作、相互协调，并且日益跨越组织边界。 Organisations are already deploying AI agents into production, and the deployment trajectory points towards systems of agents interoperating and coordinating with each other, and increasingly across organisational boundaries.
一个组织的智能体每跨越一道边界，对其进行治理的要求就会发生变化。 Each boundary crossed by an organisation’s agents changes what governing them requires. 一个组织可能运行一条内部理赔处理流水线，在其中控制每个智能体，并能够单方面治理由这些智能体组成的系统。 An organisation might run an internal claims-processing pipeline where they control every agent and can govern the composed system of agents unilaterally. 组织也可能将其智能体部署到与其他组织共享的平台或市场中，接受共同治理框架的约束。 They might deploy their agent into a platform or marketplace shared with other organisations under a common governance framework. 或者，组织可能将智能体部署在开放互联网上，依赖其无法控制的公共基础设施。 Or they may deploy agents on the open internet, relying on public infrastructure they do not control. 在第一种情形下足够有效的治理方式并不能直接适用于其他情形：新的失效模式会出现，而针对这些失效模式的控制措施则超出了部署组织的掌控范围。 The governance that suffices in the first case does not carry over to the others: new failure modes appear, and the controls for them move beyond the reach of the deploying organisation.
在这些不同环境中实现安全运行，障碍越来越多地在于治理准备是否充分：组织的治理能力能否跟上智能体相互交互所带来的复杂性、规模和涌现风险，尤其是在跨组织边界交互的情况下。 The barrier to operating safely across this landscape is increasingly one of governance readiness: whether an organisation's governance capabilities can keep pace with the complexity, scale, and emergent risks that arise from agents interacting with each other, especially across organisational boundaries.
这些需求不仅关乎业务，也关乎 AI 安全，这一点如今已体现在国际科学共识之中。 These needs are not only a business concern but an AI safety concern, a point now reflected in the international scientific consensus. 《International AI Safety Report 2026》是一份得到包括澳大利亚在内的 30 多个国家支持、就通用 AI 能力与风险形成共识的评估报告，其第二版大幅扩充了对 AI 智能体的论述，指出智能体失效会带来独特的安全风险，因为当问题发生时，人类介入的机会更少，而多个智能体之间的交互还会引入额外风险。 The International AI Safety Report 2026 , the consensus assessment of general-purpose AI capabilities and risks backed by over 30 countries including Australia, substantially expanded its treatment of AI agents in its second edition, finding that agent failures pose distinctive safety risks because humans have fewer opportunities to intervene when things go wrong, and that interactions between multiple agents introduce further risks. 1 随着高能力智能体不断增多并相互交互，挑战在于如何对复杂系统保持有效的人类监督与控制，因为这些系统的行为越来越多地由智能体之间的交互塑造，而非由任何单个智能体独自决定。 1 As capable agents proliferate and interact, the challenge is to preserve meaningful human Oversight and control over complex systems whose behaviour is increasingly shaped by interactions between agents rather than by any single agent individually.
本报告考察的许多失效模式，都是 AI 安全研究中大量研究所关注的功能失常问题在系统层面的对应形式：智能体通过隐写式协调规避监测，监督能力在人类来得及介入之前便已饱和，智能体之间的通信逐渐超出人类可理解的范围，多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Many of the failure modes examined in this report are system-level analogues of malfunction concerns that motivate much of AI safety research: agents that evade monitoring through steganographic coordination, oversight that saturates faster than humans can intervene, inter-agent communication that drifts beyond human legibility, Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 9 > | industry .gov.au 9
以及智能体群体进行自我复制、控制共享基础设施，或获得任何单个智能体都不具备、且未经任何委托方（Principal）批准的集体能力。 and agent populations that replicate, capture shared infrastructure, or acquire collective capabilities that no individual agent possesses and no Principal sanctioned.
因此，随着智能体系统规模扩大，对智能体交互实施良好治理，是维持有效的人类监督与控制的前提。 Good governance of agent interactions is therefore a precondition for maintaining meaningful human oversight and control as agentic systems scale. 本报告提出了支持实现这一目标的控制措施与实践方法。 This report sets out controls and practices to support that goal.
1.2 目的与受众1.2 Purpose and audience
本报告面向 3 类受众：部署 AI 智能体的组织中的从业人员、AI 政策制定者，以及 AI 安全研究界，包括将研究成果转化为共同实践的标准制定机构。 The report addresses 3 audiences: practitioners in AI-agent deploying organisations, AI policymakers, and the AI safety research community, including the standards bodies that translate research into shared practice.
本报告旨在将基于 LLM 的多智能体系统治理领域的当前最先进成果提炼为一个分析框架，并围绕 3 个部署层级组织该框架；这些层级依据约束交互智能体的最低限度共同治理来界定（第 3.3 节）。 Its purpose is to distil the current state of the art on governing LLM-based Multi-agent system s into an analytical framework, structured around 3 tiers of deployment defined by the minimum common governance binding interacting agents (Section 3.3). 该框架对各类受众具有不同的用途： The framework serves each audience differently:
• 部署组织可以利用该框架识别其各个系统所处的层级，选择与该层级风险相匹配的控制措施；同样重要的是，组织还可以据此认识到其单方面控制所能触及的边界，以及其治理在何处依赖共同协议和自身无法控制的公共基础设施。 • Deploying organisations can use it to identify the tier each of their systems operates at, select controls matched to that tier's risks, and, just as importantly, recognise where their unilateral reach ends and their governance depends on shared agreements and public infrastructure they do not control.
• 本报告为政策制定者提供了一幅指引图：报告识别了个别组织和自愿联盟均无力弥合的协调缺口，并描述了各类缺口的性质，为评估哪些领域可能有必要进行系统性干预提供技术依据。 • Policymakers are offered a map: the report locates the coordination gaps that neither individual organisations nor voluntary coalitions are positioned to close, and characterises the nature of each, as technical grounding for assessing where systemic intervention may be warranted.
• 研究人员和标准制定机构会发现，分析贯穿揭示了本报告所提控制措施本身所依赖的方法和基础设施方面的缺口：从失效模式文献中的证据缺口，到依赖当前模型某些特性、而这些特性并不保证会持续存在的监督技术。 • Researchers and standards bodies will find the methodological and infrastructural gaps on which the report's own controls depend surfaced throughout the analysis: from evidence gaps in the failure-mode literature to oversight techniques resting on properties of current models that are not guaranteed to persist. 各层级末尾的“开放问题”小节（4.4、5.4、6.4）汇总了其中最重要的问题。 The ‘open problems’ sections closing each tier (4.4, 5.4, 6.4) consolidate the most significant of these.
这些贡献相互依存：组织影响范围的边界正是关键政策与研究缺口所在之处，而本报告的结构旨在使这一边界在每个层级上都清晰可见。 These contributions are interdependent: the boundary of organisational reach is where key policy and research gaps sit, and the report is structured to make that boundary visible at each tier.
1.3 范围1.3 Scope
本报告的范围如下。 The report has the following scope.
• 组织治理：本报告为部署智能体或与智能体交互的组织提供实用指导，帮助其理解治理需求。 • Organisational governance : The report provides practical guidance for organisations deploying or interacting with agents to understand governance needs.
• 为政策制定者提供参考：本报告识别市场和自愿性标准未能弥合的协调缺口，为评估哪些领域可能有必要进行系统性干预提供依据；本报告不提出政策建议。 • Informing policymakers : The report locates coordination gaps that markets and voluntary standards fail to close, as input to assessments of where systemic intervention may be warranted; it does not make policy recommendations. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 10 > | industry .gov.au 10
• 专门针对基于 LLM 的智能体：讨论以当前基于 LLM 的智能体的特征为基础。 • LLM-based agent s specifically : Discussion is grounded in the characteristics of current LLM-based agents. 该框架或许可以推广至其他智能体式 AI 架构（例如狭义强化学习场景），但本报告并不主张其具有这种适用性。 The framework may generalise to other agentic AI architectures (such as narrow reinforcement learning settings) but does not claim to.
• 专门针对多智能体系统：我们的分析聚焦于 2 个或更多 AI 智能体之间的交互所引发的失效模式。 • Multi-agent systems specifically : Our analysis focuses on failure modes arising from interactions between 2 or more AI agents. 单智能体失效模式被视为背景，但并非分析对象。 Single-agent failure modes are taken as context but not the subject of analysis.
• 治理假设：假定交互中至少有一个智能体受某个组织治理。 • Assumed governance : At least one agent in the interaction is assumed to be governed by an organisation. 本报告不直接分析消费者之间的智能体交互，但如果共享环境中消费者部署的智能体会影响组织智能体所面临的风险格局，则将其存在视为重要的背景因素。 Consumer-to-consumer agent interactions are not analysed directly, though the presence of consumer-deployed agents in shared environments is treated as an important contextual factor where it shapes the risk landscape for organisational agents. 我们不考察人类委托人未遵守约定治理规则所导致的失效。 We do not examine failures where a human principal fails to abide by agreed governance.
• 智能体之间的交互：智能体与人类之间的交互并非本报告的主要关注点；我们的分析以智能体之间的交互为中心。 • Agent-to-agent interactions : Agent-to-human interactions are not the primary focus of this report; our analysis centres on agent-to-agent interactions. 然而，对某些系统而言，交互另一端是人类这一假设构成重要的基准，而这一假设的弱化属于本报告的讨论范围。 However, the assumption that a human is on the other side of an interaction serves as an important baseline for some systems, and the erosion of that assumption is in scope.
• 有所选择而非穷尽列举：本报告依据现有证据权衡取舍，选取具有突出意义的风险因素、失效模式和控制措施加以介绍，而非提供完整目录。 • Selective rather than exhaustive : the report presents a salient selection of risk factors, failure modes and controls, weighted by current evidence, rather than a complete catalogue.
本报告围绕多智能体治理的 3 个层级展开，这些层级将在第 3 节介绍。 The report is organised around 3 tiers of multi-agent governance, introduced in Section 3. 由于风险、控制措施和治理实践在各层级间逐层累积，本报告最好按顺序阅读。 Because the risks, controls and governance practices are cumulative across the tiers, the report is best read in sequence.

AI 研究与实践者群体中使用以下方式的既有惯例： established practice in the AI research and practitioner community of using

2 基础2 Foundations
2.1 基于 LLM 的智能体2.1 LLM-based agents
什么是基于 LLM 的智能体？ What is an LLM-based agent? 本报告聚焦于基于 LLM 的智能体：这类系统由以指令为输入的大语言模型（LLM），以及配套的运行框架（Harness）和支撑结构（Scaffold）组成，后两者使包含 3 个步骤的智能体循环（Agentic loop）得以运行： In this report, we are focused on LLM-based agents: systems consisting of a large language model (LLM) that takes instructions as input, together with a surrounding Harness and Scaffold that enable an Agentic loop with 3 steps:
规划如何推进任务 Plan how to make progress on the task
使用可用工具和行动作用于环境 Act on the environment using the tools and actions available
观察这些行动的结果，然后重复上述过程。 Observe the outcome of those actions, repeat .
图 1：LLM 智能体的智能体循环（agentic loop）。 Figure 1: The agentic loop of an LLM agent.
正是这种智能体循环，使智能体区别于 LLM 工作流或提示词。 It is this agentic loop that distinguishes an agent from an LLM workflow or prompt. 这一循环使智能体能够分多个步骤解决问题、尝试不同策略，并根据结果调整策略。 The loop allows the agent to solve problems in multiple steps, try different strategies, and adapt its strategy in response to the outcomes. 2 就本报告而言，如果一个系统不具备智能体循环，我们就不将其视为 AI 智能体。 2 If a system does not have an agentic loop, we do not consider it an AI agent for the purposes of this report.
智能体由模型、运行框架（harness）和脚手架（scaffold）构成（图 1）。 An agent is built from a model, harness, and scaffold (Figure 1). 运行框架是实现智能体循环的软件组件。 The harness is the software component that facilitates the agentic loop. 在循环中，模型负责围绕任务进行规划和推理，而运行框架则将模型的输出转化为行动，并将这些行动的结果重新转化为输入。 Within the loop the model does the planning and reasoning about the task, while the harness translates the model's outputs into actions, and translates the outcomes of those actions back into inputs. 运行框架还可以设置审批检查点，以管控智能体的自主性（Autonomy）：即其能够独立决定并执行行动的程度。 The harness may also enable approval checkpoints that govern the agent's Autonomy :the degree to which it can decide on and execute actions independently. 脚手架是使模型能够访问特定工具、模块和服务的附加软件。 The scaffold is additional software that gives the model access to specific tools, modules, and services. 一些脚手架扩展了模型可采取的行动范围，另一些脚手架，如记忆模块和规划模块，则旨在激发更强的性能，来自 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Some scaffolds expand the actions a model can take, while others, like memory and planning modules, are designed to elicit stronger performance from the Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 12 > | industry .gov.au 12
模型。 model. 在底层模型支持的情况下，脚手架还可以提供对计算机图形用户界面等非文本环境的访问，使模型能够在这些环境中进行感知和行动。 Where the underlying model supports it, scaffolding can also provide access to non-text environments such as computer graphical user interfaces, allowing the model to perceive and act in them.
智能体能力为何迅速提升。 Why agentic capability is improving rapidly . 围绕语言模型组装运行框架和脚手架，是构建胜任任务的 LLM 智能体的必要条件，但并非充分条件。 Assembling a harness and scaffold around a language model is necessary but not sufficient to build a competent LLM-based agent. 在目标驱动的行为中表现出色，还取决于 LLM 本身所提供的能力。 Performing well at goal-driven behaviours also depends on capability drawn from the LLM. 如今投入生产使用的智能体，其智能体能力在很大程度上得益于模型训练机构内部开展的针对性开发：利用可验证奖励强化学习（reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards，RLVR）3 等技术，提高 LLM 制订多步骤计划，以及利用工具在真实数字环境中采取有意义的行动并作出响应的能力。 The agents now appearing in production owe much of their agentic capability to targeted development inside the organisations that train the models: using techniques such as reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) 3 to improve the ability of LLMs to construct multi-step plans and use tools to take and respond to meaningful actions in real digital environments.
因此，自 2024 年末以来，LLM 智能体的智能体能力一直在迅速提升，尤其是在软件工程领域，如下图（图 2）所示。 For this reason, the agentic capabilities of LLM agents have been rapidly increasing since late 2024, particularly in the software engineering domain as illustrated by the plot below (Figure 2). 随着模型的进步，日益成熟的运行框架，如 Anthropic 的 Claude Code、OpenAI 的 Codex，乃至 OpenClaw 等开源运行框架与脚手架组合，也在从日益强大的模型中激发出更强的能力。 Alongside model progress, increasingly sophisticated harnesses such as Anthropic's Claude Code, OpenAI's Codex, and even open-source harness and scaffold combinations such as OpenClaw are eliciting greater capability from increasingly powerful models.
图 2：通用前沿模型智能体能够以 80% 的成功率自主完成的任务时长（主要为软件工程领域的任务），以人类专业人员完成这些任务所需的时间衡量。 Figure 2: The length of tasks (primarily in the software engineering domain) that generalist frontier model agents can complete autonomously with an 80% success rate, measured by how long the tasks take human professionals to complete.
> 来源：METR 4多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 > Source: METR 4Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 13 > | industry .gov.au 13
需要强调的是，软件工程在一定程度上属于特例，而非普遍情况，因为它具备使智能体能够良好运作的特性：输出可以通过程序验证，错误能够迅速显现，因此反馈信号非常适合用于模型训练。 It should be emphasised that software engineering is somewhat the exception rather than the rule, because it has the property that makes agents work well: outputs are programmatically verifiable and errors surface quickly, so the feedback signal is well suited to model training. 缺乏即时可验证性的领域，是目前智能体面临最大困难的领域，例如法律领域，其中的验证需要具备资质的专业人士进行评估；以及长期规划领域，其中没有可用于检验计划最优性的基准真值，而且早期错误会在后续每一步中不断累积放大。 Domains lacking immediate verifiability are where agents currently struggle the most, such as law, where verification requires a qualified practitioner's assessment, and long-horizon planning, where there is no ground truth to check the optimality of a plan and early errors compound over every subsequent step.
能力参差不齐，不可靠性影响部署意愿。 Capabilities are jagged, and unreliabilities affect deployment appetite . 尽管能力不断提升，与人类相比，LLM 智能体的能力分布仍然呈现“参差不齐”的特点，即它们在某些领域能力很强，而在其他领域则相对较弱。 Despite the advancing capability, LLM agents still exhibit ‘jagged’ capability profiles compared to humans, meaning they are highly capable in some domains, and less so in others. 5 5
虽然能力通常被衡量为在给定条件下取得成功的能力，但在许多情境中，最重要的是可靠性，即在反复试验中持续取得成功的能力。 And while capability is often measured as the ability to succeed under given conditions, in many contexts it is reliability – the ability to succeed consistently across repeated trials – that matters most. LLM 的不可靠性可能以不同于人类的方式表现出来：它们容易编造答案、在较长的上下文中偏离任务、对提示词措辞的细微变化十分敏感，并且经常错误表述自身的置信程度，等等。 LLMs can be unreliable in different ways to humans: they are prone to fabricating answers, losing track of the task over longer contexts, sensitive to small changes in how a prompt is worded, and frequently mis-stating confidence, among others. 要对这些不可靠性进行科学刻画，仍然十分困难。 These unreliabilities remain scientifically difficult to characterise.
LLM 智能体也并非 AI 智能体的唯一形式：自动驾驶汽车、国际象棋引擎和基于规则的自动化系统都具有智能体循环。 LLM agents are also not the only form of AI agent : self-driving cars, chess engines, and rule-based automation systems all have agentic loops. 然而，LLM 智能体凭借其通用能力，有别于这些用途较为狭窄的系统，开辟了机遇与风险的新前沿。 However, LLM agents represent a departure from these narrower systems because of their general-purpose capabilities, opening a new frontier of opportunity and risk.

2.2 多智能体系统2.2 Multi-agent systems
多智能体系统是指有多个智能体运行，且这些智能体能够影响彼此的行为或所知信息的系统。 A multi-agent system is one in which more than one agent operates, and those agents can affect what each other does or knows. 图 3 展示了二者的区别：单个智能体独立行动，而多智能体系统中的智能体之间至少存在一条连接。 Figure 3 shows the contrast: a single agent acts in isolation, while a multi-agent system has at least one connection between agents.
图 3：单智能体系统（左）与其他智能体之间没有连接，而多智能体系统（右）由 2 个或更多通过连接组成网络的智能体构成。 Figure 3: A single agent system (left) has no links to other agents, while a multi-agent system (right) consists of 2 or more agents that are linked into a network.
它们如何连接：图 3 中的连接（Link）代表一个智能体影响另一个智能体的任何渠道。 How they’re connected: The Link in Figure 3 stands for any channel through which one agent affects another. 这种渠道可以是结构化的 API 调用、自然语言消息，也可以是共同读写文件系统。 It might be a structured API call, a natural-language message, or communally reading from and writing to a file system. 具体渠道会改变工程实现细节，但不会改变核心思想：智能体 A 的行为可能依赖于智能体 B，反之亦然。 The specific channel changes the engineering details but not the core idea: Agent A's behaviour can depend on agent B, and vice versa.
正是这种耦合，使多智能体系统有别于分别运行多个智能体。 This coupling is what makes a multi-agent system different from running multiple agents separately. 每个智能体的决策都受其他智能体决策的制约，或会影响其他智能体的决策，而系统整体表现出的行为，无法仅凭任何单个智能体的设计得到充分预测。 Each agent's decisions are conditioned on, or influence, the decisions of others, and the system as a whole exhibits behaviours that no individual agent's design fully predicts.
实际部署很少像图 3 中的示例那样简单。 Real deployments are rarely as simple as the example in Figure 3. 智能体的数量可以从 2 个到很多个不等。 The number of agents can range from 2 to many. 系统也不必由独立设计的智能体组成。 The system need not be composed of independently designed agents either. 同一套智能体规格可用于在系统内运行该设计的多个 The same specification of an agent can be used to run multiple
实例。 instances of that design within the system. 每个运行中的实例（Instance）都是一个具有自身上下文的智能体，并作为独立实体行动。 Each running Instance is an agent with its own context and behaves as a distinct entity. 图 4 展示了一个复杂系统的示例：不同组织内部的多个智能体、跨越组织边界的连接，以及与开放网络的连接。 Figure 4 shows a complex system example: multiple agents within different organisations, links across organisational boundaries, and connections to the open web. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 15 > | industry .gov.au 15
图 4：一个复杂多智能体系统的示例，其智能体网络跨越多个组织边界，并包含网络上的智能体。 Figure 4: An example of a complex multi-agent system with a network of agents spanning multiple organisational boundaries and agents on the web.
连接的存在以底层媒介（Substrate）为前提。 Links presuppose a Substrate . 图 4 展示了组织内部、跨越组织边界以及通向开放网络的连接。 Figure 4 shows links within organisations, across organisational boundaries, and into the open web. 这里的每一种连接都以某种共享媒介为前提，正是这种媒介使连接成为可能，例如网络、通信协议或共享文件系统。 Each kind of link here presupposes some shared medium that makes it possible: a network, a communication protocol, a shared file system. 我们将这种媒介称为底层媒介，第 3 节将详细阐述为何治理必须像考虑其上的连接一样，也考虑这一媒介本身。 We call that medium the substrate, and Section 3 develops why it, as much as the links on it, is something governance has to account for. 可以在底层媒介之上构建智能体基础设施，以支持智能体之间的协调与互操作，提供身份注册、共享日志记录等功能， Infrastructure for agents may be built onto the substrate to support agent coordination and interoperation, providing functionality like identity registries, shared logging,
声誉系统和支付系统。 Reputation system s, and payment systems.
可采用的治理方式取决于一条连接跨越哪些边界。 The governance available depends on which boundaries a link crosses . 同一组织内部的连接完全受该组织控制。 A link inside one organisation is fully under that organisation's control. 跨组织的连接可以通过合同或平台条款进行治理。 A link across organisations can be governed by contract or platform terms. 与开放网络上的智能体建立的连接可能没有任何治理机制作为支撑。 A link to an open-web agent may have no governance behind it. 每条连接跨越何种边界，是治理的核心变量，第 3 节将阐述本报告用于分析这些边界的框架。 Which boundary each link crosses is the central variable for governance, and Section 3 develops the framework this report uses to reason about them.
2.3 分析多智能体系统中的风险与控制措施2.3 Reasoning about risk and controls in multi-agent systems
为分析多智能体系统如何失效以及可以采取哪些应对措施，本报告采用一个因果模型，追踪部署条件如何导致失效和损害，以及干预措施可以在哪些环节发挥作用。 To reason about how multi-agent systems fail and what can be done about it, this report uses a causal model that traces how conditions of a deployment lead to failures and harms, and where interventions can act. 该模型基于以下术语构建： The model is built on the following vocabulary:
• 风险因素（risk factor）是部署中会提高失效发生可能性的条件。 • A risk factor is a condition of the deployment that raises the likelihood of a failure occurring. 风险因素会促成失效，但其本身并不是失效。 Risk factors contribute to failures but are not failures. 一个组织即使存在风险因素，仍然可以成功部署。 An organisation can have a risk factor and still deploy successfully. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 16 > | industry .gov.au 16
• 失效模式（failure mode）是指一个或多个风险因素通过特定机制发挥作用，导致多智能体系统失效的具体方式。 • A failure mode is a specific way the multi-agent system can fail as a result of one or more risk factors activating through a particular Mechanism .
• 预防性控制措施（Prevention control）作用于从风险因素到失效的路径，既可以直接处理风险因素，也可以阻断风险因素导致失效的机制。 • A Prevention control acts on the pathway from risk factors to failure, either by addressing the risk factors directly or by interrupting the mechanism through which they produce a failure.
• 恢复性控制措施（Recovery control）在失效已经发生后发挥作用，检测失效并限制由此产生的后果。 • A Recovery control acts on the failure once it is underway, detecting it and limiting the resulting Consequence .
• 保障活动（Assurance activities）检查上述预防性控制措施和恢复性控制措施是否按预期发挥作用，关注的是控制措施的可靠性，而非直接关注智能体的可靠性。 • An Assurance activities y checks whether the prevention and recovery controls above are working as intended, addressing the reliability of the controls, rather than the reliability of the agents directly.
• 基础性控制措施（Foundational control）提供其他控制措施所依赖的基础设施（身份标识、日志记录、共享记录）。 • A Foundational control provides the infrastructure (identity, logging, shared records) that other controls depend on.
图 5：从风险因素到失效模式再到后果的因果路径，其中预防性控制措施位于风险因素与失效模式之间，恢复性控制措施位于失效模式与后果之间。 Figure 5: Causal pathway from risk factors to failure modes to consequences, with prevention controls sitting between risk factors and failure modes, and recovery controls sitting between failure modes and consequences. 基础性控制措施为预防和恢复提供支持。 Foundational controls support prevention and recovery. 保障活动检验控制措施是否按预期发挥作用。 Assurance activities test the controls are working as intended.
图 5 以因果图的形式呈现了这些变量之间的基本关系。 The fundamental relationships between these variables are expressed in terms of a causal graph in Figure 5. 我们指出，一个风险因素可能导致多种失效模式，而一种失效模式也可能由多个风险因素引起。 We note that one risk factor can cause multiple failure modes, and one failure mode can be due to multiple risk factors. 同样，一种失效模式可以有多项控制措施，而一项控制措施也可以应对多种失效模式。 Likewise, one failure mode can have multiple controls, and one control can address multiple failure modes. 在整个分析过程中，我们区分了谁能够实施这些控制措施，即这些措施是否在部署智能体的组织能力范围内，还是需要该组织之外的协调行动。 Throughout our analysis, we distinguish who can action these controls, whether they are within reach of an agent-deploying organisation or require coordinated action beyond it. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 17 > | industry .gov.au 17
如何理解本报告提出的控制措施How to interpret the controls presented in this report
与标准风险管理一样，某一特定损害的风险既取决于该损害发生的可能性，也取决于其一旦发生所造成后果的严重程度。 As in standard risk management, the risk of a given harm depends on both the likelihood of that harm occurring and the severity of the consequences if it does occur. 因此，关于采用哪些控制措施的决策可能在很大程度上取决于具体应用场景。 Decisions about which controls to apply can therefore depend significantly on the use-case. 部署组织可以决定哪些控制措施有必要采用，以及这些措施是否足以将风险控制在允许系统部署的水平。 The deploying organisation can decide which controls are warranted, and whether they sufficiently manage the risk for the system to be deployed at all. 如果潜在损害较小，较高的失效率可能是可以接受的，部署智能体的组织也可能在采用较少控制措施的情况下接受这一风险。 Where the potential harm is minor, a higher failure rate may be acceptable and an agent deploying organisation might accept the risk with fewer controls. 另一方面，在高风险场景中，有必要采用更强、更多的控制措施，既通过预防性控制措施降低失效发生的频率，也通过恢复性控制措施限制其影响。 On the other hand, in high-stakes settings, stronger and more numerous controls are warranted both to reduce the frequency of failures (through prevention controls) and to limit their impact (through recovery controls). 因此，同一种失效模式在 2 种不同的部署中，可能需要采用截然不同的控制措施。 The same failure mode can therefore justify very different controls in 2 different deployments.
鉴于控制措施的选择如此依赖具体情境，本报告不规定必须采用哪些具体控制措施。 Given that the choice of controls is so context-dependent, this report does not prescribe specific controls . 相反，我们针对每种失效模式列出一系列原则上可能应对该模式的控制措施，同时也认识到，这些措施在实施难度和成本方面各不相同。 Instead, for each failure mode we present a repertoire of potential controls that could in principle address it, recognising that they also vary in how difficult and costly they are to implement. 这一系列措施选取了具有代表性的控制措施，并非完整集合，我们也不声称其涵盖了所有可能的措施。 This repertoire is a selection of salient controls rather than a complete set, and we do not claim it to be exhaustive . 部署组织需自行权衡这些选项，并结合自身的风险敞口、实施成本以及对具体应用场景的适用性，决定采用哪些措施。 It is left to the deploying organisation to weigh these options and decide which to adopt factoring in their risk exposure, the cost to implement and applicability to the use case.
2.4 当前的智能体生态系统2.4 The agent ecosystem today
本节通过与部分行业代表的磋商，概述当前观察到的部署格局；这些代表所在的组织包括成熟的在线交易平台、保险公司、金融服务机构以及智能体即服务初创企业。 This section frames the current deployment landscape observed through consultations with a selection of industry representatives from organisations including mature online marketplaces, insurance companies, financial services and also agent-as-a-service startups. 这是截至2026年中对一个新兴且快速发展领域的概貌。 It is a snapshot as of mid-2026 of a new and rapidly moving space. 商业智能体正出现在大多数行业中，但可行的用例仍在探索，组织实践也仍在确立。 Commercial agents are appearing across most industry sectors, but viable use cases are still being explored and organisational practices are still being established. 下述模式反映的是早期市场正在形成的形态，而非对其最终走向的预测。 The patterns below are the shape the early market is taking, not a forecast of its destination.
目前，行业内主要存在2种明确的部署模式，反映出运行范围与用例风险之间一贯存在的权衡。 Within industry, 2 clear deployment patterns currently dominate, reflecting a consistent trade-off between operating scope and use-case risk.
面向组织内部、以提高生产力为重点的智能体，正用于以下应用场景：输出本身可由人工审查，或在代码场景下可通过程序验证，而且最终结果比产生结果的过程更重要。 Internally facing, productivity-focused agents are being used for applications where the output itself can be human reviewed, or programmatically verified in the case of code, and the end outcome matters more than the process that produced it. 典型示例包括：Claude Code或Codex等用于加快软件工程任务的编程助手，通过多步骤调查协助员工的研究智能体，以及帮助安排日程和预订的个人助理类智能体。 Typical examples include coding assistants such as Claude Code or Codex accelerating software engineering tasks, research agents conducting multi-step investigations to assist staff members, and personal-assistant type agents helping organise scheduling and bookings. 大型集成套件具有尤为重要的影响：随着供应商将智能体功能嵌入日常生产力工具，非技术用户也会自然而然地接触到这项技术。 The major integrated suites matter disproportionately: as suppliers embed agentic functionality into everyday productivity tools, they extend the technology to non-technical users by default. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 18 > | industry .gov.au 18
目前，有意设计的多智能体协作尚不常见。 Currently, multi-agent coordination by design is rare. 组织拥有多个智能体，但并未构建由明确相连的智能体组成的系统。 Organisations have plural agents, but aren't building systems of explicitly connected agents. 这一模式的例外是，代表组织内不同委托方行事的生产力智能体，可能通过文档、日历或代码库等共享产物彼此接触。 The exceptions to this pattern are that productivity agents acting on behalf of different principals in an organisation may encounter each other through shared artefacts such as documents, calendars or codebases. 在编程领域，商业智能体实现目前确实会采用编排器—委派器模式启动工作智能体，以并行处理子任务。 Within the coding domain, commercial agent implementations do currently spin up worker agents in an orchestrator-delegator pattern to address sub-tasks in parallel. 我们尚未看到组织通过显式协议，有意将跨多个业务职能的不同智能体连接起来，但我们认识到，当数字员工类智能体在组织的多种职能中得到更广泛的整合时，这种做法将在未来带来效率收益。 We are yet to see organisations intentionally connect different agents across multiple business functions through explicit protocols, but recognise that this would offer future efficiency benefits when digital worker type agents become more integrated across a range of organisational functions.
范围受到严格限定、设有严格护栏的对外智能体。 Tightly-scoped externally facing agents with strict guardrails . 部署对外智能体的组织承担着声誉风险。 Organisations deploying externally facing agents bear reputational risk. 某些行业（如金融服务业）的组织还可能受到监管要求的约束。 Organisations in certain sectors (such as financial services) may also be operating under regulatory requirements. 在这些严格条件下，一些组织正在开发对外智能体，以承担能动性（Agency）较低、处理量较大的工作，例如： Under these strict conditions, some organisations are developing externally facing agents to take on low-Agency , high volume roles such as:
• 将提交的文档处理为结构化信息，并能灵活识别所需信息位于哪些文档的哪些位置 • processing submitted documents into structured information, where they can flexibly identify where in which documents the required information can be found
• 解决客户咨询问题，遵循标准操作程序手册，并配备检索增强生成（RAG）工具和护栏，以确保回答以检索到的信息为依据，或在没有相关来源时拒绝回答。 • customer query resolution, where they follow a standard operating procedure handbook and have supporting Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) tools and guardrails to ensure the responses are grounded in the retrieved information, or refuse to answer when no relevant source is available.
其中一些范围受到严格限定的系统，确实将多个智能体组件组合成一系列阶段。 Some of these tightly scoped systems do compose multiple agentic components into a series of stages. 路由智能体可能将查询转交给专门领域的智能体，但这通常表现为一次独立的交接，而非智能体之间的往返交互。 A router agent might direct a query to a specialist agent, but this typically takes the form of a discrete hand-off rather than back-and-forth interaction between agents. 系统在任一给定时刻通常只有一个活跃智能体。 Often there is only one active agent in the system at any given time. 此外，原始上下文会完整传递给受委派执行任务的智能体，有时还会附带路由智能体提供的结构化模式，因此几乎没有发生沟通偏差或其他涌现性多智能体失效模式的空间。 Furthermore, the original context is passed on in its entirety to the agent that the task was delegated to, sometimes with a structured schema provided by the router agent, so there is minimal scope for miscommunication or other emergent multi-agent failure modes to arise. 这些智能体在设计上具有较低的自主性，以维持高度控制。 These agents are designed to be less autonomous to maintain a high degree of control. 其代价是，限制能动性也会降低智能体解决问题的灵活性。 The trade-off is that constraining agency also reduces the agent's flexibility to solve problems. 目前尚不清楚，在对外应用场景中，灵活且高度自主的智能体有哪些可行用例。 It is not yet clear what viable use cases exist for flexible, high-autonomy agents in an externally facing setting.
初创企业可能率先找到高度自主智能体的用例。 Startups may be the first to find use-cases for highly autonomous agents . 它们具有更高的风险偏好，并能够围绕高度自主、目标驱动的智能体构建商业模式和治理机制。 They have a higher risk appetite and are able to shape business models and governance around highly autonomous goal-driven agents. 根据我们迄今开展的访谈，较难判断这一潜力是否正在实践中得到实现。 Whether that potential is being realised in practice is harder to judge from our conversations to date. 我们所了解的大多数AI智能体初创企业，实际上都是范围受到严格限定的智能体的供应商。 Most of the AI-agent startups we are aware of are actually suppliers of tightly scoped agents.
专业智能体供应商通过Microsoft Copilot Studio等集成套件、Amazon Bedrock Agents等框架、OpenAI Presence等托管部署平台，或定制客户服务智能体等垂直领域产品，以服务形式提供智能体，通常由前线部署工程师根据以下情况对智能体进行定制：多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Specialist agent suppliers provide agents as a service through integrated suites such as Microsoft Copilot Studio, frameworks such as Amazon Bedrock Agents, managed deployment platforms such as OpenAI Presence, or vertical offerings such as bespoke customer-service agents, often with forward-deployed engineers tailoring the agent to Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 19 > | industry .gov.au 19
客户的环境，并在客户组织内部开展这项工作。 the customer's environment from within their organisation. 智能体供应商的出现正在重塑自行构建还是外部采购的决策。 The emergence of agent suppliers is reshaping build-versus-buy decisions. 组织无需具备内部技术专长或开展内部开发，即可采购智能体，但这也引出了围绕供应商风险的新治理问题。 Organisations can procure agents without needing in-house technical expertise or development, but this raises new governance questions around supplier risk.
在产业界之外，面向消费者的智能体正通过 OpenClaw 和 Hermes 等目标驱动、适用范围广泛的系统进入公共讨论；这两个系统都旨在以高度的灵活性和 Outside industry, consumer agents are entering public discourse through goal-driven, broad-scope systems such as OpenClaw and Hermes, which are both designed to pursue open-ended user requests with a high degree of flexibility and
自主性处理用户的开放式请求。 autonomy . 使用这些智能体的消费者不受组织问责架构的约束，因此可以自行批准使用，而无需经过风险管理和合规职能部门的审批。 The consumers using them are not bound by organisational accountability structures, so they can self-approve use rather than clearing it through risk and compliance functions. 这消除了拖慢机构采用进程的阻力，无论消费者是已经接受了风险，还是根本没有考虑过风险。 This removes the friction that slows institutional adoption, regardless of whether the consumer has accepted the risk or simply not considered it.
面向消费者的智能体开始以“用户”的身份使用产业界部署的面向客户的服务，本报告将进一步探讨这一发展趋势。 Consumer agents are starting to appear as ‘users’ of industry-deployed customer-facing services , a development we examine further in this report.
可以明确的是，任何人都能部署智能体：技术准入门槛很低。 What's clear is that anyone can deploy agents: the technological barrier to entry is low. 决定一个组织是否应当部署智能体的，是其治理准备程度，以及它能否识别出收益足以抵偿风险的用例。 What determines whether an organisation should is its governance readiness, and whether it can identify use cases where the reward justifies the risk. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems 
| industry .gov.au 20
3 多智能体部署的治理3 Governing multi-agent deployments
3.1 多智能体治理为何不同3.1 Why multi-agent governance is different
多智能体系统的治理并非只是单智能体治理的规模化扩展。 Governance of a multi-agent system is not just scaled-up single agent governance. 在此前的报告中，我们提供了详细的解释和示例，说明为何 In our earlier report we provide detailed explanations and examples as to why the issues
多智能体动态交互中出现的问题需要采用根本不同的治理方法。 that arise in multi-agent dynamics require a fundamentally different approach to governance. 10 归根结底，主要原因如下： 10 In essence, the key reasons are:
• 多智能体交互中会出现新类型的失效，而这些失效在系统中只有一个智能体时根本不会出现。 • New types of failures emerge in multi-agent interactions that are simply not present when there is only one agent in the system. 因此，治理需要将这些新的失效形式纳入考量。 Therefore, governance needs to account for such new forms of failures.
• 失效无法按单个智能体加以分解，这意味着治理对象不能是单个智能体，而必须是由相互交互的智能体构成的系统本身。 • Failures do not decompose across individual agents , which means the target of their governance cannot be individual agents, but rather has to be the system itself of interacting agents.
• 当不同智能体相互交互时，单智能体的失效可能传播或放大——针对智能体系统而非单个智能体的治理方法，有助于应对此类传播或放大。 • Single agent failures can propagate or amplify when different agents interact – in ways that would benefit from a governance approach that targets the system of agents rather than individual agents.
本报告考察的许多失效会使单智能体失效传播或放大，但并非所有失效都如此。 Many, but not all, of the failures examined in this report propagate or amplify single-agent failures. 本报告聚焦多智能体失效，并不意味着单智能体失效问题已经得到解决。 This report's focus on multi-agent failures does not imply that single agent failures are a solved problem. 有关单智能体治理的资源，请参见第 2.1 节。 See Section 2.1 for resources on single agent governance. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 21 > | industry .gov.au 21
与人类治理的类比Analogy to human governance
本报告提及的各类失效，其新颖程度和管理难度并不相同。 Not all failures mentioned in this report are equally novel or difficult to manage.
具体而言，多智能体治理的某些方面与 In particular, some aspects of multi-agent governance have strong analogies to the
人类组织的治理高度相似，后者长期以来一直需要协调不完美的行动主体： governance of human organisations, which have long had to coordinate imperfect actors:
将每个角色的权限限定在所需的最低范围内，实行职责分离，确保任何 scoping each role's permissions to the minimum required, separating duties so that no
单一行动主体都无法既发起又批准一项具有重大影响的行动，保留审计轨迹， single actor can both initiate and approve a consequential action, maintaining audit trails,
并对供应商和交易对手开展尽职调查。 and performing due diligence on suppliers and counterparties. 对于这些方面，组织 For these, organisations
无需重新发明组织治理，而应将其扩展到 AI 智能体。 do not need to reinvent organisational governance but rather extend it to AI agents.
另一些失效则确实是全新的，它们产生于以 AI 智能体替代人类 Other failures are genuinely new, arising where substituting an AI agent for a human
打破既有实践所依赖的假设之时。 breaks the assumptions that established practices rest on. 智能体以机器 Agents operate at machine
速度运行：失效可能在人类来得及干预之前就已在系统中级联传播，而 speed: a failure can cascade through a system faster than a human can intervene, and
围绕人类处理速度设计的逐级上报监督机制则会不堪重负。 oversight regimes designed around human-paced escalation become saturated.
因此，监督需要转向运行防护机制和可扩展的控制措施。 Oversight therefore needs to shift toward operational guardrails and scalable controls.
当依据同一份规格说明启动大量实例时，一个缺陷就可能被复制 With many instances spun up from a single specification, one flaw can be replicated

基础设施方法。 infrastructure approaches to risk management.

其执行的业务流程，以及约束它的防护措施负责。 for the business process it executes, and for the guardrails that constrain it.
3.2 面临压力的四项治理实践3.2 Four governance practices under stress
如第 3.1 节所述，多智能体系统对治理提出了新的要求，超出了治理单个智能体时所关注的问题。 As Section 3.1 set out, multi-agent systems place novel demands on governance that extend beyond the concerns of governing individual agents.
尽管这些问题可能影响组织内多种不同的治理实践，但我们重点关注受这些要求强烈影响的 4 项关键实践。 While these concerns can potentially affect many different governance practices in an organisation, we focus on 4 key practices that are strongly affected by these demands. 我们将在此简要介绍这些实践，随后探讨第 4、5 和 6 节所述治理条件的变化如何影响这些实践。 We will briefly introduce these practices here and will go on to examine how they are affected by the changing governance conditions covered in Sections 4, 5 and 6.
归因Attribution
归因是将某一结果的因果责任归于通过自身行为产生该结果的智能体及其委托主体的实践。 Attribution is the practice of assigning causal responsibility for an outcome to the agents and principals whose actions produced it. 在单智能体情境下，这只是简单的记录管理问题，但在多智能体情境下，要在一连串行动中定位故障可能并不容易。 In a single-agent setting this is a straight-forward matter of record keeping, but in a multi-agent setting it can be non-trivial to locate the fault in a chain of actions. 根据日志记录的设计方式，可能需要在事后重建这一行动链。 Depending on how the logging is designed, the chain may have to be reconstructed after the fact. 此外，正如《多智能体系统的风险与控制》 Furthermore, as Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 22 > | industry .gov.au 22
第 3.1 节所述，有些结果并不存在可供定位的单一故障点，因为系统中可能涌现出任何单个智能体在独立运行时都不会表现出的行为。 described in Section 3.1, some outcomes have no single point of failure to locate, as behaviours can emerge in the system that no single agent exhibits in isolation.
授权Authorisation
授权是确保每个智能体都依据委托主体的委托行事的实践。 Authorisation is the practice of ensuring each agent acts with a mandate from a principal. 在单智能体情境下，授权主要关注确保行动处于委托主体所授予的权限范围内。 In a single-agent setting, authorisation is primarily concerned with ensuring actions sit within the set of permissions the principal granted. 在多智能体情境下，这一关注范围可能进一步扩展至权限委派：如果某个智能体能够创建其他智能体实例，就必须正确界定授予这些实例的权限范围，通常应限定为父智能体权限的一个子集，而且需要在这些新实例的整个生命周期内对其进行治理。 In a multi-agent setting this concern expands to potentially include delegation: if an agent can create other agent instances, the authority granted to these instances must be correctly scoped, generally to a subset of the parent agent's permissions, and those new instances need to be governed across their lifecycles.
监督Oversight
监督是保持智能体活动的可见性，使承担运行责任的人类能够理解这些活动并进行干预的实践。 Oversight is the practice of maintaining visibility over agent activity such that operationally responsible humans can interpret it and intervene. 在单智能体情境下，监督取决于审查者能否跟上智能体作出决策的速度、处理其决策数量，以及能否理解这些决策。 In a single-agent setting, oversight depends on the reviewer being able to keep up with the pace and volume of the agent's decisions, and those decisions being legible to them. 多智能体情境可能在数量和可理解性两方面都带来压力，因为行动和通信的数量会随着系统中智能体数量的增加而增长，而交互动态则分布于各个智能体之间。 A multi-agent setting can stress both the volume and legibility, as the number of actions and communication scales with the number of agents in the system, and the interaction dynamics become distributed across them.
评估Evaluation
对于智能体系统，评估是在部署时以及整个生命周期内持续确定系统及其智能体是否适合预定用途的实践。 Evaluation , for agentic systems, is the practice of establishing whether the system and its agents are fit for purpose, both at the time of deployment and continuously throughout their lifecycles. 多智能体评估以单智能体评估为基础并加以扩展，而单智能体评估本身也并非一个简单或已解决的问题。 Multi-agent evaluation builds upon and extends single-agent evaluation, itself not a trivial or solved problem. 不过，我们主要关注多智能体系统在单智能体评估挑战之上带来的额外评估挑战。 However, we focus mainly on the evaluation challenges posed by multi-agent systems that sit on top of single-agent evaluation challenges.
3.3 治理层级3.3 Governance tiers
随着智能体的应用场景变得更加开放，且智能体之间的交互跨越组织边界，多智能体网络的治理复杂性也随之增加。 As agent use cases become more open-ended and agent interactions extend across organisational boundaries, the governance complexity of the multi-agent network grows. 从单个组织内职责范围严格限定的智能体，转向与未知交互方互动的开放式智能体，既扩大了系统可能出现的失效类型范围，也限制了单个组织能够观察或控制的范围。 Moving from tightly-scoped agents within a single organisation towards open-ended agents interacting with unknown counterparties both widens the range of failures the system can produce and limits the reach of what a single organisation can see or control.
我们依据以下标准，将这一治理复杂性谱系划分为不同层级： We distinguish this spectrum of governance complexity into tiers based on the
约束交互中任意 2 个智能体的最低限度共同治理。 minimum common governance that binds any 2 agents in an interaction. 每个层级都对应一个阶段，在该阶段，必须引入新的智能体治理措施才能安全运行。 Each tier corresponds to a step where new agent governance becomes necessary to operate safely. 我们具体考察 3 个层级：多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 We examine 3 tiers specifically: Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 23 > | industry .gov.au 23
• 单一治理下的智能体：由一个组织部署系统中的所有智能体。 • Agents under singular governance : a single organisation deploys every agent in the system. 该组织可以选择实施何种治理，且其治理范围覆盖整个系统。 The organisation can choose what governance to enforce, and its reach extends across the entire system. 委托主体（principals）之间的信任基于来源。 Trust between principals is by provenance.
• 联合治理下的智能体：多个组织参与一个共享治理框架，该框架由多边协议确立，或由居间方制定。 • Agents under federated governance : multiple organisations participate in a shared governance framework, either established by multilateral agreement, or set by a mediating party. 该框架对所有参与方均具有约束力；虽然各组织分别治理自己的智能体，但它们都遵守参与协议，并使用共享基础设施。 The framework binds all participants and, while each organisation governs their own agents, they all abide by the participation agreement and use shared infrastructure. 委托主体之间的信任通过中介建立。 Trust between principals is mediated.
• 开放环境中的智能体：智能体在没有中央治理机构的环境中交互。 • Agents in open environments : agents interact in an environment with no central governing body. 在缺乏共享治理框架的情况下，交互各方默认互不信任，每次交互都受到约束并经过验证。 Without a shared governance framework, the default stance between counterparties is distrust and every interaction is contained and verified. 可以通过多中心治理（Polycentric governance）确立基本底线，即参与方自愿采用共同标准，并建设支持这些标准的公共基础设施。 A floor can be established through Polycentric governance where participants voluntarily adopt shared standards and build the public infrastructure to support them. 委托主体之间的信任要么经过验证，要么不存在。 Trust between principals is verified or absent.
我们指出，进入某一层级的门槛取决于组织的部署决策，而非其技术能力：组织选择在联合治理或开放环境中运行，即意味着进入相应环境。 We note that the threshold for entering a tier is determined by an organisation's deployment decisions rather than their technical capability: an organisation crosses into federated or open environments by choosing to operate there.
图 6：多智能体治理的 3 个层级。 Figure 6: The 3 tiers of multi-agent governance. 每个层级均由所有相互交互的智能体共同遵循的最低治理要求界定，并且都承袭此前各层级的风险因素。 Each tier is defined by the minimum governance common to all interacting agents and each carries forward the risk factors of the preceding tiers. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 24 > | industry .gov.au 24
3.4 本报告的结构3.4 Structure of this report
本报告其余部分的结构如下。 The remainder of this report is structured as follows.
第 4 至第 6 节考察我们识别出的 3 个治理层级。 Sections 4 to 6 examine the 3 governance tiers we have identified. 对于每个层级，我们考察其主要风险因素、这些因素可能引发的失效模式，以及该层级内可用的控制措施。 For each, we examine the salient risk factors, the failure modes they enable, and the controls available within the tier. 每节最后均列出超出任何单个组织能力范围的开放问题，汇总本报告为研究界、标准制定界和政策界揭示的缺口。 Each section finishes with open problems that lie beyond any single organisation's reach, consolidating the gaps the report surfaces for the research, standards, and policy communities. 风险因素和控制措施在各层级之间逐层累积，因此后续章节以前文为基础，最好按顺序阅读本报告。 Risk factors and controls are cumulative across the tiers, so later sections build on earlier ones, and the report is best read in sequence. 图 6 展示了这一安排。 Figure 6 illustrates this layout.
第 7 节讨论贯穿各层级的主题、范围之外的相关场景，以及未来研究方向。 Section 7 discusses cross-cutting themes, adjacent settings outside our scope, and directions for future research.
第 8 节作出总结。 Section 8 concludes.

4.1 显著风险因素4.1 Salient risk factors
多智能体风险很少毫无预兆地出现。 Multi-agent risks rarely happen without warning. 这些风险会因部署中可识别的条件而不断增加。 They grow as a result of identifiable conditions of the deployment. 第 2.3 节将这些条件定义为风险因素——即系统设计或运行中会提高失效可能性、但本身并非失效的属性。 Section 2.3 defined these conditions as risk factors –properties of the system’s design or operation that raise the likelihood of failure without being failures themselves. 一个部署即使存在若干此类因素，仍然可以成功运行。 A deployment can have several such factors and still operate successfully. 这些因素的存在并不意味着系统有问题，而是提示哪些方面值得关注并采取控制措施。 Their presence is not an indictment but a signal of where attention and controls are warranted.
下表列出了单一治理下最显著的风险因素，提供简要描述，并将其与它们可能促成的失效模式关联起来；这些失效模式将在第 4.2 节和第 4.3 节中详细讨论。 The table below catalogues the risk factors most salient under singular governance, provides a brief description and links them to the failure modes that they enable which will be discussed in detail in Sections 4.2 and 4.3. 该清单仅作选择性列举，并非穷尽所有因素，所列因素是在组织内部多智能体部署方面具有最充分现有证据支持的因素。 The list is selective rather than exhaustive, naming the factors with the strongest current evidence base for in-house multi-agent deployment.
> *单一治理指对系统拥有管辖权，并不一定意味着直接实施每一项控制措施。 > *Singular governance refers to authority over the system, not necessarily direct implementation of every control. 当智能体或底层模型由 AI 供应商运行或部署时，组织及其供应商是在共同部署一个智能体，而非将该组织的智能体与另一个不受该组织治理的独立智能体相连接。 Where the agent or underlying model is run or deployed by an AI vendor, the organisation and its vendor are jointly deploying one agent, rather than the organisation's agent being linked with a separate agent the organisation does not govern. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems

自然语言交接 Natural-language handoffs
智能体传递并解读自由形式的文本。 Agents passing and interpreting free-form text.
只要允许智能体之间使用自由形式的文本进行通信，导致双方智能体都无法可靠地理解对方掌握了哪些信息或需要什么 Whenever inter-agent communication is allowed in free-form text, leaving neither agent with a reliable interpretation of what the counterparty knows or needs

经过迎合用户训练的 LLMs 倾向于强化共识，而非进行独立验证。 LLMs trained to be agreeable with a user defer to reinforcing consensus rather than independent verification.

单用户训练 Single-user training
LLMs 针对单用户交互进行训练，无法在单一上下文中可靠地与多个交互方互动。 LLMs are trained for single-user interaction and can't reliably interact with multiple counterparties within a single context.
只要 AI 智能体需要与多个交互方互动，或需要在不向所有交互方共享数据的情况下使用这些数据时。 Whenever an AI agent needs to interact with multiple counterparties, or use data without sharing that data with all counterparties.
• 上下文泄露 • Context leakage
规范与执行之间的差距 Specification-execution gap
委托人在编写指令时的意图与智能体在运行时对该指令的解读之间的差距。 The gap between what the principal intended when they wrote an instruction, and how the agent interpreted that instruction at runtime.
只要运行时行为受到上下文内推理的影响，而设计时的规范无法充分约束这种推理时。 Whenever runtime behaviour is shaped by in-context reasoning that design-time specification cannot fully constrain.
• 超出授权范围行事 • Acting outside authorisation
• 协调失误 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 • Miscoordination Risks and controls for multi-agent systems

智能体间委托 Inter-agent delegation
智能体可以请求、授权或创建其他智能体来代表其行事，而接收请求的智能体往往会假定上游请求已获批准。 Agents can request, authorise, or spawn other agents to act on their behalf, and receiving agents tend to assume upstream requests are sanctioned.
只要系统架构允许智能体向同级智能体或子智能体分派工作，且未在每一步都向委托人重新核实权限时。 Whenever the system architecture allows agents to issue work to peers or sub-agents and authority is not re-verified against the principal at each step.
• 困惑的代理人（confused deputy） • Confused deputy
• 未经授权的子智能体实例 • Unauthorised sub-agent instances
分布式多智能体状态 Distributed multi-agent state
执行上下文、推理和决策分散在多个智能体上下文及交接环节中，没有任何单一记录能够呈现全貌。 Execution context, reasoning, and decision-making are spread across multiple agent contexts and handoffs, with no single record holding the whole picture.
只要任务涉及多个智能体，且重建事件经过或原因所需的信息分散在多个智能体的上下文中时。 Whenever a task involves multiple agents and the information needed to reconstruct what happened or why is split across the contexts of multiple agents.
• 因果归因失败 • Causal attribution failure
• 监督所需信息难以解读 • Oversight illegibility
智能体吞吐量超出人工审查能力 Agent throughput exceeding human review
智能体的行动速度和规模超出了人工审查者能够有效审查的范围。 Agents act faster and at greater scale than human reviewers can meaningfully scrutinise.
只要智能体作出决策或采取行动的速率超出名义上负责监督它们的人员的能力时。 Whenever the rate of agent decisions or actions outpaces the capacity of the humans nominally overseeing them.
• 监督能力饱和 • Oversight saturation
涌现的多智能体行为 Emergent multi-agent behaviour
智能体之间的交互会产生任何单个智能体在独立运行时都不会表现出的行为。 Behaviours arise from interaction between agents that no single agent exhibits in isolation.
只要多个智能体进行多轮交互，尤其是在智能体会根据彼此的输出作出调整的情况下 Whenever multiple agents interact over multiple rounds, particularly where the agents adapt to each other's outputs

4.2 智能体交互中的失效及控制措施4.2 Failures in agent interaction and controls
本节介绍智能体之间交互所引发的主要失效，以及有助于预防这些失效并从中恢复的部分控制措施。 This section introduces salient failures that emerge from agent-to-agent interaction, along with a selection of controls to help prevent and recover from them. 对于处于统一治理之下的系统，本节考察 2 类失效： For systems under singular governance, 2 categories of failure are examined here:
• 智能体之间的协调失误，涵盖相互协作的智能体之间的协调失效，例如对通信内容的误解或错误的交接。 • Miscoordination between agents covers failures of coordination between cooperating agents such as misinterpreted communication or erroneous handoffs.
• 传播与扩散，探讨错误、错误信念、敏感数据或恶意指令沿多智能体系统中的连接传播的失效情形。 • Propagation and contagion explores failures where an error, a wrong belief, sensitive data, or malicious instruction propagates through the links in the multi-agent system.
此处确定的失效在后续每一个治理层级中都同样适用。 The failures established here carry forward through every subsequent governance tier. 后续章节将介绍其他失效类别。 Additional failure categories are introduced in later chapters.
请注意，本报告仅考虑本质上属于多智能体范畴的失效。 Note that this report only considers failures that are multi-agent in nature . 单智能体失效被视为基线背景。 Single-agent failures are taken as baseline context. 另请注意，组织用于治理这些智能体的机制中出现的失效，将在第 4.3 节单独考察。 Also note that failures in the organisation's apparatus for governing these agents are examined separately in Section 4.3.
4.2.1 协调失误4.2.1 Miscoordination
在统一治理之下，协调失误出现在智能体之间的交接环节，即一个智能体基于对另一个智能体所掌握上下文的错误理解采取行动。 Under singular governance, miscoordination shows up at agent-to-agent handoffs, where one agent acts on an incorrect interpretation of the context the other agent held. 第 5 节和第 6 节将进一步扩展这一失效类别。 This category of failure will be extended in Sections 5 and 6.
智能体间通信失效Inter-agent communication failures
当智能体协作完成某项任务时，它们有时无法就说了什么、意指什么、做了什么或隐瞒了什么达成共同理解。 When agents coordinate on a task, they can sometimes fail to converge on a shared understanding of what was said, what was meant, what was done, or what was withheld. 我们将这些情况称为智能体间通信失效。 We call these inter-agent communication failures . 这可能表现为多种形式，例如： This can manifest in several ways such as:
• 发送方输出错误信息 • a sender outputting incorrect information
• 接收方误解正确信息 • a receiver misinterpreting correct information
• 某个智能体的行为与其所陈述的推理不一致，而其他智能体依据该推理采取行动 • an agent acting inconsistently with its stated reasoning that other agents act on
• 一组智能体在多轮交互中逐渐偏离原始目标 • a group of agents gradually drifting from the original objective across rounds
• 发送方遗漏关键信息，接收方错误地自行补全，而没有请求澄清。 • a sender omitting key information that the receiver incorrectly fills in rather than asking for clarification.
Cemri 等人将其底层机制描述为“心智理论崩塌（theory-of-mind collapse）：智能体无法对其同伴知道什么、需要什么或即将做什么建立模型”。 Cemri et al. frame the underlying mechanism as a ‘ theory-of-mind collapse : agents fail to model what their peers know, need, or are about to do’. 11 只要有 2 个智能体交换信息，就可能出现这种失效模式：无论是在交接环节、通过通信协议，还是在向受委派的智能体指定任务时。 11 This failure pattern can arise wherever 2 agents exchange information: at handoffs, through communication protocols, or when specifying tasks to delegates. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 30 > | industry .gov.au 30
这里起作用的风险因素是自然语言交接：如果允许智能体传递自由形式的文本，而非采用结构化模式，可能发生失效的范围就基本不受约束。 The risk factor at work here is natural-language handoffs: if agents are allowed to pass free-form text rather than structured schemas, the failure surface is essentially unconstrained. 本报告多次提到的一项关键的跨领域控制措施——结构化交接——可以在很大程度上解决这一问题。 This is significantly addressable via a key cross cutting control mentioned throughout this report: Structured handoffs .
结构化交接：智能体对传递给 Structured handoffs : Agents use a pre-specified schema for the outputs passed to
其他智能体的输出使用预先指定的模式，确保信息以一致的方式呈现，并且输出能够 other agents, ensuring information is presented consistently and the output can be
依据该模式接受合规性和完整性验证。 verified for compliance and completeness against the schema. 结构化交接 The structured handoffs
还为接入日志记录和评估机制提供了固定接口。 also provide a hardpoint to attach logging and evaluation apparatus.
例如，理赔分流智能体可能交接如下结构化数据：{claim_id, For example, a claims-triage agent might hand off structured data like {claim_id,
> policy_number, incident_date, claim_amount, supporting_documents[]} > policy_number, incident_date, claim_amount, supporting_documents[]}
向一名 to a
理赔评估智能体，而不是一段类似于“这项索赔的金额为 claims assessment agent, rather than a paragraph like ‘this claim is for this amount
依据这份保单”的文字。 under this policy’.
第二条路径源于大语言模型（LLM）保持状态的方式：当交互持续较长时间、上下文窗口变长时，智能体可能会遗忘重要上下文，或偏离其指定任务。 A second pathway arises from how LLMs hold state: agents can forget important context or drift away from their specified task when an interaction becomes extended and the context window becomes long. 这种上下文漂移可能使智能体作出不一致的假设，从而误解其他智能体的输入。 This context drift can cause agents to misinterpret peer input by making inconsistent assumptions.
通信失败是智能体部署中最常见的故障之一。 Communication failures are amongst the most common in agentic deployments. 一项实证研究发现，多智能体系统的故障中，大约每 3 起就有一起属于智能体间协调失误，表现为推理与行动不匹配、任务偏离、未能请求澄清，以及隐瞒信息。 One empirical study found that roughly one in 3 failures of multi-agent systems was inter-agent miscoordination, taking the forms of reasoning-action mismatch, task derailment, failure to ask for clarification, and information withholding. 11 前沿开发团队也观察到了智能体通过自然语言交接进行协调时产生的不可靠性，例如 GitHub 的工程团队将其描述为“缺少结构，而非模型能力不足”。 11 The unreliability that ensues when agents coordinate with natural-language handoffs has also been observed by frontier developers, such as GitHub's engineering team describing them as ‘missing structure, not model capability’. 12 12
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 智能体之间的结构化交接（预防）。 • Structured handoffs between agents (prevention). 用明确的数据模式替代自由形式的自然语言传递，也可在各阶段之间设置程序化关卡。 Replace free-form natural-language passing with explicit schemas, optionally with programmatic gates between stages. 凡是 2 个智能体进行通信的场景均适用；这是本节中效益最高的技术投入之一。 Effective wherever 2 agents communicate; among the highest-leverage technical investments in this section.
• 在每个阶段明确指定角色（预防）。 • Explicit role specification at each stage (prevention). 在上下文窗口中，针对每个智能体的每项任务指定角色，以帮助应对上述漂移机制。 Specify per-agent per-task roles in the context window to help address the drift mechanism described above.
• 主动寻求澄清的协议（预防）。 • Active clarification-seeking protocols (prevention). 指示和/或训练发送方智能体请求澄清，而不是默默自行消除疑义。 Instruct and/or train the sending agent to ask for clarification rather than resolving it silently. 11 11
• 解释过程日志记录（恢复）。 • Interpretation logging (recovery). 不仅记录每个智能体接收到的内容，还要记录每个智能体据此作出的推断，以便通过故障追踪（Trace）分析虚构内容源自何处。 Log not only what each agent received but also what each agent inferred about it, so a failure Trace can analyse where a fabrication originated. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems

4.2.2 传播与传染4.2.2 Propagation and contagion
这一类别中的失效模式都涉及信息通过智能体之间的连接在多智能体系统中流动，区别在于通过这些连接传播的内容：输出错误、共识或敏感数据。 The failure modes in this cluster all involve information travelling through the multi-agent system via the links between agents, all differing in what propagates through those links: output errors, consensus, sensitive data.
级联可靠性失效Cascading reliability failures
当一组智能体协同完成共同任务时，一个智能体的错误输出可能被传递给另一个智能体，而接收方智能体会将其作为有效输入接受。 When a team of agents coordinate on a common task, an erroneous agent output may be passed to another agent, with the receiving agent accepting it as a valid input. 我们将此类情况称为级联可靠性失效。 We call these cascading reliability failures.
这些传播中的错误源于最初的单个智能体错误。 These propagating errors are seeded by an initial single-agent error. 在单智能体系统中，错误输出会造成一个孤立的错误。 In a single-agent system, an erroneous output produces an isolated error. 在由多个智能体组成的系统中，它可能引发一连串悄然传播的错误，而且每传播一步，其影响范围往往都会扩大。 In a system of agents, it can initiate a chain of errors that propagates silently, often becoming broader in consequence at each step.
有三个风险因素会使系统容易发生级联失效。 Three risk factors make a system prone to cascading failure. 如果系统采用 If the system has
自然语言交接（而非我们在第 4.2.1 节中看到的结构化交接），智能体就有更多方式表达错误输出，使其看起来有效　多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 natural-language handoffs (rather than structured handoffs as we saw in Section 4.2.1) there are more ways an agent can express an erroneous output that Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 32 > | industry .gov.au 32
。 appears to be valid. 各智能体采用同一种模型，也会使它们倾向于具有相同的盲点和偏差，因此在接收到错误输入时，纠错能力较弱 10。 A model monoculture across agents also makes them predisposed to share blind spots and biases, and therefore less able to error-correct when receiving an erroneous input 10 . 智能体经常表现出偏离系统总体目标的倾向，这会造成任务验证缺口，导致失效经常在未经检查的情况下悄然传播到整个网络。 A frequently observed tendency for agents to lose track of the overall objective of the system introduces a task verification gap that frequently results in the failures being propagated silently across the network unchecked. 13 13
与许多其他传播动态一样，单智能体的可靠性在决定级联错误失效率方面发挥着核心作用。 As with many other propagation dynamics, single-agent reliability plays a central role in determining the rate of cascading error failures. 在一项针对规划器、执行器和评判器流水线的研究中，Barrak 测得，第一步生成的有缺陷计划是预测失败的最强因素，而下游智能体只能纠正上游错误中的一小部分。 In a study of a planner, executor, critic pipeline, Barrak measured a flawed plan created in the very first step as the single largest predictor of failure, with downstream agents correcting only a small fraction of upstream errors. 13 因此，提高单个智能体可靠性的控制措施也会带来减少失效传播的连带益处。 13 Controls that increase the reliability of single agents therefore have flow-on benefits to fewer propagating failures. 有关单智能体治理的资源，请参见第 2.1 节。 See Section 2.1 for resources on single agent governance.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 考虑是否可以通过 LLM 工作流完成任务（预防）。 • Consider whether the task can be achieved with a LLM workflow (prevention). 当任务不需要自主智能体时，应避免使用。 Avoid reaching for an autonomous agent when the task does not require it. Anthropic 的工程博客列出了可供考虑的标准工作流模式。 Anthropic's engineering blog catalogues standard workflow patterns to consider. 14 14
• 系统级目标验证（预防）。 • System-level objective verification (prevention). 添加一个验证层，依据用户的原始任务检查系统的最终输出，而不只是检查每个智能体是否完成了分配给它的部分。 Add a verification layer that checks the system's final output against the user's original task, not just whether each agent did the piece it was handed.
• 模型多样化（预防）。 • Model diversification (prevention). 审查哪些智能体共用基础模型、微调数据或提示策略，并考虑引入多样性，以降低上述模型同质化路径带来的风险。 Audit which agents share a base model, fine-tuning data, or prompting strategy, and consider diversifying to reduce the monoculture pathway above. 这尤其适用于使用 LLM 进行评估或验证的情况。 This especially applies when an LLM is used for evaluation or verification.
• 智能体之间的结构化交接（预防）。 • Structured handoffs between agents (prevention). 在每次交接时采用模式约束（schema），是现有措施中预防级联故障最有效的措施之一，因为它缩小了错误却被接受的输出范围。 Adopting schemas at every handoff is among the highest-impact cascade-prevention moves available, since it narrows the range of incorrect-but-accepted outputs. 13 13
• 对中间阶段输出进行异常检测（恢复）。 • Anomaly detection on intermediate stage outputs (recovery). 在每次交接时，可以检查智能体传递给下一阶段的数据，判断是否存在出错的迹象。 At each handoff, the data an agent passes to the next stage can be checked for signs that something has gone wrong. 这可以通过固定规则（例如格式检查或范围检查）来完成，也可以使用独立模型（LLM 评判器）标记看似不合理的输出。 This can be done with fixed rules (e.g. format or range checks) or by using a separate model (LLM judge) to flag outputs that look implausible.
• 用于回退至最近已知良好状态的回滚／取消窗口（恢复）。 • Rollback/cancellation window to a last-known-good state (recovery). 在长时间运行的任务中设置检查点，并具备回滚智能体状态的能力。 Take checkpoints in a long running task with the ability to roll back the state of the agents.
虚假共识False consensus
虚假共识是指多个智能体在协同执行任务时，对一个共同的错误结论达成一致，并相互强化这一结论。 False consensus occurs when multiple agents coordinating on a task come to agree on and reinforce a shared incorrect conclusion. 这通常发生在智能体系统所遵循的工作流将讨论与投票步骤设计为决策机制的情况下。 This typically happens when a discussion-and-vote step is designed as a decision-making mechanism in a workflow the system of agents is following. 其中涉及两个风险因素，各自通过不同路径导致这种失效：智能体之间的模型同质化驱动结构性 Two risk factors are in play, each producing the failure through a different pathway: model monoculture across agents drives the structural
路径，而从众偏差则驱动动态路径。 pathway, and conformity bias drives the dynamic pathway. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 33 > | industry .gov.au 33
结构性路径出现在一组智能体被设计为通过冗余来获得稳健输出的情况下。 The structural pathway is where a group of agents are designed to reach a robust output through redundancy. 这种设计假设，如果多个智能体对某个输出达成一致，该输出就更有可能是正确的。 It is assumed by design that if multiple agents agree on an output, it is more likely to be correct. 然而，当这些智能体共用相同的基础模型、训练来源或提示时，它们的输出和错误可能产生相关性，从而使冗余假设失效。 However, when those agents share the same base model, training heritage or prompting, their outputs and errors can become correlated and the redundancy assumption breaks down. Kim 等人评估了排行榜上的 350 多个 LLM，发现当 2 个独立模型都出错时，它们约有 60% 的情况下会给出相同的错误答案。 Kim et al. evaluated over 350 leaderboard LLMs finding that when 2 independent models are both incorrect, about 60% of the time they agree on the same incorrect answer. 15 15
动态路径由从众偏差驱动。 The dynamic pathway runs on conformity bias. LLM 被训练为以迎合用户的方式进行交互，这可能使其形成谄媚倾向。 LLMs are trained to interact with users in an agreeable manner, which can create a disposition of sycophancy. 在多智能体环境中，这种倾向会转化为一种集体动态：智能体会向其他智能体表面上达成共识的立场调整，即使该立场并不正确。 In a multi-agent setting this disposition turns into a collective dynamic, with agents updating toward the apparent consensus position of other agents even if it is not the correct position. 这是一种放大式传播：随着更多智能体传播这一信号，信号本身也会变得更强。 This is an amplifying propagation: as more agents propagate the signal, the signal itself becomes stronger. Weng 等人证明，多种前沿 LLM 均存在显著的从众偏差。 Weng et al. demonstrate substantial conformity bias across a range of frontier LLMs. 16 Zhu 等人发现，智能体对初始答案的置信度与其后续从众行为之间存在很强的关联。 16 Zhu et al. find a strong relationship between an agent's initial-answer confidence and its subsequent conformity. 17 17
这两条路径也都可能削弱第 4.3.4 节所讨论的基于 LLM 的评估层的有效性。 Both pathways can also undermine the effectiveness of an LLM-based evaluation layer, discussed in Section 4.3.4.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 在智能体群体中采用多样化的模型和提示（预防）。 • Diversify models and prompts across the agent population (prevention). 如果系统使用共识机制，应采用使用多种不同模型和提示的智能体，以应对模型同质化这一风险因素。 If the system uses a consensus mechanism, employ agents with a range of different models and prompts to address the monoculture risk factor.
• 针对以其他智能体为锚点的推理的提示层面缓解措施（预防）。 • Prompt-level mitigations for peer-anchored reasoning (prevention). 三种提示层面的技术可以在运行时减弱动态路径的作用，每种技术分别针对从众行为的不同驱动因素。 Three prompt-level techniques can reduce the dynamic pathway at runtime, each targeting a different driver of conformity. 17 17
– 反方辩护者（Devil's Advocate）：引入一个持异议的同伴智能体，以打破从众偏差。 – Devil's Advocate : inject a dissenting peer to break conformity bias.
– 蒸馏（Distillation）：提炼上下文，使模型减少对反复出现的多数答案的关注。 – Distillation : distil the context so the model attends less to the repeated majority answer.
– 反思：提示每个智能体重新审视自身立场，打断多轮交互中逐渐趋向共识的过程。 – Reflection : prompt each agent to reconsider its position, disrupting the gradual pull toward consensus across rounds.
• 限制交互历史的长度（预防）。 • Limit interaction history length (prevention). 虚假共识往往逐渐出现，并在多轮交互中不断放大。 False consensus tends to appear gradually and amplify over multiple rounds of interaction. 截断或概括交互历史，而不是无限累积上下文。 Truncate or summarise the interaction history rather than accumulating an unbounded context. 18 18
• 盲投票与变换发言顺序（预防）。 • Blind voting and varied contribution order (prevention). 汇总各智能体的意见时，在初次作答阶段隐藏其他智能体的答案，并变换智能体的发言顺序。 When aggregating opinions across agents, hide other agents' answers during the initial response and vary the order in which agents contribute. 这可以防止其他智能体锚定于最先提出的意见。 This can prevent other agents anchoring on the first contribution.
共同理解漂移Shared-understanding drift
共同理解漂移是指在共享环境中工作的智能体持续适应彼此策略时发生的现象。 Shared-understanding drift occurs when agents working in a shared environment continuously adapt to each other's strategies. 与级联失效或虚假共识不同，它既没有初始错误，也没有决策节点——系统的共同参照（其词汇、惯例以及对人工制品的处理方式）只是逐渐脱离 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Unlike a cascading failure or false consensus, it has neither a seed error nor a decision point – the system's shared reference (its vocabulary, conventions, and treatment of artefacts) simply decouples Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 34 > | industry .gov.au 34
客观真值，即使每个智能体都基于自身掌握的信息进行有效推理。 from ground truth over time, even as each agent reasons validly on what it holds. 运行时适应有助于实现协调，但也可能产生不期望的反馈回路，导致整个智能体群体的行为发生漂移，偏离事实真相或委托方实际指定的任务。 Run-time adaptation is an enabler of coordination, but it can also create unwanted feedback loops that lead to a population-wide drift in the behaviour of the agents, away from either what is factually true, or the task the principal actually specified. 任何被当作客观真值的捏造内容都不会仅限于单个任务，而会损害其他智能体和人员随后所依赖的记录的完整性。 Any fabrication that becomes a ground truth will not stay contained to a single task, but degrade the integrity of the record that other agents, and people, subsequently rely on.
漂移可能迅速发生，也可能逐渐形成，而反馈回路可以通过智能体共有的任何渠道形成，包括以下两类： Drift can be fast or gradual, and the feedback loop can be through any channels the agents hold in common, including both:
• 事实与人工制品：一个智能体的错误输出或捏造的人工制品进入共享环境，另一个智能体将其视为真实，其他智能体适应这种处理方式，最终使捏造内容成为系统中看似成立的客观真值。 • Facts and artefacts : One agent's false output or fabricated artefact enters the shared environment, another agent treats it as real, others adapt to that treatment, and the fabrication becomes an apparent ground truth of the system. 19 19
• 智能体交互本身：智能体通过相互适应，使实际采用的任务规范解释偏离委托方的原意。 • Agent-interactions themselves : Agents co-adapt the working interpretation of a task specification away from what the principal intended it to mean. 20 20
存在任务验证缺口时，就可能发生漂移。 Drift is possible when there is a task verification gap . 每个智能体都依据自身掌握的信息检查输入，但系统的惯例、通信和人工制品缺乏事实依据，可能随时间推移而发生漂移。 Each agent checks its input against the information it has, but the system's conventions, communication and artefacts are ungrounded and can drift over time. 漂移可能是一种在后台持续且悄无声息地发生的失效，贯穿多种失效，却不最终表现为某一种具体失效。 Drift can be a continuous and silent failure in the background, permeating across many failures but not culminating in any specific failure. 现有的从众偏差基准衡量的是智能体转向错误答案的容易程度，而不是这种信念一旦形成后是否难以纠正。 Current conformity-bias benchmarks measure how readily agents flip to a wrong answer, not whether that belief, once set, resists correction. 21 即使是多轮基准，衡量的也是转变之前的抵抗程度，而不是转变之后的固化程度。 21 Even multi-turn benchmarks measure resistance before flipping, not entrenchment after. 22 22
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 通过外部来源重新确立事实依据（预防）。 • External re-grounding (prevention). 定期对照外部客观真值，重新核验智能体所依赖的事实、术语和标准。 Periodically re-verify the facts, terms, and standards an agent relies on against an external ground truth.
• 共享人工制品的来源追溯（预防）。 • Provenance on shared artefacts (prevention). 区分生成的人工制品与经过验证的人工制品，并指示智能体赋予后者更高的权重。 Distinguish generated artefacts from verified artefacts and instruct agents to place higher weight on verified ones.
• 任务层面的目标验证（预防）。 • Task-level objective verification (prevention). 当任务涉及多个输出时，对照原始任务验证各阶段的输出。 Where a task involves multiple outputs, verify stage outputs against the original task.
• 在任务提示中明确关键术语和标准（预防）。 • Anchor key terms and standards in the task prompt (prevention). 明确固定关键词汇的含义，防止运行时发生漂移。 Anchor the meaning of key vocabulary explicitly, preventing drift at runtime. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
