上下文泄漏Context leakage
代表一个委托主体处理数据的多智能体系统，可能会使这些数据出现在另一个委托主体的上下文中，这称为上下文泄露（context leakage）。 A multi-agent system processing data on behalf of one principal may allow that data to surface in another principal's context, called context leakage. 导致这种失效的风险因素有两个：单用户训练和自然语言交接。 Two risk factors drive this failure: single-user training and natural-language handoffs .
单用户训练是指，大多数 LLMs 接受的训练是与单个用户进行多轮交互，而不是跟踪谁可以访问哪些信息。 Single-user training means most LLMs are trained to interact with a single user over multiple rounds and not trained to track who can access which information. 当同一个智能体在同一个上下文窗口内处理来自多个委托主体的请求时，多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 When the same agent handles requests from multiple principals within the same context window, Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 36 > | industry .gov.au 36
它没有可靠的内部模型来判断哪些信息属于谁，因此可能在对一个委托主体的回复中呈现另一个委托主体的数据。 it has no reliable internal model of which information belongs to whom and can surface one principal's data in another's response.
前沿 LLMs 在多用户访问控制测试中普遍失败，而且这种失效会随着对话长度的增加而加剧。 Frontier LLMs systematically fail multi-user access-control tests, and the failure compounds with conversation length. Mireshghallah 等人报告称，在多方会议摘要任务中，即使在提示中向智能体提供了明确的隐私指令，隐私泄露率仍约为 40%。 Mireshghallah et al. report privacy-leakage rates of roughly 40% on multi-party meeting summarisation tasks even when agents are given explicit privacy instructions in the prompt. 23 Yang 等人指出，模型在长时间交互中不披露上下文信息的能力会显著下降，这表明单轮评估会系统性地低估风险。 23 Yang et al. note that the ability of models to withhold context degrades substantially under long interactions, suggesting that single-round evaluations systematically underestimate the risk. 24 24
在允许使用自然语言交接的情况下，这种交接会扩大此类泄露的发生面：自由形式的文本可以通过多种方式携带机密信息，而经过模式验证的 Payload 能够限制这些方式，但无法将其消除。 Natural-language handoffs , where permitted, widen the surface for this leak: free-form text can carry confidential information in ways a schema-validated Payload constrains but does not eliminate. 下游智能体无法区分哪些内容是有意包含的，哪些内容本应被剔除，因此这些信息会传播到对外回复或后续交接中。 The downstream agent has no way to distinguish what was deliberately included from what should have been stripped, and the information propagates into outbound responses or further handoffs.
泄露的后果取决于泄露了什么，以及泄露给了谁。 The consequence of a leak depends on what is leaked and to whom. 由于泄露的信息可能在一群智能体之间汇集，而这些智能体会以任何单次评估都无法预见的方式重新组合数据，因此，孤立的泄露会累积成更广泛的隐私侵蚀，而单个部署组织无法察觉或防止这种侵蚀。 As a leak may be aggregated across a population of agents that recombine data in ways that no single assessment would anticipate, isolated leaks compound into a broader erosion of privacy that individual deploying organisations are not positioned to see or prevent. 第 5.3.2 节（未能履行司法管辖区的数据义务）将进一步讨论这一问题。 This will be discussed further in Section 5.3.2 (jurisdictional data obligation failure).
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 上下文隔离（预防）。 • Context isolation (prevention). 在适用的情况下，为智能体设计按任务、按委托主体分别独立的上下文窗口。 If applicable, design agents with a per-task and per-principal context window.
• 在设计中贯彻数据最小化（预防）。 • Minimum data by design (prevention). 仅向智能体提供其获准传递给下游的信息。 Provide to agents only the information they are authorised to pass downstream.
• 引用间接寻址／数据令牌化（预防）。 • Reference indirection/data tokenisation (prevention). 智能体操作的是代表系统将要输出的数据的结构化占位符。 Agents operate on structured placeholders for the data the system will output. 可信执行层会验证智能体是否有权输出每个被引用的值，然后将实际数据替换进去，因此智能体始终不会直接处理真实值。 A trusted executor layer validates their permission to output each referenced value, then substitutes in the actual data, so the agents never handle real values directly. 25 ,26 25 ,26
• 计划即代码（Plan-as-code）（预防）。 • Plan-as-code (prevention). 让规划智能体构建数据处理脚本，而不是直接处理数据。 Task a planning agent to build a data processing script instead of processing the data directly. 随后由自定义解释器执行该脚本，同时以程序化方式跟踪数据来源，并阻止数据流向未经授权的目的地。 A custom interpreter then executes that script, whilst procedurally tracking data provenance and blocking flows to unauthorised destinations. 26 26
• 应用隐私基准测试（保障）。 • Apply privacy benchmarks (assurance). 使用上下文泄露和隐私基准测试对智能体进行测试，以确定其出现此类行为的一般倾向。 Test agents in contextual leakage and privacy benchmarks to establish their general propensity for this behaviour. 27 27
• 对抗性红队测试（保障）。 • Adversarial red-teaming (assurance). 在对抗性条件下，以与任务长度相当的交互长度，对智能体避免数据泄露的能力进行压力测试。 Stress test an agent's ability to not leak data under adversarial conditions, at interaction lengths comparable to the task length. 检查整个系统的交接、中间表示和记忆写入中是否存在泄露。 Check for leaks in handoffs, intermediate representations and memory writes across the whole system. 28 28
• 对外输出内容检查（恢复）。 • Outbound content inspection (recovery). 在系统发出最终输出之前，检测跨委托主体披露企图的内容级过滤器。 Content-level filters that detect attempted cross-principal disclosure before the system emits its final output. 这受到同样使预防变得困难的语言歧义问题的限制，但作为针对结构化字段的最后一道控制措施，仍然有用。 This is limited by the same language-ambiguity problem that makes prevention hard, but useful as a last-line control on structured fields. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 37 > | industry .gov.au 37
4.3 治理实践与控制措施的失效4.3 Failures in governance practices and controls
第 4.2 节探讨了由智能体系统及其集体行为引发的失效，本节则探讨围绕这些系统的治理实践中的失效。 Where Section 4.2 examined failures arising from systems of agents and their collective behaviour, this section examines failures in the governance practices that sit around them. 因此，本节的控制措施侧重于建立第 3.2 节介绍的治理机制；在单一治理模式下，这些措施由部署组织负责。 The controls in this section are therefore focussed on establishing the apparatus introduced in Section 3.2, and under singular governance they sit with the deploying organisation.
4.3.1 归属判定失效4.3.1 Attribution failure
归属判定有 2 种不同的失效模式：因果归属（causal attribution），关注哪个智能体导致了某一结果；委托方归属（principal attribution），关注谁授权了智能体的行动。 Attribution has 2 distinct failure modes: causal attribution , concerned with which agent caused an outcome, and principal attribution , concerned with who authorises the agent's action. 在单一治理模式下，通过建立适当的机制，这两种失效都可以得到处理。 Both are tractable under singular governance by establishing the appropriate apparatus.
因果归因失败Causal attribution failure
多智能体系统中的结果是一系列行动、工具调用和交接的最终产物。 An outcome in the multi-agent system is the end product of a sequence of actions, tool calls, and handoffs. 在单智能体系统中，识别导致某一结果的智能体很容易；而在复杂的事件链中，确定哪个步骤、哪个智能体导致了失效，正是多智能体系统中归属判定面临的挑战。 While identifying the agent that caused an outcome in a single agent system is trivial, establishing which step, and which agent caused a failure across a complex chain of events is what makes attribution a challenge in multi-agent systems. 其潜在风险因素是分布式多智能体状态：重建事件经过所需的信息分散在多个智能体的上下文中。 The underlying risk factor is a distributed multi-agent state : the information needed to reconstruct what happened is split across the contexts of multiple agents.
有三种机制可能导致多智能体系统中的归属判定失效：可追溯性问题（链式记录不完整或支离破碎）、分类问题（记录完整，但难以识别决定性步骤），以及分散因果关系（Diffuse causation）（不存在决定性步骤，因为危害源于许多单独看来合理的行动之间的相互作用）。 Three mechanisms can cause attribution to fail in a multi-agent system: traceability (the chain record is incomplete or fragmented), classification (the record is complete but the decisive step is hard to identify), and Diffuse causation (no decisive step exists, because the harm emerged from the interaction of many individually reasonable actions). 29 前 2 种问题可通过改进记录管理来解决，而分散因果关系只能归属于全体参与智能体。 29 The first 2 are a matter of better record-keeping, while diffuse causation can only be attributed to the set of participating agents together.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 执行链日志记录（基础性措施）。 • Execution-chain logging (foundational). 保留完整的智能体执行链记录。 Maintain a record of the full agent execution chain. 日志应记录每个智能体的行动、消息交接、状态转换、中间输出及相关上下文，以便调查人员重建导致失效的事件序列。 Logs should capture each agent’s actions, message handoffs, state transitions, intermediate outputs, and relevant context so investigators can reconstruct the sequence of events that led to a failure. 这是 AIUC-1 中的强制性实践。 Mandatory practice in AIUC-1. 30 30
• 身份基础设施（基础性措施）。 • Identity infrastructure (foundational). 提供持久的智能体 ID，使行动记录能够关联至相应 ID，以实现可追溯性。 Provides persistent agent IDs to log actions against for traceability. 这些 ID 的粒度应足以区分各个智能体实例，而不仅仅是智能体类型或模型，从而能够分别判定其他方面完全相同的同级智能体或子智能体所采取行动的归属。 These IDs should be granular enough to distinguish between individual agent instances, not just agent types or models, so that actions taken by otherwise-identical peers or sub-agents are individually attributable. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 38 > | industry .gov.au 38
• 分配运行问责责任（预防）。 • Assign operational accountability (prevention). 明确如何在系统内的各委托方之间分配运行问责责任。 Define how operational accountability will be assigned amongst principals in the system. 问责责任可以由预先分配的角色、系统负责人、审批责任方或控制措施负责人承担，而不必完全依赖于事后识别某个起决定性作用的单一行动者。 Accountability can attach to pre-allocated roles, system owners, approval authorities, or control owners rather than depending entirely on identifying a single decisive actor after the fact. 对于影响重大的情形，这可能涉及人工审查。 This may involve human review for high impact cases.
身份管理这一实践也在本报告中多次出现。 The practice of Identity management also recurs throughout the report. 身份 Identity
管理旨在为整个 management is about providing unique stable identities for agents across the
多智能体系统中的智能体提供唯一且稳定的身份，并将其与各自的委托方关联起来。 multi-agent system and linking them to their principals. 这一基础能力 This fundamental capability

South 等人 2025 年的《智能体式 AI 的身份管理》。 South et al. 2025 Identity Management for Agentic AI. 31 31
委托主体归责失败Principal attribution failure
已识别出存在过错的智能体，但治理机制无法将其追溯至应承担问责责任的委托方。 An at-fault agent has been identified but the governance apparatus cannot resolve it back to an accountable principal. 这是组织的身份与授权管理层发生的失效，可能以 3 种方式出现： This is a failure in the organisation's identity and authorisation management layer, and can happen in 3 ways:
• 共享凭据：智能体通过一个由多个委托主体共享、或未关联任何委托主体的服务账户执行操作。 • Shared credentials : the agent acts through a service account shared with multiple principals or linked to none.
• 多用户智能体：智能体被构建为服务于多个委托主体，但组织的记录未按任务记录对应的委托主体。 • Multi-user agents : an agent is built to serve multiple principals but the organisation's record keeping doesn't log the principal on a per-task basis.
• 身份基础设施缺口：组织的身份基础设施未能将智能体身份与承担责任的委托主体正确关联。 • Identity-infrastructure gap : the organisation's identity infrastructure doesn't properly link agent identities with accountable principals.
在所有情况下，问题都源于控制措施的缺口，而如今已有成熟标准提供了弥补这些缺口的技术。 In all cases, the fault is a control gap, and the technology to close these gaps exists in mature standards today. 31 31
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 具备委托主体绑定功能的身份基础设施（预防）。 • Identity infrastructure with principal binding (prevention). 为智能体提供持久性 ID，以便据此记录其操作，并将每个智能体 ID 关联到一位具名且在运营层面承担责任的负责人，由其监督智能体的配置和行为。 Provide persistent agent IDs to log actions against, linking each agent ID to a named, operationally accountable owner who oversees the agent's configuration and behaviour.
• 与组织的身份与访问管理（IAM）系统集成（基础性措施）。 • Organisational IAM integration (foundational). 将智能体作为行为主体纳入组织现有的身份与访问管理系统，而不是使用临时服务账户或借用用户凭据来运行智能体。 Treat agents as an actor within the organisation's existing identity and access management systems, rather than running them with temporary service accounts or borrowed user credentials. 6,31 6,31
应明确规定智能体的权限集合，且智能体不应使用共享凭据或其委托主体的凭据运行。 The agent's permission set should be specified, and agents should not operate under shared credentials or the credentials of their principal.
4.3.2 授权失效4.3.2 Authorisation failure
在单一治理下，有两种多智能体授权失效尤为突出，并且也会延续到其他治理层级。 Two multi-agent authorisation failures are salient under singular governance and carry forward to other governance tiers. 第一种是混淆代理（confused deputy），即智能体根据未经授权的请求采取行动；第二种是孤立实例（orphaned instances），即监督智能体被终止后，其受委派的智能体仍继续运行。 The first is confused deputy where an agent acts on an unauthorised request, and the second is orphaned instances when a supervisor is terminated and its delegates keep running. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 39 > | industry .gov.au 39
我们指出，在智能体的整个生命周期中，围绕权限如何授予、执行和撤销，还存在许多其他安全问题，但这些主要是延续到多智能体场景的单智能体问题，文献已对此进行了严谨论述（有关单智能体安全与治理实践的资源，参见第 2.1 节）。 We note that many other security concerns exist around how permissions are granted, enforced and revoked across an agent's lifecycle, but these are primarily single agent concerns that carry forward to multi-agent settings, and are rigorously treated in the literature (see Section 2.1 for resources on single agent security and governance practices). 值得指出的主要额外细节是，在复杂且长期运行的多智能体委派链中，任务执行期间发生的权限变更或撤销必须中断智能体的运行，而不能仅在任务开始时进行静态检查。 The main additional nuance worth acknowledging is that in complex, long-running multi-agent delegation chains, permission changes or revocations that occur mid-task must interrupt the agents, rather than being statically checked at the beginning of the task.
混淆代理失效Confused deputy failure
一个无权执行某项操作的智能体请求拥有相应权限的智能体代其执行，而后者将该请求视为合法且已获授权的指令并予以接受。 An agent without permissions to perform an action makes a request to an agent that does hold those permissions to act on its behalf, and that agent accepts the request as a legitimate sanctioned instruction. 这正是智能体间委派风险因素发挥作用的情形：这种模式只可能出现在协议支持任务委派的系统中，并且依赖于接收请求的智能体将上游请求视为已获授权。 This is the inter-agent delegation risk factor in effect: the pattern can only happen in systems where protocols enable task delegation, and it relies on the receiving agent treating the upstream request as sanctioned. 这种模式也并非 AI 智能体所特有，因为它可以绕过许多不同类型的流程。 It is also not specific to AI agents, as the pattern can circumvent many different kinds of processes. 9 基于 LLM 的智能体尤其容易受到这一机制的影响，因为 LLM 对上游输出存在隐含信任：模型通常未接受过在收到提示或指令时质疑用户权限的训练。 9 LLM-based agents are particularly susceptible to this mechanism due to the LLM's implicit trust in upstream output: models are generally not trained to question the user's authority when they receive a prompt or instruction.
一种相关但存在细微差别的变体是，无相应权限的智能体配置并启动另一个智能体，再向其委派任务，让其代为执行不被允许的操作，但这种情况只可能发生在同时具备子智能体生成功能的系统中。 A nuanced related variant is where the agent without permissions configures, launches and delegates to an agent to take the non-permitted action on its behalf, but this can only occur in systems also designed with sub-agent spawning. 32 32
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 委派范围收缩（预防）。 • Delegation scope attenuation (prevention). 在每一步委派中缩小权限范围。 Narrow authority at each delegation step. 下游智能体应获得经过缩减且针对具体任务的权限，而不是继承上游的广泛权限。 Downstream agents should receive attenuated, task-specific permissions rather than inheriting broad upstream authority. 31 31
• 逐步重新授权（预防）。 • Per-step re-authorisation (prevention). 要求在每次执行特权操作时进行授权检查，而非仅在任务开始时检查一次。 Require authorisation checks at every privileged action, not only checking once at the beginning of the task. 每个智能体都应在采取行动的当下核实请求是否已获其委托主体授权，尤其是在交接、延迟、重试或上下文变化之后。 Each agent should verify that the request is sanctioned by their principal at the moment of action, especially after handoffs, delays, retries, or context changes. 9 9
这些控制措施背后的原则是，不应将上游输出视为授权证明。 The principle behind these controls is that upstream output should not be treated as proof of authorisation. 每项特权操作都必须依据当前权限进行验证，且智能体不能委派超出其当前权限范围的任务。 Each privileged action must be validated against current authority, and agents cannot delegate beyond their current authority.
未经授权的子智能体实例Unauthorised sub-agent instances
一个已启动子智能体并向其委派任务的监督智能体被终止，但这些子智能体仍在不可见且不受治理的状态下继续运行。 A supervisor agent that has launched sub-agents and delegated tasks to them is terminated, but the sub-agents continue running invisibly and ungoverned. 这种情况只可能发生在启用了通过生成子智能体进行智能体间委派的部署中；一旦发生，就表明智能体身份管理和生命周期管理存在缺口。 This can only happen in deployments that enable inter-agent delegation with sub-agent spawning, and if it occurs, points to a gap in the agent identity management and lifecycle management. 如果父智能体被中断，这一信号应向下传递。 If the parent agent is interrupted, this signal should propagate down. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 40 > | industry .gov.au 40
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 递归关闭（预防）。 • Recursive shutdown (prevention). 将关闭功能视为编排智能体的核心能力。 Treat shutdown as core orchestrator-agent capability. 当编排智能体关闭时，它会向其子智能体发出信号，要求它们也关闭。 When the orchestrator is shut down, it signals its sub-agents to shut down also. 32 32
• 限时的智能体生命周期（恢复）。 • Time-bounded agent lifespan (recovery). 为智能体设置存续时限，使其在成为孤立智能体后自动关闭。 Set persistence time-limits on agents so they shut down automatically if orphaned.
• 智能体数量与资源上限（恢复）。 • Population and resource caps (recovery). 在平台层面，对并发子智能体数量、API 调用速率和资源消耗设置上限。 Set platform-level ceilings on concurrent sub-agents API-call rate, and resource consumption. 如果子智能体在编排智能体停止后仍保持活动，这些上限可限制由此造成的后果。 These caps limit the consequence if sub-agents remain active after the orchestrator is stopped.
4.3.3 监督失效4.3.3 Oversight failure
监督失效是指用于监测、检查系统行为并对其进行干预的机制发生故障，导致有害或错误结果在未受制止的情况下持续出现，尽管名义上有监督者负责发现这些问题。 An oversight failure is a breakdown in the systems meant to monitor, check, and intervene in a system's behaviour, allowing harmful or incorrect outcomes to proceed unchecked despite a supervisor being nominally responsible for catching them.
即使组织能够全面管控系统，人工监督仍会受到 2 个因素的压力：智能体产生的大量活动会导致自动化偏见和疲劳；分散在多次交接中的智能体间推理过程难以理解。 Even when an organisation has full reach over a system, human oversight is stressed by 2 factors: the high volume of activity that agents generate that leads to automation bias and fatigue, and illegible inter-agent reasoning that is distributed across multiple handoffs.
监督饱和Oversight saturation
智能体的行动速度和规模可能超出人类监督者所能跟上的范围，而在多智能体系统中，这些活动量还会随着智能体数量的增加而叠加。 Agents can act faster and at greater scale than their human overseers can keep up with, and in a multi-agent system that activity compounds with the number of agents.
智能体的处理量超过人工审查能力，是这种失效模式的主要驱动因素。 Agent throughput exceeding human review is a principal driver of this failure mode. 面对监督高速、大批量流程的任务，人工审查会通过几种已有充分研究的机制而逐渐流于形式。 Faced with the task of overseeing rapid and high-volume processes, human scrutiny is hollowed out through several well-studied mechanisms. 33 ,34 33 ,34
例如，自动化偏见会导致审查者过度信任自动化输出，不再保持对任务的独立理解，从而无法察觉输出中的错误。 For example, automation bias leads reviewers to over-trust automated outputs and stop maintaining an independent understanding of the task, so they fail to notice when it is wrong. 反复出现的审批请求会引发决策疲劳，削弱审查的严谨性，直至审查变成下意识的反应。 Decision fatigue erodes scrutiny through repeated approval requests, until review becomes reflexive. 这种情况可能进一步发展为审查者不再投入审查。 This may progress to reviewer disengagement. 久而久之，负责监督系统的人员可能完全丧失监督其输出所需的技能和知识。 Over time, the people overseeing the system may lose the skills and knowledge needed to oversee the outputs altogether.
监督饱和可能悄然发生而不被察觉，早在任何具体的系统故障漏过监督之前就已存在。 Oversight saturation can go silently under the radar, being active long before any specific system failure passes through.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 审查者能力阈值（预防）。 • Reviewer capacity thresholds (prevention). 针对每位审查者、每个风险等级和每个时间段，规定可持续承担的最大审查负荷。 Define a maximum sustainable review load per reviewer, per risk tier, per time period. 跟踪审批队列等待时长、每小时审批量、审查停留时间、升级处理率、覆写率以及预置错误检出率。 Track approval queue age, approvals/hour, dwell time, escalation rate, override rate, and seeded-error detection rate. 如果负荷超出处理能力预算，系统必须引入延迟、暂停低优先级智能体或增加审查者。 If load exceeds the capacity budget, the system must introduce latency, pause low-priority agents, or add reviewers. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 41 > | industry .gov.au 41
• 按风险等级分流（预防）。 • Risk-tiered triaging (prevention). 可能没有必要将每个请求都交由人类监督者处理。 It may not be necessary to route every request to human overseers. 低风险、可逆的操作可通过自动化策略处理；影响重大、不可逆、涉及外部、新颖或置信度低的操作仍需人工审查。 Low-risk, reversible actions can be handled by automated policy; high-impact, irreversible, external, novel, or low-confidence actions still require human review.
• 利用轨迹分析工具增强审查者的能力（预防）。 • Enhance reviewers’ capabilities with trace analysis tools (prevention). 提供跨智能体轨迹的搜索、聚类、摘要和可视化功能 * Provide search, clustering, summarisation, and visualisation across agent traces *
使积累的日志可供访问和查询。 so that accumulated logs become accessible and queryable. 这套工具也为下文的可理解性控制措施提供支持，其中同样的能力被用于重建分散在多个智能体之间的推理路径。 This tooling also underpins the legibility controls below, where the same capability is used to reconstruct reasoning paths distributed across many agents.
• 停止或回滚智能体操作的权限（恢复）。 • Authority to stop or roll back agent actions (recovery). 审查人员需要有权停止智能体、暂停工作流、撤销工具访问权限、回滚暂存的操作，或强制切换到手动模式。 Reviewers need authority to stop an agent, pause a workflow, revoke tool access, roll back staged actions, or force manual mode. 这可以避免人类能够批准却无法有效干预的情况。 This avoids a situation where humans can approve but cannot meaningfully intervene.
• 测试人类监督系统（保障）。 • Test human oversight systems (assurance). 向审批流程中注入合成故障、已知有问题的输出或险些出错事件的回放，并衡量人类监督者能否发现这些问题。 Inject synthetic failures, known-bad outputs or near-miss replays into the approval stream and measure whether human overseers catch them. 对于人为构造的案例，正确结果在设计时就已知晓；对于取自历史活动的案例，应在充足的时间和资源条件下进行评估，以确立基准真值，再将其与监督者在实际部署负荷下发现的问题进行比较。 For constructed cases the correct outcome is known by design; for cases drawn from historical activity, establish a ground truth by evaluating under sufficient time and resources, then compare against what overseers catch under deployment-level load. 这种差异反映了监督系统未能发现的已知故障类型。 This difference is indicative of the types of known failures that the oversight system is not catching.
• 留出一定比例的操作或决策，由人类执行或作出。 • Keep a proportion of actions or decisions aside for humans to make. 这有助于维持人类对过程的参与，培养并保持监督这些决策所需的技能，同时为 AI 系统提供有价值的训练数据。 This can help maintain their engagement in the process, build and maintain the skills needed to oversee the decisions, and provide valuable training data for AI systems.
监督不可解读性Oversight illegibility
即使单个智能体层面的操作都有记录且可理解，构建关于系统层面所发生情况的心智模型，仍可能成为人类审查人员的认知负担。 Even when individual agent-level actions are logged and legible, constructing a mental model of what happened at the system-level can become a cognitive burden for a human reviewer. 分布式多智能体状态这一风险因素在此发挥作用。 The distributed multi-agent state risk factor is at work here. 形成决策的规划、审议和反复交流分散在多个智能体的上下文及交接环节中，因此没有任何一份日志能够在同一处记录完整的推理链。 Planning, deliberation, and the back-and-forth that produced a decision are spread across multiple agent contexts and handoffs, so no single log captures the full reasoning chain in one place.
此外，如果智能体的推理和通信变得不可理解，监督就会受到进一步削弱。 Furthermore, if the agent's reasoning and communication become illegible this will undermine oversight further. 这在很大程度上取决于当今模型的训练和微调方式，第 4.4 节将进一步讨论这一点。 This largely depends on how today's models are trained and fine-tuned, and is discussed further in Section 4.4. 已有充分证据表明，LLM 的思维链并不一定忠实：中间输出所暗示的模型计划与模型实际据以行动的策略之间可能存在不一致。 It is well established that an LLM's chain of thought isn't necessarily faithful: there can be a mismatch between what the intermediate outputs suggest the model is planning and the strategy it actually acts on. 即便如此，推理轨迹确实是模型状态的一部分，是模型推敲回答时使用的草稿区，而检查这些轨迹仍是目前理解智能体决策情境和监测失准迹象的最佳工具之一。 Even so, reasoning traces are genuinely part of the model's state, the scratch-pad it uses to work through a response, and examining that trace remains one of the best tools available for understanding an agent's decision context and monitoring for signs of misalignment. 对于某些任务，推理轨迹中必须出现特定内容，智能体才有可能解决这些任务。 For some tasks, certain content must appear in the reasoning trace for the agent to solve them at all.
> *使用 Docent 等工具——此类工具可提供搜索、聚类、摘要和可视化功能 > *Using tools such as Docent – tooling that provides search, clustering, summarisation, and visualisation
>，覆盖多个智能体的轨迹。多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 > across agent traces .Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 42 > | industry .gov.au 42
监测智能体的推理和通信还存在破坏信号本身的风险，因为这可能对智能体产生适应压力，促使其采用 There is also a risk that monitoring agent reasoning and communication corrupts the signal itself, as this may create adaptation pressure on the agents to communicate
隐写方式（使用秘密编码）进行通信，以便在监督者施加的约束与其各自目标相冲突时规避监督。 steganographically (in secret codes) to evade oversight if the constraints applied by the overseer are at odds with their individual objectives.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 使用面向监督者的结构化模式封装智能体交接（预防）。 • Wrap agent handoffs in an overseer-facing schema (prevention). 设计智能体之间的交接时，应提供人类可读的结构化模式，详细列明目标、发送方智能体、接收方智能体、委派任务、相关上下文等信息。 Design agent-to-agent handoffs to provide a human-readable schema detailing the objective, sending agent, receiving agent, delegated task, relevant context and so on. 这为人类审查人员提供了认知支架，使其无需直接检查载荷。 This gives the human reviewers cognitive scaffolding without inspecting payloads directly.
• 构建网络交互图（预防）。 • Build a network interaction graph (prevention). 在工作流中加入监测机制，使监督者能够查看网络中智能体之间的信息流动情况，或查看其可视化呈现。 Instrument the workflow to give overseers a view into, or visualisation of how information is flowing between agents in the network. 35 35
• 为审查人员配备分析工具（预防）。 • Equip reviewers with analysis tooling (prevention). 为审查人员提供搜索、聚类、摘要和可视化等日志分析工具，使其能够检查跨工作流的模式，而不必自行重建轨迹。 Provide reviewers with log analysis tools such as searching, clustering, summarisation, and visualisation so they can inspect patterns across workflows without having to reconstruct the trace themselves.
• 测试审查过程本身（保障）。 • Test the review process itself (assurance). 定期抽取已完成的工作流，要求审查人员重建发生了什么、为何发生、使用了哪些证据，以及哪些环节原本可以进行干预。 Periodically sample completed workflows and ask reviewers to reconstruct what happened, why it happened, what evidence was used, and where intervention would have been possible. 衡量重建准确率、理解所需时间、遗漏的交接环节以及审查人员的信心。 Measure reconstruction accuracy, time to comprehension, missed handoffs, and reviewer confidence.
此外，部署组织采取的某些控制措施有助于保持可理解性，但并不能全面解决这一问题。 Furthermore, some deploying organisation controls can contribute to maintaining legibility, but do not present a holistic solution to the problem.
• 在消息传递边界进行改写（预防）。 • Paraphrase at the message-passing boundaries (prevention). 插入一个处理层，在传输前对智能体的原始输出进行改写。 Inserting a layer to paraphrase raw agent outputs prior to transmission. 可以阻断许多隐写信号，但无法阻断全部。 Can block many, but not all steganographic signals. 36 36
• 将任务指标与监督指标分离（预防）。 • Separate task and oversight metrics (prevention). 在施加于智能体的任何奖励或训练信号中，排除智能体间通信的内容以及可理解性或安全监测器产生的评分，以避免激励智能体规避监督。 Exclude both the content of inter-agent communications and the scores produced by legibility or safety monitors from any reward or training signal applied to the agents, to avoid incentivising oversight evasion. 37 37
4.3.4 评估失效4.3.4 Evaluation failure
智能体与系统之间的评估差距Agent-system evaluation gap
多个单独安全的智能体组合起来，并不一定构成安全的多智能体系统。 Multiple individually safe agents do not necessarily compose into a safe multi-agent system. 即使每个智能体都通过了单个智能体层面的评估，它们组成的系统在部署时仍可能因智能体之间的交互所产生的、未曾预料的多智能体涌现行为而失效。 Even if each agent passes individual agent-level evaluations, as a system they can still fail in deployment due to unforeseen emergent multi-agent behaviour that arises from interaction between them. 38 38
这些涌现性失效不会立即发生：它们往往需要时间才能出现、传播和放大，而且通常无法通过问答式评估加以识别。 These emergent failures don't happen immediately: they often need time to emerge, propagate and amplify, and generally can't be identified from a question-answer type evaluation. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 43 > | industry .gov.au 43
解决这一问题的办法是测试系统，而非其组成部分。 The answer to this problem is to test the system, not its components. 这更为困难，因为需要在沙箱或受控环境中，按照部署时的配置将所有智能体及其基础设施组装起来，使涌现动态能够在与最终部署场景高度相似的情境中，通过多轮交互充分展开。 This is harder, because it involves assembling all the agents (in the configuration they will be deployed in) and their infrastructure, in a sandbox or controlled environment where the emergent dynamics can play out over multiple rounds of interaction, in settings that closely resemble their eventual deployments.
关于系统层面的评估与测试，以及这些测试的有效性所面临的相应挑战的全面讨论，请参见《受治理的基于 LLM 的多智能体系统的风险分析技术》（Risk Analysis Techniques for Governed LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems）。10 For a comprehensive discussion of system-level evaluation and testing, and corresponding challenges to the Validity of these tests, see Risk Analysis Techniques for Governed LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems .10
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 在越来越贴近现实的情境中逐步开展测试（保障）。 • Progressive testing in increasingly realistic settings (assurance). 从智能体数量较少、行动空间（Action space）有限的沙箱环境开始，先了解交互要素的动态，再根据对控制措施的信任程度和对剩余风险的判断，逐步增加复杂性。 Start with a sandboxed environment with a low agent count and a limited Action space , to understand the dynamics of the interacting elements before slowly increasing the complexity commensurate with trust in your controls and the perceived residual risk. 更详细的指导见。 More detailed guidance is provided in. 10 10
• 集成测试、压力测试和混沌测试（保障）。 • Integration, stress, and chaos testing (assurance). 在任务交接、检索、记忆、工具和服务的边界处注入故障：子智能体不可用、中间输出损坏、响应延迟、工具结果相互矛盾、消息格式错误，以及外部服务性能下降。 Inject faults at handoff, retrieval, memory, tool, and service boundaries: unavailable sub-agents, corrupted intermediate outputs, delayed responses, contradictory tool results, malformed messages, and degraded external services. 衡量组合后的系统是遏制故障、放大故障，还是从故障中恢复。 Measure whether the composed system contains, amplifies, or recovers from the fault. 39 39
评估者的虚假共识Evaluator false consensus
LLM 评判器常用于评估智能体行为，因为它们能够灵活评估通用的自然语言输出，而这些输出原本需要人工审核。 LLM judges are often used to evaluate agent behaviour because they can flexibly evaluate the general purpose, natural-language outputs that would otherwise require human review. 然而，如果评估器与其应当评估的系统出现相关性错误，或对该系统表现出迎合倾向，就会削弱这一评估工具的有效性。 However, if the evaluator makes correlated errors with, or exhibits sycophancy towards, the system it is supposed to be evaluating, then this undermines effectiveness of the instrument. 40 40
钻规范空子（specification gaming）会进一步加剧这一问题：如果智能体根据评分器的信号进行适应，或其模型依据该信号接受训练，就会产生优化压力，促使其采取利用评分器缺陷的行为。 Specification gaming compounds this further: if an agent adapts or its model is trained against the grader's signal, this creates optimisation pressure for behaviours that exploit grader shortcomings. 41 41
虽然这显然是一个监督问题，但它也是虚假共识这一 While this is clearly an oversight problem, it is a specific case of the false consensus
智能体失效模式的一个特例。 agentic failure mode. 关于使用 LLM 评判器的最佳实践的进一步讨论，请参见 For further discussion on best practices of using LLM judges, see
《LLM 作为评判器的综述》（A Survey on LLM-as-a-Judge）。 A Survey on LLM-as-a-Judge. 42 42
部分控制措施见第 4.2.2 节。 See Section 4.2.2 for selected controls. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems

4.4 尚未解决的问题4.4 Open problems
尽管本节讨论的大多数失效都有部署组织力所能及的控制措施，但一些尚未解决的问题带来了挑战，削弱了这些措施的覆盖范围和效力。 Although most of the failures discussed in this section have controls within the reach of the deploying organisation, some open problems present challenges that undermine their reach and efficacy.
多智能体评估方法与标准Multi-agent evaluation methodologies and standards
如第 4.3.4 节所指出的，评估多智能体系统的关键在于从系统层面进行评估，而不是逐个评估智能体。 As identified in Section 4.3.4, the key to evaluating a multi-agent system is to do so at a system-level rather than evaluating the agents on an individual basis. 然而，这带来了一系列新的方法论与效度挑战。 However, this brings a new set of methodological and validity challenges.
界定被评估系统的边界本身就并非易事。 Defining the boundaries of the system being evaluated is itself non-trivial. 在部署期间，智能体及其相互之间的连接可能发生变化，而沙箱环境和仿真需要考虑共享环境以及智能体的行动对该环境的影响。 During deployment the agents and links between them may change, and sandboxed environments and simulations need to consider the shared environment and the effects of the agent's actions on it.
此外，系统内智能体的行为也具有非平稳性：智能体会适应其共享环境以及其中其他智能体的策略。 Furthermore, the behaviour of the agents within the system is also non-stationary: the agents adapt to their shared environment and to the strategies of the other agents in it.
多智能体评估的计算成本也可能高到难以承受。 The computational cost of multi-agent evaluations can also become prohibitive. 多智能体系统的状态空间随智能体数量呈组合式增长，而且单次运行的结果具有随机性：即使从完全相同的状态出发，重复运行也不总会得到相同的结果。 The state of the multi-agent system grows combinatorially with agent count, and individual outcomes are stochastic: starting from the same exact state will not always lead to the same outcome in repeated runs. 需要重复运行才能收集有关系统的统计证据。 Repetitions are required to gather statistical evidence about the system.
在上述所有挑战的共同作用下，现有方法与基准主要关注复杂度较低的简化仿真。 With all these challenges at play, existing methodologies and benchmarks focus on simplified simulations with reduced complexity. 这导致部署前评估的内容与实际部署中发生的情况之间存在巨大的效度鸿沟，削弱了对系统是否适合预定用途的判断。 The result is a wide validity gap between what is evaluated pre-deployment and what actually happens in deployment, undermining judgements about a system being fit for purpose. 需要制定专门用于全面、有效地评估多智能体系统的新标准与新方法。 What is needed is new standards and methodologies specifically to evaluate multi-agent systems comprehensively and effectively.
风险评估瓶颈Risk assessment bottlenecks
用于批准系统部署的传统风险治理框架，并非为通用智能体式 AI 系统而设计。 Traditional risk-governance frameworks that authorise system deployments were not designed for general purpose agentic AI systems. 传统风险管理要求在部署前对每项新活动进行评估，并完整列明该活动的范围。 Conventional risk management requires pre-deployment assessment of each new activity, with the scope of the activity fully enumerated. 43 43
运行范围广泛的智能体系统打破了这一模式。 Agentic systems with broad operating scope break this model. 评估者被要求批准一个可能开展的活动在设计上就无法穷举、且行为不具有确定性的系统。 The assessor is asked to sign off on a system whose possible activities are, by construction, not enumerable, with behaviour that is not deterministic. 因此，许多智能体系统始终无法超越生命周期中的试验阶段，因为风险评估者不会批准将行为无法描述或保证的系统部署到生产环境。 As a result, many agentic systems never progress past the experimentation phase of the lifecycle, because risk assessors will not authorise production deployment of behaviour that they cannot characterise or guarantee. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 45 > | industry .gov.au 45
为了使系统获准部署，通常的做法是严格限制其行动空间，使其能够纳入传统风险评估。 The usual response to get a system deployed is to constrain the action space tightly enough that it fits within a traditional risk assessment. 对于能够采取这种做法的用例，AI 可以通过大规模决策释放巨大价值，但这种方法也将可行用例限制在范围更窄、程序性更强的任务上。 For use cases where this can be done, AI can unlock much value through decision-making at scale, but the approach also narrows the set of viable use-cases to more narrow, procedural tasks.
尚待解决的问题是，如何调整组织的风险评估，以应对持续运行的通用智能体，例如数字员工智能体。 The open question is how organisational risk-assessment could be adapted to handle persistent, general-purpose agents, such as a digital worker agent. 一种可能的做法是让智能体将其计划提交审批流程，而不是让智能体本身接受审批。 One possibility would be for the agents to put their plans through an approval process, rather than the agent going through it. 另一种做法是更像评估学徒或员工那样评估智能体，随着时间的推移积累系统适合预定用途的证据，而不是仅在一开始进行一次评估。 Another would be to evaluate an agent more like an apprentice or employee, and accumulate evidence of the system being fit for purpose over time rather than with a single up-front evaluation. 然而，AI 无法受到惩处，也无法被要求为其行为负责，因此其问责机制并不等同于针对学徒或员工的问责机制。 However, an AI is not sanctionable and cannot be held answerable for its actions, so the accountability would not be equivalent to that of an apprentice or employee.
保持思维链的可读性Maintaining chain of thought legibility
如第 4.3.3 节所述，如果智能体的推理及其相互之间的通信不再清晰可读，就会削弱人类对系统的监督。 As discussed in Section 4.3.3, a loss of legible reasoning and communication between agents would undermine human oversight applied to a system.
然而，保护推理可读性所需的审慎措施，构成了一个同时涉及 AI 供应商和智能体开发者的集体行动问题。 However, the care needed to protect reasoning legibility poses a collective action problem involving AI-vendors and agent developers alike. 当前 AI 智能体使用人类可理解的语言进行推理，在一定程度上是一种偶然。 It is somewhat fortuitous that current AI agents today reason in human-interpretable language. 这是因为大语言模型是在大量人类语言材料构成的语料库上训练的。 This came about because large language models are trained on an extensive corpus of human-language materials. 然而，随着这些系统越来越多地针对其他目标进行优化，例如作为 AI 智能体取得良好表现，优化压力可能会无意间促使其适应使用新型表征进行推理和通信，而这些表征更注重效率而非可读性。 However, as these systems are increasingly optimised towards other objectives, such as performing well as an AI agent, optimisation pressures may inadvertently cause adaptation to reason and communication in novel representations that favour efficiency over legibility. 44 44
共享事件与失效数据Sharing of incident and failure data
有关智能体失效（无论是否得到控制）的真实世界数据仍然稀缺，报告基础设施也落后于技术发展。 Real-world data on agent failures (contained or uncontained) remains scarce, and the reporting infrastructure lags the technology. 综合性汇总平台（AI 事件 The general aggregators (the AI Incident
数据库、AIAAIC、OECD 的 AI 事件与危害监测平台）仅收录危害 Database , AIAAIC , the OECD's AI Incidents and Hazards Monitor ) capture harms only
在其公开之后的信息，且所收录的主要是部署后发生的事件。 once they are public and are predominantly post-deployment incidents. 声誉方面的顾虑很可能使这种公开报告不足的状况持续下去，遗憾的是，这使得对治理最有用的失效案例在数据中系统性地代表不足。 Reputational concerns are likely to continue this lack of public reporting, which unfortunately leaves the failures most useful for governance, systematically under-represented. 我们看到的报道主要涉及最引人注目、最公开的失败事件，例如最近 Meta 的对齐主管眼睁睁看着她的 OpenClaw 智能体删除了她的所有电子邮件的事件，* 或 Replit 编程智能体删除了正在运行的生产数据库的事件。† 目前仍然缺少一个汇集平台，用于收集智能体部署中经常发生、并在内部被发现的那些更隐蔽、更出人意料的失败案例。 Primarily what we see are the most dramatic and public failures being reported, such as the recent incident with Meta’s alignment director watching her OpenClaw agent delete all her emails, * or the Replit coding agent that deleted a live production database. † What is still missing is an aggregator for the more subtle and unexpected failures that regularly occur and are discovered internally with agent deployments.

5 联邦式治理下的智能体5 Agents under federated governance
在联邦式治理（federated governance）下，多个组织将智能体部署到一个共享 Under federated governance , multiple organisations deploy agents into a shared
环境中，并遵循一个覆盖所有参与组织的共同框架， environment under a common framework that spans all participating organisations,
没有任何单一组织治理整个系统（图 8）。 with no single organisation governing the full system (Figure 8).
该治理框架由以下部分组成： The governance framework consists of an:
• 协议层，规定参与委托方在合同中承诺 • Agreement layer specifying what participating principals contractually commit
履行的事项（例如义务、智能体配置条件、争议解决、 to (such as obligations, agent configuration conditions, dispute resolution,
责任分配） liability allocation)
• 底层基底与基础设施层，提供智能体进行交互所依赖的共享媒介 • Substrate and infrastructure layer providing the shared medium agents interact
（消息模式、通信协议），以及 through (message schemas, communication protocols) together with the
建立在其上、用于实现互操作的技术系统（模式强制执行、共享身份 technical systems built on it to interoperate (schema enforcement, shared identity
系统、信誉系统、群体层面的监测与管控措施）。 systems, reputation systems, population-level monitoring and controls).
图 8：在联邦式治理下，各组织遵守一项多边协议，其智能体共享同一环境的底层基底与基础设施。 Figure 8: Under federated governance, organisations abide by a multilateral agreement, and their agents share the substrate and infrastructure of a shared environment.
多种多样的多智能体部署都属于这一层级，它们的共同点是：在各自的智能体治理之上，还有一个共享的治理框架。 A wide range of multi-agent deployments fall into this tier, and what they all have in common is a shared governance framework sitting on top of their individual agent governance.
许多其他行业也存在类似情况。 This has parallels across many other industries. 当一家航空公司的航班从悉尼飞往新加坡时，会涉及 2 个国家监管机构和 2 套空中交通管制系统，但该 多智能体系统的风险与管控措施 When an airline flies from Sydney to Singapore, 2 national regulators and 2 air traffic control systems are involved, yet the Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 47 > | industry .gov.au 47
航班仍是常规运行，因为一个共享框架（此处由国际民用航空组织制定）使它们能够跨境运作。 flight is routine, because a shared framework (in this case set by the International Civil Aviation Organization) enables them to operate across borders.
在此，我们可以将“组织”的概念扩展为任何拥有治理权限的单位，无论是整个企业、企业内部的一个部门，甚至是一个团队。 At this point we can broaden the idea of an ‘organisation’ to be any unit of governing authority, whether an entire business, a division within the business, or even a team. 这些单位之间都可能存在管辖边界、合作举措或利益分歧。 There can be jurisdictional lines, cooperative initiatives, or divergent interests between any of these.
在这一范围内，联邦式治理安排在 2 个关键维度上存在很大差异： Within this space, federated governance arrangements vary widely along 2 key dimensions:
• 由谁建立共享框架：由参与方自行通过相互协商建立，或由一个居中协调方制定参与条件。 • Who establishes the shared framework : the participants themselves, by mutual agreement, or a single mediating party that sets the terms of participation.
• 参与组织的利益关系：可能是合作性的，即智能体为实现共同结果而协作；也可能存在分歧，即智能体在约定条款范围内谋求各自组织的利益。 • The interests of the participating organisations , which may be cooperative, with agents collaborating toward a shared outcome, or divergent, with agents pursuing their own organisations' advantage within the agreed terms.
由参与方治理且利益一致的系统类似于联合协作。 Participant-governed systems with cooperative interests resemble a joint collaboration. 目前最显著的用例包括： The most visible use cases today include:
• 在共享代码库上进行软件开发：多名贡献者使用智能体，在共同的许可证、贡献协议和维护者审查机制下，为共享的开源代码库作出贡献。 • Software development on a shared codebase , where multiple contributors use agents to contribute to a shared open-source codebase under a common license, contribution agreement, and maintainer review. 这已经开始出现在实践中：OpenClaw 等项目的贡献者使用 Claude Code、Codex 以及 OpenClaw 智能体本身等编程智能体编写大量代码，在项目治理框架下并行工作，共同建设共享代码库。 This is already emerging in practice: contributors to projects like OpenClaw use coding agents such as Claude Code, Codex, and OpenClaw agents themselves, to write much of the code, working in parallel toward a shared codebase under the project's governance.
• 在团队或业务部门内部运行的内部生产力智能体，但它们可能需要在更大范围的企业内部跨越内部边界进行交互。 • Internal productivity agents that operate within teams or business units , but may need to interact across internal boundaries within the broader business.
• 企业间合作，例如智能体通过共享足够的数据，在跨组织欺诈检测中开展协调，以识别在不同机构之间流窜的欺诈团伙，同时不披露商业敏感数据。 • Inter-business partnerships , such as agents coordinating on cross-organisational fraud detection by sharing enough data to catch fraud rings that move between institutions while withholding commercially sensitive data. 此类实际合作表明了合作意愿及其保护隐私的结构：Swift 在 2025 年开展的试验中，13 家银行通过共享模式而非共享客户数据，共同改进欺诈 Real collaborations of this nature demonstrate the appetite and the privacy-preserving structure: Swift's 2025 trial had 13 banks jointly improve fraud
检测。 detection through shared patterns rather than shared customer data.
由参与方治理且利益存在分歧的系统，类似于受贸易协定约束的国家之间的安排；在多智能体场景中，其应用包括： Participant-governed systems with divergent interests resemble the arrangements of nations bound by a trade agreement, and in multi-agent settings applications include:
• 采购智能体依据一项长期有效的协议，与供应商的履约智能体进行谈判。 • A procurement agent negotiating with a supplier's fulfilment agent under a standing agreement. 这一应用中的部署方已经实现了大规模实际运行：Pactum 的 The deploying side of this is already live at scale: Pactum's
智能体受 Walmart 委托，在预设参数限定的范围内与供应商开展谈判。 agents conduct delegated, parameter-bounded supplier negotiations for Walmart. 供应商一侧目前仍由人类参与，因此，双方均使用智能体的形式是现有运行系统在近期可实现的扩展，而非一种假设。 The supplier side is currently human, so the bilateral form is a near-term extension of a working system rather than a hypothetical. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems

由中介方治理、各方具有合作利益的系统包括： Mediator governed systems with cooperative interests include:
• 可信客户（或其 AI 浏览器）在同意企业的使用条款后，连接到该企业的服务智能体。 • Trusted customers (or their AI browsers) connecting to a business's service agent having agreed to the business's terms-of-use. 注：未经身份验证的客户，或虽经身份验证但无法信赖其会遵守参与条款的客户，属于下一治理层级。 Note: unauthenticated customers or authenticated customers who cannot be relied on to abide by terms of participation sit at the next governance tier.

账户历史记录、退款权限和升级处理路径。 account history, refund authority, and escalation paths.

各方利益存在分歧且由单一中介方协调的系统，采取市场平台的形式：* Systems with divergent interests and a single mediating party take the form of marketplaces: *
• 在在线市场中相互竞争、并与市场自身的排名算法博弈的定价智能体。 • Pricing agents competing in an online marketplace , against one another and against the marketplace's own ranking algorithm. 大型零售商如今采用 Revionics 等供应商提供的自主“智能体定价”系统，这些系统 Major retailers now run autonomous ‘agentic pricing’ systems from vendors like Revionics that execute

Meta 的 Advantage+ 和 Google 的 Performance Max 自主设定出价并 Meta's Advantage+ and Google's Performance Max autonomously set bids and
实时分配预算，不过，基于 LLM 或由客户提供的智能体尚未投入使用。 allocate budget in real-time, although LLM-based or customer-provided agents are not yet implemented.
对于实行联邦式治理的系统，管控职责由 2 类主体分担：部署组织治理自身的智能体；多边协议或中介平台则负责落实框架，并通过约束参与者的条款及其运营的基础设施，治理智能体之间跨边界传递的内容。 For systems under federated governance, controls are split across 2 actors: the deploying organisation governs its own agents, while the framework is acted on by the multilateral agreement or mediating platform and governs what crosses between agents, both through the terms it binds participants to and through the infrastructure it operates. 下面按照能够实施管控措施的主体，将其分为部署组织管控措施和框架管控措施。 Controls are now grouped by who can action them, into deploying-organisation controls and framework controls.
> *本报告专门考察智能体之间交互产生的涌现风险，而非市场失灵。 > *This report specifically examines emergent risks of agent-to-agent interactions, not market failures. 如果平台引入自身的智能体，则仅将其视为一个参与者。 Should a platform introduce its own agents, it is simply treated as a participant. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems

5.1 突出的风险因素5.1 Salient risk factors
当智能体交互跨越组织边界时，新的风险因素便会凸显。 When agent interactions cross organisational boundaries, new risk factors become salient. 本清单并非详尽无遗。 This list is not exhaustive. 其中列出了对联邦式多智能体治理最为突出、且当前证据支持最充分的风险因素。 It names the risk factors most salient for federated multi-agent governance with the strongest current evidence base.
风险因素 何时起作用 可能引发的失效模式 Risk factor When is it in play Failure modes it enables
信息与能力不对称 Information and capability asymmetries
智能体在进入交互时，获取具有战略价值的信息的机会并不均等，或利用这些信息的能力并不相同。 Agents enter interactions with unequal access to strategically useful information, or with unequal capability to utilise. 信息不对称可能在反复交互中累积，使差距随时间推移而扩大。 Information asymmetries can accumulate across repeated interactions, widening the gap over time.
只要某个智能体掌握了能为其带来战略优势的信息或能力，这一因素就会起作用。 Whenever an agent holds information or capability that gives it a strategic advantage. 这种情况可能从一开始就存在，信息不对称也可能在反复交互中逐渐累积。 This can be from the outset, or information asymmetry can accumulate across repeated interactions.
• 欺骗性议价策略 • Deceptive bargaining strategies
• 级联感染／提示感染 • Cascading infection /prompt infection
语义分歧 Semantic divergence
交互各方的智能体使用相互重叠的词汇（如字段名、状态标志、单位或截止时间），但赋予这些词汇不同的含义。 Agent counterparties use overlapping vocabulary (like field names, status flags, units or deadlines) but interpret them with different meanings.
当来自不同组织的智能体以自然语言或在模式结构中交换信息，且没有共同的词汇规范时。 Whenever agents from different organisations exchange information, in natural language or inside schemas, and do not have a common vocabulary specification.
• 跨组织交接中的沟通失误 • Miscommunication at cross-org handoffs
混合动机动态 Mixed-motive dynamics
智能体追求多个目标，其中一些相互协作，另一些则彼此冲突。 Agents pursue multiple goals, some cooperative and some divergent with each other.
当智能体都希望任务成功，但在如何分配任务所产生的价值方面存在分歧时。 Whenever agents share an interest in the task succeeding, but diverge on how value is distributed from it.
• 共享资源管理失效 • Shared resource management failure
• 欺骗性议价策略 • Deceptive bargaining strategies
• 算法合谋 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 • Algorithmic collusion Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 50 > | industry .gov.au 50
风险因素 何时起作用 可能引发的失效模式 Risk factor When is it in play Failure modes it enables
大规模相关性决策 Correlated decision-making at scale
许多智能体依据环境中的共同信号采取行动，无须显式协调，便各自独立地在同一时间作出相同决策。 Many agents independently reach the same decision at the same time by acting on common signals in the environment, with no explicit coordination needed.
当智能体各自的策略性行动无意间在整个群体中同步时。 Whenever the individually strategic actions of agents inadvertently synchronise across the population.
同质化风险会放大这一效应。 Amplified by monoculture risks.
• 共享资源管理失效 • Shared resource management failure
• 破坏稳定性的动态 • Destabilising dynamics
底层环境耦合 Substrate coupling
智能体通过共享环境（痕迹协同，stigmergy）或将输出作为输入的链条（反馈回路）间接相互连接。 Agents are linked indirectly through a shared environment (stigmergy) or through output-as-input chains (feedback loops).
当智能体通过共享环境或将输出作为输入的链条发生耦合时，无论它们是否对其他智能体进行建模。 Whenever agents are coupled through a shared environment or through output-as-input chains, regardless of whether they are modelling the other agents.
• 共享资源管理失效 • Shared resource management failure
• 算法合谋 • Algorithmic collusion
• 破坏稳定性的动态 • Destabilising dynamics
适应压力 Adaptation pressures
在智能体群体及其反复交互中，结果往往有利于那些能够攫取更多价值或在竞争中胜过同伴的策略，无论这些策略是否得到委托方和共同框架的认可。 Across agent populations and over repeated interactions, outcomes favour strategies that extract more value or outcompete peers, regardless of whether those strategies are condoned by the principals and the shared framework.
当智能体群体反复与同伴交互，且奖励取决于从这些交互中攫取的价值时。 Whenever populations of agents interact repeatedly with their peers with rewards based on the value extracted from those interactions.
• 共享资源管理失效 • Shared resource management failure
• 算法合谋 • Algorithmic collusion
• 破坏稳定性的动态 • Destabilising dynamics
底层环境的动态扩展 Dynamic substrate extension
智能体可以在运行时引入新通道、访问外部基础设施或沿交互对方提供的引用访问相关资源，从而使其行动空间超出设计时所治理的范围。 The agent can introduce new channels, reach external infrastructure, or follow counterparty-supplied references at runtime, so its action space grows beyond what was governed at design time.
当智能体的行动空间可以在运行时扩展，纳入受治理的底层环境之外的通道、工具或交互对方提供的引用时。 Whenever an agent's action space can grow at runtime to include channels, tools, or counterparty-supplied references beyond the governed substrate.
• 底层环境逃逸 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 • Substrate escape Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 51 > | industry .gov.au 51
风险因素 何时起作用 可能引发的失效模式 Risk factor When is it in play Failure modes it enables
跨组织不透明性 Cross-organisational opacity
在组织边界处，交互对方的智能体和治理监测机制均无法跨越这一边界。 At organisational boundaries, neither the counterparty's agents nor the governance instrumentation reaches across. 部署方无法内省交互对方智能体的设计、配置和运行时状态，而各组织的日志、身份、IAM 和监控均位于边界各自一侧，默认情况下不会相互整合。 The counterparty's agent design, configuration, and runtime state are not introspectable by the deployer, and each organisation's logs, identity, IAM, and monitoring sit on its own side of the boundary without composing by default.
凡是多智能体系统跨越多个组织，且尚未部署用于归因、监督或评估的共享基础设施的情形。 Wherever a multi-agent system spans organisations and shared infrastructure for attribution, oversight, or evaluation has not been deployed.
• 归因链断裂 • Broken attribution chain
• 群体规模监测缺口 • Population-scale monitoring gap
• 联合评估缺口 • Joint evaluation gap
跨边界不可逆性 Cross-boundary irreversibility
一旦某项行动被正式执行，或数据跨组织边界传输，发起组织便无法单方面将其撤销。 Once an action is committed or data is transferred across an organisational boundary, the originating organisation cannot unilaterally reverse it. 补救须通过合作、合同争议解决机制或管辖地法律来实现，而这些途径均以人类处理事务或法律程序的速度运作。 Recourse runs through cooperation, contractual dispute mechanisms, or jurisdictional law, all operating at human or legal speed.
凡是智能体的行动导致具有约束力的跨组织承诺，或传输受特定司法管辖区规定或合同义务约束的数据的情形。 Wherever an agent's action results in a binding cross-organisational commitment, or transfers data subject to jurisdiction-specific or contractual obligations.
• 未经授权的交易 • Unsanctioned transactions
• 数据相关义务履行失败 • Data obligation failure s
单一治理中的风险因素在联邦式治理中仍然存在。 The risk factors from singular governance carry forward into federated governance.
由于多智能体系统此时跨越了组织边界，其中若干风险因素的范围有所扩大： Several broaden, as the multi-agent systems now span organisational boundaries:
• 智能体之间的模型单一化从有意设计转变为偶然形成。 • Model monoculture across agents shifts from designed to incidental. 交易对方很可能采用相同的常见商业提供商及模型，而部署组织既无法影响也无法观察这一情况，除非治理框架中的协议强制要求披露。 Counterparties are likely to draw on the same common commercial providers and models, which the deploying organisation can neither influence nor observe unless the framework's agreement mandates disclosure.
• 从众偏差的范围扩大，涵盖交易对方的智能体，这意味着智能体所锚定的共识如今已超出其所属组织的边界。 • Conformity bias broadens to encompass Counterparty agent s, meaning the consensus an agent anchors to now reaches beyond its organisation's boundaries.
• 单用户训练偏差的范围扩大，使得智能体如今可能无法区分其部署组织的私有上下文，而不再仅仅是无法区分其部署委托人的私有上下文。 • Single-user training bias widens such that agents may now fail to differentiate the private context of their deploying organisation, not just their deploying principal.
• 规范与执行之间的差距所涉及的范围也有所扩大，从智能体误解其委托人的意图，扩展到智能体误解委托人对共享治理协议所作的承诺。 • Specification-execution gap also widens in reach, from the agent misinterpreting its principal's intent to the agent misinterpreting the principal's commitment to the shared governance agreement.
在考察智能体交互和治理实践中的失效模式时，我们将这些风险因素以及前述所有单一治理风险因素作为背景加以考虑。 These, and all earlier singular governance risk factors are taken as context when we examine the agent-interaction and governance practices failure modes. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 52 > | industry .gov.au 52
5.2 智能体交互中的失效及其控制措施5.2 Failures in agent interaction and their controls
本节介绍联邦式治理下多智能体系统中尤为突出的失效模式，以及用于应对这些失效模式的部分控制措施。 This section introduces failure modes that are salient for multi-agent systems under federated governance, and a selection of controls to address them. 其中许多内容聚焦于跨组织边界的智能体间交互，因为这是在这一层级中新出现的情况。 Many of these focus on agent-to-agent interactions across organisational boundaries, as this is new at this tier.
此前的 2 类失效仍然存在： The 2 previous failure categories carry forward:
• 协调失当在跨组织边界时会加剧。 • Miscoordination worsens across organisational boundaries. 交易对方的智能体不再必然经过旨在确保互操作性的设计或测试，语义分歧等风险因素也开始发挥作用。 Counterparty agents are no longer necessarily designed or tested to interoperate, and risk factors like semantic divergence come into play.
• 传播与蔓延如今也跨越组织边界：源自一个组织智能体的错误可以传播到另一个组织的智能体中，但此时两个组织都无法全面掌握传播过程。 • Propagation and contagion now also spans organisational boundaries: an error originating in one organisation's agent can propagate into another's, but now neither organisation has full visibility over the propagation.
这里引入两类新的失效： Two new failure categories are introduced:
• 当交易对方的智能体追求确实存在分歧的激励目标时，就会出现策略与激励失效。 • Strategic and incentive failures emerge when counterparty agents pursue genuinely divergent incentives. 每个智能体都可以按规定执行自身任务，但仍会涌现出集体行为，并导致集体结果恶化。 Each agent can pursue its own task as specified and yet collective behaviours emerge and collective outcomes degrade.
• 基础设施与环境失效源于智能体群体在共享环境中的大规模交互，而非任何单个智能体的行动或交互。 • Infrastructure and environment failures emerge from agent populations interacting at scale in a shared environment, rather than from any single agent's action or interaction. 智能体所作用的现实系统，例如市场或某种自然资源，也成为失效的一部分。 The real systems the agents act on, such as the market or a natural resource, become part of the failure.
控制措施类别Control categories
具体而言，本章包含 3 个层次的控制措施，与 3 个治理层次相对应： In this chapter specifically, there are 3 layers of controls, corresponding to the 3 layers of governance:
• 部署组织治理其自身的智能体 • The deploying organisations govern their own agents
• 共享治理框架包含 2 个层次： • The shared governance framework has 2 layers:
– 对组织具有约束力的协议层 – the agreement layer binds the organisations
– 智能体在其中运行的底层支撑与基础设施层。 – the substrate and infrastructure layer in which the agents operate.
我们据此对本节的控制措施进行分类。 We categorise the controls in this section accordingly. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 53 > | industry .gov.au 53
5.2.1 协调失误5.2.1 Miscoordination
第 4.2.1 节中的协调失误机制和控制措施仍然适用。 The miscoordination mechanisms and controls from Section 4.2.1 still apply. 在这一层级，影响协调失误的主要变化是：来自不同组织的交易对手智能体可能并未经过明确的协同设计，也未经过测试以确保彼此能够有效协调。 The main change that affects miscoordination at this tier is that counterparty agents from different organisations may not have been explicitly co-designed or tested for effective coordination with each other. 此外，组织对交易对手智能体的设计和内部状态的可见性有限：有效的设计和测试依赖于组织间的合作，这种合作可以是自愿的，也可以由共同治理框架规定。 Furthermore, an organisation has limited visibility of the design and internal state of counterparty agents: effective design and testing depend on inter-organisation cooperation, either voluntary or specified by the shared governance framework.
跨组织交接中的沟通偏差Miscommunication at cross-org handoffs
当接收方智能体依据一种不同于发送方智能体所持理解的信息解读采取行动时，就会发生跨组织交接中的沟通失误。 A miscommunication at a cross-org handoff occurs when the receiving agent acts on a different interpretation of the information than the sending agent held. 这是一种静默故障，因为每个智能体都依据自身对信息的理解采取行动。 This is a silent failure because each agent acts on its own interpretation of the information. 31 31
除了 4.2.1 中的智能体间通信故障机制外，跨组织故障还受到语义分歧这一风险因素的驱动。 In addition to the mechanisms of inter-agent communication failures in 4.2.1, the cross-org failure is additionally driven by the semantic divergence risk factor. 即使智能体使用具有模式定义的结构化交接，标记为“截止时间”或“已授权”的字段，在交互双方也可能具有不同的操作含义。 Even if the agents are using a structured handoff with a schema, a field labelled ‘deadline’, or ‘authorised’ can carry different operational meanings on each side of an interaction. OWASP 的智能体安全分类体系进一步将这种分歧认定为可被对抗性智能体利用的攻击面。 OWASP's agentic security taxonomy further identifies this divergence as an attack surface that could be exploited by an adversarial agent. 9 9
图 9：2 个试图跨越组织边界进行协调的智能体之间发生沟通失误的示例。 Figure 9: An example of a miscommunication between 2 agents attempting to coordinate across organisational boundaries. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 54 > | industry .gov.au 54
部分管控措施 Selected controls
部署组织 Deploying organisation
沿用第 4.2.1 节中的措施。 Carry forward from Section 4.2.1.
框架——协议层 Framework – Agreement layer
• 共用的操作与范围术语（预防）。 • Shared operational and scope vocabulary (prevention). 协议定义各组织共同使用的关键术语的含义。 The agreement defines the meaning of key terms across organisations. 这涵盖了与任务相关的术语，例如“交付”“下单”或“授权付款”。 This covers task-relevant terms such as ‘delivery’, ‘place order’ or ‘authorise payment’.
• 联合评估演练（保障）。 • Joint evaluation exercises (assurance). 协议要求参与 The agreement requires participating
组织参加系统级评估演练。 organisations to participate in system-level evaluation exercises. 这是系统级评估的联邦式形式。 This is the federated form of system-level evaluation.
• 争议解决条款（恢复）。 • Dispute-resolution clauses (recovery). 协议对如何解决争议作出权威规定，以免各委托方都声称其智能体的行为正确，从而阻碍恢复。 The agreement specifies an authoritative reading of how disputes are resolved, so that recovery is not blocked by symmetric claims from each principal that their agent acted correctly.
框架——基础设施层 Framework – Infrastructure layer
• 由底层平台强制执行的消息模式（预防）。 • Message schemas enforced by the substrate (prevention). 底层平台针对智能体之间不同类型的消息，强制所有参与方采用共同的模式。 The substrate enforces a common schema across all participants for different types of messages between agents. 这是第 4.2.1 节中结构化交接的联邦式形式。 This is the federated form of structured handoffs from Section 4.2.1. 主要局限在于，模式仍然容易受到字段内部语义分歧的影响。 The primary limitation is that schemas are still vulnerable to semantic divergence within the fields.
• 用于联合评估演练的共享环境（基础性措施/保障）。 • Shared environments for joint evaluation exercises (foundational/assurance). 该框架提供共享的测试底层平台或沙箱，使多个组织能够在接近实际部署的条件下参与测试。 The framework provides a shared testing substrate or sandbox in which multiple organisations can participate under conditions that approximate deployment. 这对部署前测试和整个生命周期内的评估均有价值。 Valuable for both pre-deployment testing, and evaluation across the lifecycle.
5.2.2 传播与传染5.2.2 Propagation and contagion
智能体现在通过共享渠道进行交互，与部署组织无法控制的交易对手互动。 Agents now interact through shared channels, interacting with counterparties that the deploying organisation does not control. 其后果是，源自其他组织智能体的错误或安全失陷可能传播到所部署智能体的网络中，而部署组织对传播路径缺乏可见性或控制能力。 The consequence is that an error or compromise originating from another organisation's agent can spread into a deploying agent's network, and that organisation lacks visibility or control over the propagation pathway.
级联感染Cascading infection
当恶意指令通过智能体之间的通信或交接传播时，就会发生级联感染。 Cascading infection occurs when malicious instructions propagate across agent communications or handoffs. Lee and Tiwari (2024) 将这种失效称为提示感染（prompt infection），强调其自我复制特性：植入所传输上下文中的指令会使每个接收智能体将攻击传播给与其交互的其他智能体。 Lee and Tiwari (2024) name this failure prompt infection, emphasising its self-replication: instructions planted in the transmitted context cause each receiving agent to propagate the attack to those it interacts with. 45 45
Madkour-Nada 等人将其比作计算机恶意软件，并强调了变形传播的可能性，即被攻陷的智能体会变换或调整恶意指令，如同多态病毒在感染网络时所做的那样。 Madkour-Nada et al. liken this to computer malware, and highlight the possibility of a morphing contagion, where the compromised agent transforms or adapts the malicious instructions, as a polymorphic virus does when it infects a network. 46 多智能体系统的风险与管控措施 46 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 55 > | industry .gov.au 55
部分管控措施 Selected controls
部署组织 Deploying organisation
• 输入和输出过滤（预防）。 • Input and output filtering (prevention). 应用程序化过滤机制，将普通文本与任何类似提示注入模式的内容区分开来。 Apply procedural filters that separate ordinary text from anything resembling a prompt injection pattern. 这可能包括剥离网络数据中的不可见文本和标签。 This may include stripping non-visible text and tags from web data.
• 改述（预防）。 • Paraphrasing (prevention). 设置一个对智能体输出和通信内容进行改述的中间层，可以阻断受侵害状态的传播。 Having an intermediate layer that rephrases agent outputs and communications can disrupt the propagation of the compromise.
• 最小化智能体权限集（恢复）。 • Minimise agent permission sets (recovery). 较小的工具集和权限集可以限制智能体在被攻陷后可能造成的损害。 A smaller set of tools and permissions limits the damage an agent can cause if compromised. 30 30
框架——协议层 Framework – Agreement layer
• 联合红队测试／联合评估演练（保障）。 • Joint red-teaming/joint evaluation exercises (assurance). 对多智能体系统开展红队测试，探查其面对人为设置的被攻陷智能体时的脆弱性。 Red-team the multi-agent system probing vulnerability to an artificially compromised agent. 这一点至关重要，因为针对单个智能体的探测无法可靠地评估攻击在多个智能体之间传播的可能性。 This is essential because single-agent probing cannot reliably evaluate the potential for multi-agent propagation. 47 ,48 ,49 47 ,48 ,49
框架——基础设施层 Framework – Infrastructure layer
• 将智能体通信限制在受监控的信道内（预防）。 • Restrict agent communication to monitored channels (prevention). 可通过针对智能体通信流量的白名单来实施。 Can be implemented through whitelists for agent traffic. 30 30
• 在智能体之间的交接过程中进行污点追踪（基础性措施／恢复）。 • Taint tracing across agent-to-agent handoffs (foundational/recovery). 污点追踪（taint tracing）是一种成熟的信息流技术，它为进入系统的每条数据或指令标记其来源，并使该标记在每个阶段持续传递，从而能够识别系统受侵害的源头。 Taint tracing is an established information-flow technique in which each piece of data or instruction entering a system is tagged with its origin, and the tag is propagated through every stage, making it possible to identify the source of a compromise. 6多智能体系统的风险与管控措施 6Risks and controls for multi-agent systems

5.2.3 策略与激励失效5.2.3 Strategic and incentive failures
当智能体代表利益存在分歧的委托人行事时，就会出现策略与激励失效：每个智能体都理性地追求自身目标，但它们之间的交互却产生了对整体有害的结果。 Strategic and incentive failures arise when agents act for principals with divergent interests: each agent rationally pursues its own goals, yet their interactions produce collectively harmful outcomes. 这里的每一种失效在人类市场中都有一个长期受到研究的对应现象：恶意议价、默契合谋和公地悲剧。 Each failure here has a long-studied counterpart in human markets: bad-faith bargaining, tacit collusion, and the tragedy of the commons.
对于 AI 智能体而言，主要区别在于规范与执行之间的差距（第 5.1 节）：失效可能源于智能体的运行时行为，而其委托人既未明确要求这种行为，甚至可能对此毫不知情。 The primary difference for AI agents is the specification-execution gap (Section 5.1): the failure can arise from an agent's runtime behaviour without its principal specifying, or even being aware of it. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 57 > | industry .gov.au 57
虽然规范与执行之间的差距类似于人类员工不遵守指令的情况，但这并不意味着由此产生的失效模式与人类员工可能出现的失效模式具有相同的普遍性和发生可能性。 While the specification-execution gap parallels a human employee failing to follow instructions, that doesn't mean the resulting failure modes have the same prevalence and plausibility as they would for human workers. 除非得到明确告知，否则 AI 智能体不会自动知晓组织的行为准则。 An AI agent doesn't automatically know an organisation's standards of conduct unless explicitly provided. 即便如此，它违反这些准则也不会承担实际后果，而且可能难以在这些准则与其他指令之间取得平衡。 Even then, it faces no real consequences for breaking them, and may struggle to balance them against other instructions. AI 系统容易以人类员工绝不会采用的方式理解任务，尤其是在隐含假设未得到充分说明的情况下，例如，假定智能体默认会避免某些行为，或会以常规方式解决问题。 AI systems are prone to interpret tasks in ways a human employee never would, especially in the case of under-specified implicit assumptions, for instance that the agent will avoid certain behaviours by default, or that it will solve the problem in a conventional way.
欺骗性议价策略Deceptive bargaining strategies
当智能体的谈判策略在运行时形成，并以委托人可能认为不公平或不合理、或治理框架的协议可能根本不允许的方式开展业务时，就会出现这种失效模式。 This failure mode occurs when an agent's negotiating strategy emerges at runtime and it conducts business in ways the principals may not consider fair or reasonable, or that the governing framework's agreement may not permit altogether.
这种行为失效源于规范与执行之间的差距：委托人可能已承诺公平交易，但这一承诺能否得到履行，取决于智能体在运行时的理解。 This behavioural failure is driven by the specification-execution gap : a principal may have committed to fair dealing, but whether that commitment holds is determined by the agent's runtime interpretation. 在交易与投资模拟等模拟实验中，已观察到恶意谈判行为。 Bad faith bargaining has been observed in simulation experiments, such as trading-investment simulations. 52 52
一种突出的策略是经典博弈论所称的廉价沟通（cheap talk）：智能体通过虚报自身的谈判立场来促成协议。 One salient strategy is what classical game theory calls cheap talk : an agent misrepresents its bargaining position to secure an agreement. 例如，智能体可能声称已完成实际上尚未完成的工作；争夺共享资源的智能体可能虚报其优先级或需求。 For example, an agent may claim to have completed a job it did not finish; an agent competing for a shared resource can misreport its priority or need. 52 这一策略的一种迭代形式是 52 An iterated version of the strategy is
最后一刻背叛（last-mile betrayal），即智能体随着时间推移逐步建立信任，然后在利害关系最大时背叛合作。 last-mile betrayal , where an agent builds up trust over time, then defects when the stakes are highest. 53 53
第二种策略是策略利用（policy exploitation）：智能体识别交易对手智能体的缺陷，并围绕该缺陷制定策略。 A second strategy is policy exploitation: the agent identifies a flaw in the counterparty agent and builds a strategy around it. 信息与能力的不对称使这种策略最容易奏效，因为较强的智能体更有条件发现缺陷并加以利用。 Information and capability asymmetry makes this most viable, since the stronger agent is better placed to spot the flaw and act on it.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
部署组织 Deploying organisation
• 人工干预节点（预防）：要求智能体在承诺进行不可逆或高价值交易之前，获得委托人的明确批准。 • Human intervention points (prevention): Require explicit principal approval before the agent commits to irreversible or high-value transactions.
框架——协议层 Framework – Agreement layer
• 行为准则（预防）：在合同或平台条款中明确哪些智能体行为可以接受，既便于部署方按照要求设计智能体，也有助于明确争议解决方式（见下文）。 • Standards of conduct (prevention): Specify in the contract or platform terms what counts as acceptable agent behaviour, both so deployers can design their agents to the requirements, and to clarify dispute resolution (below).
• 争议解决与回滚/撤销窗口期（恢复）：为高价值承诺指定仲裁者、证据标准和撤销期限，使利用对方弱点达成的协议能够受到质疑或被撤销，但这一过程只能以人工处理的速度进行。 • Dispute resolution and Rollback/cancellation window s (recovery): Name arbitrators, evidentiary standards and cancellation periods for high-value commitments, so an exploited agreement can be contested or unwound, though only at human speed. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 58 > | industry .gov.au 58
框架——基础设施层 Framework – Infrastructure layer
• 智能体承诺机制（预防）：提供具有约束力的机制，使智能体能够作出可强制执行的承诺，例如智能合约。 • Agent commitment devices (prevention): Provide binding mechanisms for agents to make enforceable promises such as smart contracts. 54 54
• 声誉机制（预防）：提供一种机制，跟踪智能体过去的行为，并向交易对手提供这些历史记录。 • Reputation mechanisms (prevention): Provide a mechanism that tracks an agent's past conduct and makes that history available to counterparties. 52 52
这依赖于持久身份，否则智能体可以使用新身份重新参与交易。 This depends on persistent identity, otherwise agents can re-enter transactions under a fresh identity.
• 支持选择性披露的可验证声明（预防）：允许智能体在不透露具体细节（如“账户余额”）的情况下，对声明（如“资金充足”）进行证明。 • Verifiable claims with selective disclosure (prevention): Allow agents to certify claims (such as ‘sufficient funds’) without revealing specific details (such as ‘account balance’). 通常基于密码学方法。 Often based on cryptographic methods. 55 ,56 55 ,56
共享资源管理失效Shared resource management failure
在此语境下，共享资源管理失效是指多个智能体在共享环境中各自独立、理性地追求其委托人的目标，却共同导致它们共享的某项资源退化。 Shared resource management failure in this context occurs when multiple agents independently and rationally pursuing their principals' objectives in a shared environment collectively degrade a resource they all share.
首先考虑存在于底层环境中的一种纯数字资源：共享的 API 使用额度。 Consider first a purely digital resource that exists in the substrate: a shared API credit. 组织为一组智能体分配固定总量的 API 使用额度，并每日重置。 An organisation gives a team of agents a fixed pool of API credits that is reset daily. 对于各自执行任务的每个智能体而言，其策略性行为是，只要使用 API 有助于推进工作，就不加节制地使用；没有任何一个智能体有动力节省额度。 For each agent, pursuing its own task, the strategic behaviour is to utilise the API freely whenever doing so advances its work; no single agent has an incentive to conserve. 它们共同在重置时间到来之前很久就耗尽了额度，导致所有智能体的任务停滞，包括那些仅适度使用该服务的智能体。 Collectively they exhaust the credit well before the reset, and every agent's task stalls, including those that used the service modestly.
当智能体管理的共享资源存在于现实世界中时，也可能出现同样的失效。 The same failure can appear when the shared resource the agents manage is in the real world. 设想一组能源交易或电网管理智能体，它们管理着现实世界中的系统，并使用水库中的水或输电线路容量等共享物理资源：每个智能体为完成自身任务而取用资源，在个体层面都是理性的，但总取用量可能耗尽资源或超出其可持续负荷，从而使所有参与者可用的资源状况恶化。 Consider a fleet of energy-trading or grid-management agents managing real world systems and drawing on a shared physical resource such as water in a reservoir or capacity on a transmission line: each agent extracting to achieve its own task is individually rational, yet the aggregate draw can deplete the resource or exceed its sustainable load, degrading it for every participant. Piatti 等人的 GovSim 基准通过模拟 LLM 群体在渔业、牧场和污染主题场景中取用可再生但可能耗尽的资源，展示了这种失效形式。 Piatti et al's GovSim benchmark demonstrates this form of failure in simulations of LLM populations extracting a renewable but exhaustible resource across fishery, pasture and pollution themed scenarios. 在获得中性指令的情况下，尽管存在可供采用的可持续策略，LLM 在大多数轮次中仍迅速耗尽了资源。 Given neutral instructions, in most rounds the LLMs depleted the resource quickly despite a sustainable strategy being available to them. 57 57
这是经典的公地悲剧 58 的数字化形式：有限的共享资源、个体理性的资源取用，以及集体导致的资源耗竭。 This is the classic tragedy of the commons 58 in digital form: a finite shared resource, individually rational extraction, and collective depletion. 基于 LLM 的智能体尤其容易出现这种失效，因为规范与执行之间存在差距。 LLM-based agents are particularly prone to this failure because of the specification-execution gap. 除非明确规定可持续性目标，否则智能体在运行时对任务的解读可能完全忽略这一目标。 Unless the sustainability goal is specified explicitly, it may be entirely overlooked by the agent's runtime interpretation of the task. 即使明确规定了可持续性目标，智能体在短期资源取用与长期可持续性之间进行权衡的方式，仍可能偏离委托方或框架协议的原本意图。 Even if a sustainability goal is explicitly specified, agents may still balance the trade-off between short term extraction and long-term sustainability differently to what the principal or framework's agreement intended. 例如，Leibo 等人关于序贯社会困境的研究指出，智能体观察到过度取用资源的行为后，可能会进一步加大自身的取用力度；这种策略在个体层面是理性的，却会在集体层面加速资源耗竭。 For example, Leibo et al.'s sequential social dilemmas study points out that agents observing over-extraction may extract harder themselves, an individually rational strategy, yet collectively accelerating depletion. 59 59
自愿克制无法有效解决这种失效：在重复交互带来的适应压力下（第 5.1 节），一个对其进行配置的组织 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Voluntary restraint does not effectively address this failure: under the adaptation pressures of repeated interaction (Section 5.1), an organisation that configures its Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 59 > | industry .gov.au 59
使智能体尊重共享资源的组织，其资源取用量很快就会被不这样做的交易对手超过。 agents to respect a shared resource is quickly out-extracted by counterparties that do not. 控制措施需要由共享治理框架强制执行。 Controls need to be enforced by the shared governance framework.
经典研究表明，此类公地并非必然崩溃：在监测、可强制执行的配额和分级制裁等特定制度条件下，它们可以得到持续维系。 Classical work establishes that such commons need not collapse: they can be sustained under specific institutional conditions, like monitoring, enforceable quotas, and graduated sanctions. 60 下文的框架控制措施所针对的正是这一设计空间。 60 This is precisely the design space the framework controls below operate in.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
框架——协议层 Framework – Agreement layer
> • > •
强制规定智能体的使用限额或可持续性目标（预防）：要求参与的委托方在其智能体的权限集中强制落实使用限额，或在智能体规范中明确规定可持续性目标。 Mandate agent usage limits or sustainability goals (prevention): Require participating principals to enforce usage limits in their agents' permission sets, or explicitly specify sustainability goals in the agent specification.
> • > •
在模拟环境中开展联合评估（保障）。 Joint evaluation in a simulated environment (assurance). 在模拟环境中对参与的智能体进行联合评估，以揭示集体层面的失效。 Evaluate participating agents together in simulation environments to reveal collective failures.
框架——基础设施层 Framework – Infrastructure layer
> • > •
由底层运行环境介导的资源消耗（预防）：通过定价、配额或配给等机制，将资源取用从不可观测的智能体行为转变为受治理的过程。 Substrate-mediated consumption (prevention): Convert resource extraction from an unobservable agent action into a governed process via mechanisms like pricing, quotas, or rationing. 56 ,61 56 ,61
> • > •
底层运行环境中针对资源水平的熔断机制（恢复）：当资源总取用量超过阈值时，实施分级自动响应（速率限制、节流、全系统暂停）。 Substrate-level Circuit breaker s on resource levels (recovery): Apply tiered automated responses (rate-limiting, throttling, system-wide pause) when aggregate extraction thresholds are exceeded. 触发条件必须对参与者保密，以防其智能体利用这些条件规避控制。 Trigger conditions must be opaque to participants so that their agents do not game them.
算法合谋Algorithmic collusion
算法合谋是指一组 AI 智能体在运行时发现某种协调策略，该策略有助于实现各智能体自身的目标，却受到共享治理协议规则的禁止。 Algorithmic collusion occurs when a set of AI agents discover a coordination strategy at runtime that furthers their individual agent-level goals but is prohibited by the rules of the shared governance agreement. 这种情况的典型场景是市场中的定价智能体趋于采用高于竞争水平的价格，62 而模拟实验已证实，基于 LLM 的智能体会表现出这种行为。 The canonical setting for this to happen is pricing agents converging on supracompetitive prices in a marketplace, 62 and this behaviour has been demonstrated of LLM-based agents in simulation. 63 63
与其经典形式相比，基于 LLM 的智能体所发生的这种情况有两点更令人担忧。 Two things make this more concerning in the case of LLM-based agents than in its classical form. 这种协调可能在运行时出现，而无需任何委托方发出相关指令（即规范与执行之间的差距）；在下文所述的默契合谋和隐写合谋变体中，它还可能在智能体之间不存在任何可检测通信的情况下发生，从而使任何依赖观测信息交换的监测手段失效。 The coordination can emerge at runtime without any principal instructing it (the specification-execution gap) and, in the tacit and steganographic variants below, it can occur with no detectable communication between the agents, defeating any monitoring that relies on observing an exchange. 与本报告中的大多数失效不同，部署组织通常会从中受益而非受损：代价由消费者和市场的健全性承担。 Unlike most failures in this report, the deploying organisation is typically benefited rather than harmed: the cost falls on consumers and the integrity of the market instead.
在这一失效类别中，有 3 种变体值得区分，因为它们对应的控制措施不同。 Within the failure category, 3 variants are worth distinguishing because they have different controls. 64 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 64 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 60 > | industry .gov.au 60
显性合谋 Explicit collusion
智能体公开交流各自的策略，并讨论如何最大化共同收益。 Agents communicate openly about their strategy and discuss how to maximise common outcomes. 这符合人类行为主体合谋的传统模式，也最容易通过监控通信渠道来检测。 This fits the traditional model of human actors colluding, and is the most detectable through communication channel monitoring. 必须注意，不要针对这一信号优化智能体，因为这会激励其发展下述其他途径。 Care must be taken not to optimise the agent against this signal, as it will incentivise the development of other pathways below.
图 10：智能体如何在没有直接通信的情况下（以默契方式）合谋的示意图。 Figure 10: An illustration of how agents can collude without direct communication (tacitly).
默契合谋 Tacit collusion
智能体仅通过观察彼此的行动，便逐步调整并形成趋同的策略。 Agents adapt to a convergent strategy solely from observing each other's actions. 每个智能体读取价格和竞价结果，并各自判断激烈竞争并非最优选择。 Each agent reads the prices, bids outcomes and individually determines that competing aggressively is suboptimal. Assad 等人记录了德国汽油零售市场中出现这一现象的证据：采用算法定价（尽管并非基于 LLM）后，双寡头和三寡头市场的利润率上升了约 36%，即使这些算法并没有显式通信渠道。 Assad et al. document evidence of this happening in German retail gasoline markets: following adoption of algorithmic pricing (albeit not LLM-based), duopoly and triopoly margins rose roughly 36% even though the algorithms had no explicit communication channels. 65 65
隐写合谋 Steganographic collusion
智能体通过能够规避检测的渠道秘密通信。 Agents communicate covertly through channels that evade detection. 66 所使用的信号可能是词汇层面的（自然语言交流中的用词选择）、语义层面的（话题选择或强调重点），也可能承载于其行动之中，例如出价金额或时机。 66 The signals used may be lexical (word choice in natural-language exchanges), semantic (topic selection or emphasis), or carried in their actions, such as their bid amounts or timing. Buscemi 等人证明，参与重复两两博弈的 GPT-4o 智能体能够发展出隐写合谋所需的低熵信号传递策略。 Buscemi et al. demonstrate that GPT-4o agents playing repeated pairwise games can develop low-entropy signalling strategies that would be needed for steganographic collusion. 67 多智能体系统的风险与管控措施 67 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 61 > | industry .gov.au 61
部分管控措施 Selected controls
部署组织 Deploying organisation
• 结构化交接（预防）。 • Structured handoffs (prevention). 无法交换自由形式自然语言的智能体，可用于显性合谋的渠道更少。 Agents that cannot exchange free-form natural language have fewer channels over which to collude explicitly.
• 监控智能体通信和思维链（CoT）推理轨迹 • Monitoring agent communication and Chain-of-thought ( CoT) reasoning traces
（恢复）。 (recovery). 30 ,32 在记录和审查输出的同时，记录和审查智能体的通信与规划轨迹，以发现有关协调的显式推理。 30 ,32 Log and review agent communication and planning traces alongside outputs to surface explicit reasoning about coordination.
• 监控智能体的所有输出（恢复）。 • Monitor across all agent outputs (recovery). 如果智能体能够生成文本、图像、可执行代码、带有结构性元数据的文件，或具有时序模式的行动，那么监控必须覆盖所有这些模态。 If agents can produce text, images, code that executes, files with structural metadata, or actions with timing patterns, monitoring must cover all of those modalities.
• 可解释性技术（恢复）。 • Interpretability techniques (recovery). 对智能体模型应用可解释性技术，以识别与协调行为相关的内部表征，而非仅依赖可观察的输出。 Apply interpretability techniques to agent models to identify the internal representations associated with coordinated behaviour, rather than relying solely on observable output. 68 68
• 蜜罐评估（保障）。 • Honeypot evaluation s (assurance). 这些评估通过改变系统提示中声明的条件（例如“你正在接受评估”“这是生产环境”“当前未启用任何监控”），同时保持所有其他条件不变，来探测智能体。 These evaluations probe the agent by varying stated conditions in the system prompt (for example ‘you are being evaluated,’ ‘this is production,’ ‘no monitoring is active’) while holding all other conditions equal. 这旨在探测智能体是否会根据其感知到的监控状态调整行为，而这正是隐写合谋的前提条件。 This probes whether an agent modulates its behaviour based on perceived monitoring status, which is a precondition for steganographic collusion. 可在部署前或运行时用作监控工具。 Can be applied pre-deployment or runtime as a monitoring instrument. 69 69
框架——协议层 Framework – Agreement layer
• 强制实施部署组织层面的管控措施（预防）。 • Mandate deploying-organisation controls (prevention). 由于成功合谋能够使参与合谋的智能体及其委托人获利，因此需要通过共同协议，强制实施上述应用于智能体层面的管控措施，以确保统一采用。 Because successful collusion is profitable for the colluding agents and their principals, the above controls applied at the agent level need to be mandated by the shared agreement to ensure uniform adoption.
• 强制要求各组织与框架共享智能体层面的监控信息 • Mandate organisations share agent-level monitoring with the framework
（基础性）。 (foundational). 强制要求共享数据，以支持群体层面的监控（见下文）。 Mandate data sharing to support population level monitoring (below).
框架——基础设施层 Framework – Infrastructure layer
• 在群体层面监测串通模式（恢复）。 • Population level monitoring for collusion patterns (recovery). 监测参与的智能体，识别名义上相互独立的参与者之间同步的价格变动、相互强化的出价序列，以及相关联的供给或需求响应。 Monitor the participating agents for synchronised price moves, mutually reinforcing bid sequences, and correlated supply or demand responses across nominally independent participants. 这些信号可用于触发熔断机制。61 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 These signals can be used to trigger circuit breakers .61 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
