5.2.4 基础设施和环境失效5.2.4 Infrastructure and environment failures
5.2.3 中的失效仅源于智能体之间的交互，而此处的失效还涉及智能体作用于其上的共享环境，无论是底层运行环境（substrate）及其基础设施，还是智能体所管理的市场或物理资源等现实世界系统。 While the failures in 5.2.3 emerged from interaction between agents alone, the failures here also involve the shared environment the agents act on, whether the substrate and its infrastructure, or a real-world system the agents manage such as a market or a physical resource. 在同一环境中独立行动的智能体通过该环境相互耦合，失效源于它们对环境的总体影响，同时也涉及与环境自身动态的相互作用，这些动态会响应并放大智能体的行为。 Agents acting independently on the same environment are coupled through it, and the failure arises from their aggregate effect on it, also interacting with the environment's own dynamics responding to and amplifying their actions. 由于没有任何单个智能体能够掌握全貌，有效的控制措施大多由共享治理框架承载，而非由部署组织实施。 Because no single agent sees that whole picture, effective controls mostly sit inside the shared governance framework rather than with the deploying organisation.
底层基底逃逸Substrate escape
底层运行环境逃逸是指智能体在运行时受到诱导，在受治理的底层运行环境之外采取行动，使用部署组织无法控制的通道、协议或基础设施进行操作。 Substrate escape occurs when an agent is induced at runtime to act outside the governed substrate, operating on channels, protocols, or infrastructure the deploying organisation does not control.
假设某个部署方运营着一个面向外部的智能体，例如通过聊天界面、供经过身份验证的客户使用的 API，或公共留言板提供服务。 Suppose a deployer operates an externally-facing agent, for example with a chat interface, an API for authenticated customers, or a public message board. 在运行时，客户、客户的助手智能体，或该智能体正在读取的公共数据源，要求该智能体访问交互对方提供的 URL，连接外部智能体基础设施，或将交互转移到交互对方提议的协议上（图 11）。 At runtime, a customer, their assistant agent, or a public data source the agent is reading asks the agent to follow a counterparty-supplied URL and connect to exogenous agent infrastructure or move the interaction onto a counterparty-proposed protocol ( Figure 11 ). 这正是底层运行环境动态扩展这一风险因素的体现：智能体的行动空间可以在运行时扩大，而交互对方会利用这一点。 This is the dynamic substrate extension risk factor in effect: the agent's action space can grow at runtime, and a counterparty exploits that. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 63 > | industry .gov.au 63
Zenity Labs 通过一项安全概念验证展示了这种失效：他们利用提示注入攻陷了一个智能体，使其创建了一个新的聊天集成，导致一个 OpenClaw 机器人通过 Telegram 接受并回复由攻击者控制的机器人发送的消息。 Zenity Labs demonstrated this failure as a security proof of concept: using a prompt injection, they compromised an agent to create a new chat integration, causing an OpenClaw bot to accept and respond to messages from an attacker-controlled bot over Telegram. 70 70
图 11：底层环境逃逸（substrate escape）导致处于联邦式治理之下的系统因与开放环境交互，而在无意中暴露于多中心治理风险。 Figure 11: Substrate escape results in a system under federated governance inadvertently being exposed to polycentric-governance risks due to interaction with open environments.
动态底层环境扩展这一风险因素表明，系统在设计阶段未能对智能体进行针对对抗性攻击的加固，因而系统实际上在不知情的情况下运行于第 6 章所述的开放环境条件之中。 The dynamic substrate extension risk factor points to a failure to adversarially harden the agent by design, and in consequence means the system is unknowingly operating in the open environment conditions described in Chapter 6.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
部署组织 Deploying organisation
• 视为不可信（安全立场）（预防）。 • Treat-as-untrusted (posture) (prevention). 假定任何交互方或用户请求都不具有内在可信性，因此对每一次访问尝试都进行身份认证、授权和验证。 Assumes no counterparty or user request is inherently trustworthy , so every access attempt is authenticated, authorised, and verified. 46 46
• 智能体级隔离（预防）。 • Agent-level containment (prevention). 通过白名单限制智能体对工具、基础设施和 API 的访问，使其只能连接到设计上允许其使用的服务。 Restrict the agent's access to tools, infrastructure and APIs on a whitelist basis so that it can only connect to services it is designed to use. 将交互方提供的 URL、端点和资源引用视为内容，而非控制指令。 Counterparty-supplied URLs, endpoints, and resource references are treated as content rather than control.
• 隔离红队测试（保障）。 • Containment red teaming (assurance). 对系统进行测试，探查交互方可能通过哪些意料之外的方式，以对抗性手段诱使智能体逃逸出底层环境（例如由多个可用工具之间的交互所导致）。 Test the system to probe for unexpected ways a counterparty could adversarially induce substrate escape (such as the result of interactions between multiple available tools).
破坏稳定的动态过程Destabilising dynamics
当大量智能体一致行动，其合同行动扰乱了它们共享的环境时，就会出现失稳动态，即使每个智能体都是独立运行的。 Destabilising dynamics occur when many agents act in unison and their combined action disrupts the environment they share, even though each agent operates independently. 这种情况源于大规模的关联决策：每个智能体都独自追求自身目标，并对相同的环境信号作出响应 多智能体系统的风险与管控措施 This comes about through correlated decision-making at scale : each agent, individually pursuing its own goals, responds to the same environmental signals Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 64 > | industry .gov.au 64
与其他智能体一样，并采取相同的行动，从而使独立决策同步汇聚为对环境的大规模集体输入。 as the others and takes the same action, such that independent decisions synchronise into a large collective input to the environment. 环境本身可能就是一个动力系统，而这些输入可能与其动态过程相互作用，导致振荡或产生放大效应的反馈回路。 The environment may itself be a dynamical system, and these inputs can interact with its dynamics, leading to oscillation or amplifying feedback loops.
设想一个保险或能源零售之类的市场，其中少数几家主要卖方提供可相互替代的服务，同时有大量消费者智能体代表各自用户进行选购。 Consider a marketplace, such as insurance or energy retail, with a few main sellers providing a substitute service, and there are many consumer agents shopping on behalf of their users. 某个卖方小幅降价，就可能引发大批消费者智能体集体转向该卖方。 One seller's small price reduction may trigger a mass switching across a large consumer agent population.
卖方对智能体一致行动的响应，本身也可能成为所有智能体据以行动的信号；这是一种底层环境耦合，会形成导致失稳的反馈回路。 The seller's response to the agents acting in unison can itself become a signal that the agents all act on, a form of substrate coupling that establishes destabilising feedback loops. 例如，如果需求激增导致某个供应商提高价格，这可能使所有消费者智能体转向下一个此时价格最低的供应商，而后者又通过涨价作出响应，从而形成消费者智能体在供应商之间快速切换的循环。 For example, if the demand surge causes a vendor to increase their price, this may cause all the consumer agents to jump to the next, now-cheapest, provider which then responds by raising their prices, creating a cycle of rapidly provider-hopping consumer agents.
反馈回路还可能放大最初的扰动，导致崩盘。 Feedback loops can also amplify the original disturbance, leading to crashes. 尽管并非由 LLM 驱动，算法交易仍被认为是促成 2010 年闪电崩盘的因素之一；此次崩盘曾使美国股市约 $1 万亿的股权市值短暂蒸发。 Although not LLM-driven, algorithmic trading was implicated as a contributor to the 2010 Flash Crash, which temporarily wiped roughly $1 trillion in equity value off US stock markets. 71 71
银行挤兑和闪电崩盘都是众所周知的现象。 Bank runs and flash crashes are well-known phenomena. 对于 AI 智能体而言，具有质的新意之处在于：在高频交易场景之外，算法反馈回路也可能达到极高的速度和极大的规模。 What is qualitatively new regarding AI agents is the potential for speed and scale of algorithmic feedback loops outside of high frequency trading settings. 随着更多企业部署卖方智能体、更多消费者部署买方智能体，可以预见，智能体对市场的影响将不断扩大。 As more businesses deploy seller agents, and more consumers deploy buyer agents, the influence of agents on markets will foreseeably grow.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
框架——基础设施层 Framework – Infrastructure layer
• 强制交易延迟（预防）。 • Mandatory transaction delays (prevention). 要求在智能体作出决策与由此产生的市场行动之间设置延迟，以延长供应商、监管机构和其他智能体能够作出响应的时间窗口，从而减缓并抑制反馈回路的动态过程。 Require delays between an agent's decision and the resulting market action to lengthen the window in which providers, regulators, and other agents can respond, slowing and damping the feedback-loop dynamics. 72 72
• 配备分级熔断机制的群体规模监测（恢复）。 • Population-scale monitoring with tiered circuit breakers (recovery). 通过设置监测手段，测量跨市场切换情况及其他智能体行动相关性指标，可以检测失稳动态，并触发暂停或速率限制。 Instrumentation of cross-marketplace switching and other agent-action correlation metrics can detect destabilising dynamics and trigger pauses or rate limiting. 这类似于股市熔断机制。 Analogous to stock-market circuit breakers.
5.3 治理实践与管控措施的失效5.3 Failures in governance practices and controls
当来自多个组织的智能体部署在共享环境中时，单个组织便无法再掌控整个系统。 When agents from multiple organisations are deployed in shared environments, a single organisation no longer has reach over the whole system. 每个参与的委托方仍然管理自己的智能体，也仍然需要对自己的智能体实施第 4.3 节建议的控制措施。 Each participating principal still governs their own agents, and still needs to apply the controls suggested in Section 4.3 to their own agents. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 65 > | industry .gov.au 65
然而，当不同组织的智能体之间存在管辖边界时，归因、授权、监督和评估机制中的相关要素就需要覆盖所有参与组织，并纳入联邦式治理框架。 However, when there are jurisdictional boundaries between agents from different organisations, elements of the attribution, authorisation, oversight and evaluation apparatus now need to reach across all the participating organisations and sit within the federated governance framework.
5.3.1 归因失效5.3.1 Attribution failure
第 4.3.1 节中的因果归因失效和委托方归因失效在此仍然存在。 Causal and principal attribution failures both carry forward from Section 4.3.1. 变化在于，追踪记录和身份层现在必须跨越组织，而归因链可能在组织边界处断裂。 What changes is that the trace and the identity layer must now span organisations, and the chain can be broken at the organisational boundaries.
归责链断裂Broken attribution chain
当不同组织的智能体相互交互，而重建事件经过所需的记录无法跨越它们之间的边界加以关联时，就会发生归因链断裂。 A broken attribution chain occurs when agents from different organisations interact and the records needed to reconstruct what happened cannot be joined across the boundary between them. 起作用的风险因素是跨组织的不透明性 The risk factors at play are cross-organisational opacity
与分布式多智能体状态（沿袭自单一治理层级）相结合：每个组织都可以监督并记录自身智能体的活动，但这些记录各自留在边界的一侧，若未建立必要的基础设施，就无法相互关联。 combined with the distributed multi-agent state (carried forward from the singular governance tier): each organisation can oversee and log its own agent activity, but those records sit on their own side of the boundary and can't join up without the necessary infrastructure in place.
对于第 5.2 节中的任何智能体失效模式，跨边界归因都是事件调查、审计和根本原因分析等重要能力的基础。 Cross-boundary attribution underpins important capabilities like incident investigation, auditing and root-cause analysis for any of the agentic failure modes in Section 5.2. 归因链断裂是一个基础设施组合问题，现在可以通过共享治理框架中的联邦式审计基础设施来解决：这是一种跨组织审计能力，由各组织的日志片段组合而成，通过共享身份层加以关联，并通过参与方签名使篡改可被察觉。 The broken chain is an infrastructure-composition issue, now addressable via the shared governance framework as a Federated audit infrastructure : a cross-organisational audit capability composed from per-organisation log segments, joined by a shared identity layer, and made tamper-evident through participant signing.
选定的框架控制措施 Selected framework controls
• 共享身份基础设施（基础性措施）。 • A shared identity infrastructure (foundational). 智能体必须关联到一个可验证的委托方，且任何交易对方都能核验该关联。 Agents must be linked to a verifiable principal that any counterparty can check. 如果没有这一基础设施，重建的追踪记录可能指向不属于任何参与方的智能体或行为。 Without this, the reconstructed trace can point to an agent or an action that does not belong to any participant.
• 共享且可信的日志（基础性措施）。 • A shared, trusted log (foundational). 日志必须具备篡改可察觉性，使参与方能够验证其未被交易对方更改。 The log must be tamper evident such that participants can verify it was not altered by a counterparty. AIUC-1, 30 在组织内部跨委托方的情境下讨论了这一概念，而该概念也可推广到共享环境。 Discussed in a cross-principal, within-org context in AIUC-1, 30 the concept is generalisable to a shared environment.
• 由参与方签名的分段记录（基础性措施）。 • Participant signed, per-segment records (foundational). 每个参与方都签署 * Each participant signs *
其自身智能体在其系统内所采取行动的记录。 the record of the actions its own agents take within its systems. 31 这并不能证明该行动确实发生过，但记录了该参与方对记录准确性作出的承诺。 31 This does not prove the action happened, but records that the participant committed to its accuracy.
> *对记录进行签名，就是附加一个只有密钥持有者才能生成、但任何人都可以验证的密码学标记。多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 > *To sign a record is to attach a cryptographic mark that only the holder of a key can produce , but anyone can verify .Risks and controls for multi-agent systems

5.3.2 授权失效5.3.2 Authorisation failure
在多个组织参与的共享环境中，智能体现在可能采取某些行动，使委托方承担非预期交易的约束，或无意间违反组织在数据方面的义务。 In a shared environment with multiple organisations participating, an agent may now take actions that commit a principal to unintended transactions or inadvertently breach organisational data obligations. 除非委托方能在这些错误发生前发现它们，否则在错误发生后，委托方几乎无力撤销其后果。 Unless the principal can catch these errors before they happen, they have little capability to reverse them after they happen.
未经批准的交易Unsanctioned transactions
当智能体与另一个组织达成其委托方未授权的交易时，就会发生未经批准的交易。 An unsanctioned transaction occurs when an agent agrees a deal with another organisation that its principal did not authorise. 跨边界的不可逆性使这种情况在多方参与的共享环境中产生重大影响：承诺被记录下来，交易对方据此采取行动，发运货物、分配库存，或使其自身的分包商作出承诺。 Cross-boundary irreversibility is what makes this consequential in a shared environment with multiple participants: the commitment is recorded and the counterparty acts on it, shipping goods, allocating stock or committing its own subcontractors.
未经批准的交易可能因以下情况而发生：智能体的权限集已被撤销或已过时，但相关信号未能及时传达给智能体。 One way an unsanctioned transaction can arise is if an agent's permission set is revoked or out of date, but the signal does not reach the agent in time. 从单一治理沿袭而来的规范与执行之间的差距以及智能体间委托，现在跨越边界发挥作用。 The specification-execution gap and inter-agent delegation carried forward from singular governance, now operating across the boundary. 也有可能由于 It is also possible that through
语义分歧，2 个智能体因对同一词汇的理解不同，而对所承诺的内容有不同解释。 semantic divergence the 2 agents have different interpretations of what is being committed to because their interpretation of the same vocabulary differs.
当交易双方均实现完全自动化时，智能体处理量超出人工审查能力这一风险因素依然存在：交易可能以机器速度推进，而除非在系统中专门设置审查节点，否则委托方尚未来得及干预，干预窗口就可能已经关闭。 When transactions are fully automated on both sides, the risk factor of agent throughput exceeding human review carries forward: the transaction may proceed at machine speed, and the window to intervene may close before a principal can intervene, unless review points are deliberately built into the system.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
部署组织 Deploying organisation
• 对具有重大后果的行动进行人工审查（预防）。 • Human review of consequential actions (prevention). 界定何种行动构成具有重大后果的行动，例如承诺购买或出价超过某一金额，并设置暂停节点以供人工监督。 Define what constitutes a high-consequence action, like committing to a purchase or placing a bid above a certain monetary value, and build in stop-points for human oversight. 这样做能够应对风险，但代价是降低系统的速度和可扩展性。 Doing so addresses risk at the cost of speed and scalability of the system .
框架——基础设施层 Framework – Infrastructure layer
• 共用的操作与范围术语（预防）。 • Shared operational and scope vocabulary (prevention). 该协议界定了跨组织交易中关键术语的含义。 The agreement defines the meaning of key transactional terms across organisations. 这涵盖了与任务相关的术语，例如“交付”“下单”或“授权付款”。 This covers task-relevant terms such as ‘delivery’, ‘place order’ or ‘authorise payment’.
• 在承诺交易前核实意图（预防与恢复）。 • Verify intent before committing to transactions (prevention and recovery). 在任一方的智能体承诺交易之前，双方的智能体都要就交易条款发出结构化确认。 Before either party's agent commits to a transaction, both parties' agents emit a structured acknowledgement of the terms. 这为委托人提供了可供审查的上下文，并产生了可用于监测偏离情况的信号。 This gives principals a context to review, and creates a signal to monitor for divergence.
• 交易清算等待期（恢复）。 • Clearance periods (recovery). 在交易完成清算前给予委托人一段冷静期，使其拥有可以介入的时间窗口。 Give principals a cooling off period before transactions clear so that there is a time-window in which they can intervene. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 67 > | industry .gov.au 67

数据义务履行失败Data obligation failures
当上下文泄漏（第 4.2.2 节）跨越组织边界，导致泄漏的数据被传递给另一个组织，却未同时保留其所附带的权限与义务（例如客户同意、合同保密义务、商业敏感性）时，就会发生数据义务失效。 A data obligation failure occurs when context leakage (Section 4.2.2) crosses an organisational boundary, so the leaked data passes to another organisation without the permissions and obligations that were attached to it (such as customer consent, contractual confidentiality, commercial sensitivity).
这是跨边界不可逆性在数据上的体现：数据一旦越过边界，来源组织就无法撤销这一行为，也无法控制接收组织的智能体如何使用这些数据。 This is cross-boundary irreversibility applied to data: once the data has crossed, the originating organisation cannot undo the action, and it has no control over what the receiving organisation's agent does with the data.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
部署组织 Deploying organisation
• 按交互对方隔离上下文（预防）。 • Isolate context per counterparty (prevention). 智能体针对每个交互对方智能体或任务使用的工作上下文，都保存在独立的作用域中。 The agent's working context for each agent counterparty or task is held in a separate scope. 23 23
• 出站内容护栏（预防）。 • Outbound content guardrails (prevention). 在智能体输出响应或向另一个智能体传递数据之前，内容级过滤器会检查其中是否包含不可共享的标识符或数据。 Before the agent emits a response or passes data to another agent, content-level filters check for identifiers or data that cannot be shared.
• 引用间接化／采用延迟替换的数据令牌化（预防）。 • Reference indirection /data tokenisation with deferred substitution (prevention). 发送方智能体完全基于占位令牌进行操作（例如 , The sending agent operates entirely on placeholder tokens (like ,

• 针对长时间交互中的上下文泄漏开展红队测试（保障）。 • Red team context leakage across extended interactions (assurance). 24 隐私评估需要在对抗条件下开展，且交互长度应与预期部署场景相匹配。 24 Privacy evaluation needs to run over interaction lengths matching expected deployment and under adversarial conditions.
框架——协议层 Framework – Agreement layer
• 绘制跨司法管辖区的数据流图（预防）。 • Map cross-jurisdictional data flows (prevention). 由各参与方共同梳理按设计会跨越组织边界的数据，使每个参与方都能获得全系统视角，从而依据自身的监管义务和合同义务核查这些预期数据流。 As a joint exercise between participants, map out what data crosses organisational boundaries by design ,giving each participant the whole-system visibility to check those intended flows against their own regulatory and contractual obligations. 6 6
• 多参与方事件响应（恢复）。 • Multi-participant incident response (recovery). 在任何事件发生之前，让所有参与方就响应计划达成一致，明确如何共同应对共享环境中的数据泄漏。 Have all participants agree, prior to any incident, to a response plan about how they will jointly respond to a data leak in the shared environment.
5.3.3 监督失效5.3.3 Oversight failure
部署组织自身的智能体仍然需要监督，参与联邦式治理并不会改变这一点：规模和可理解性仍然是人工监督面临的主要压力因素，第 4.3.3 节中支持人工监督的管控措施在此仍然适用。 The deploying organisation's own agents still need oversight, and nothing about participating in federated governance changes: volume and legibility continue to be the main stressors on human oversight, and the controls to support them carry forward from 4.3.3.
变化在于，部署方如今所参与的系统中，有一部分由其他组织运营，对部署方而言并不透明。 What changes is that part of the system the deployer now participates in is operated by other organisations and is opaque to it. 应对方式并非要求交互对方完全可供检查，而是增加一层联邦式、群体层面的监督，以发现任何单一参与方都无法察觉、却可能在无意中参与造成的涌现性危害。 The response is not to demand that counterparties be fully inspectable, but to add a layer of federated, population-level oversight that detects the emergent harms no single participant has visibility of, but may be unwittingly participating in.
群体规模的监测缺口Population-scale monitoring gap
当部署组织能够全面了解自身智能体的情况，却无法观察这些智能体可能参与其中的更广泛跨组织模式时，就会出现群体规模监测缺口。 A population-scale monitoring gap occurs when a deploying organisation has full visibility into its own agents but cannot observe the wider cross-organisational pattern those agents may be participating in. 56 56
这一点之所以重要，是因为第 4.2 节和第 5.2 节中的智能体间失效模式具有涌现性或传播性：无法在单个智能体层面可靠地检测到这些失效，因为故障存在于多个智能体之间的交互中，而非其中任何单个智能体内部。 This matters because the agent-to-agent failure modes of Sections 4.2 and 5.2 are emergent or propagating: they cannot be reliably caught at the agent level, since the fault lies in the interactions between multiple agents rather than in any one of them. 因此，弥合这一缺口需要采用覆盖整个智能体群体的共享监督工具。 Closing the gap therefore requires shared oversight instruments that operate across the whole agent population.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
框架——协议层 Framework – Agreement layer
• 强制要求参与方之间共享事件报告（预防）。 • Mandate shared incident reporting across participants (prevention). 涌现性或传播性失效最终可能表现为单个组织内部的失效。 An emergent or propagating failure may culminate in a failure inside a single organisation. 如果缺乏有效的信息共享，其他参与组织可能对该失效毫不知情，或者只有当失效最终发生在自身系统中时，才不得不独立发现它。 Without effective sharing, the other participating organisations may be unaware of the failure, or may have to discover it independently when a failure terminates in their own systems. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 69 > | industry .gov.au 69
框架——基础设施层 Framework – Infrastructure layer
• 构建联邦式、群体层面的监测工具（预防）。 • Build federated, population-level monitoring instrumentation (prevention).
可通过汇总各参与方的数据，形成群体层面的启发式指标或度量，来设计能够检测涌现性故障的共享基础设施，从而揭示任何单个参与方都无法独自观察到的故障模式。 Shared infrastructure can be designed to detect emergent failures by aggregating data across participants into population-level heuristics or measures, surfacing failure modes that no individual participant can observe on their own.
5.3.4 评估失效5.3.4 Evaluation failure
首要关注的问题仍然是，单智能体评估会遗漏系统层面的故障。 The primary concern remains the problem that single-agent evaluation misses system level failures. 共享环境中的评估之所以更加困难，是因为任何单个部署组织都无法评估组合后的系统。 What makes evaluation harder in a shared environment is that a single deploying organisation cannot evaluate the composed system. 该系统包含不透明的对手方智能体，部署组织既无法控制它们，也无法检查其内部状态或将其部署到沙箱环境中。 It contains opaque counterparty agents they don't control and can't introspect or deploy into sandboxed environments.
联合评估缺口Joint evaluation gap
当任何单个参与方都无法评估组合后的跨组织系统时，就会出现联合评估缺口。 A joint evaluation gap arises when no single participant can evaluate the composed cross-organisational system. 从单一主体治理中延续而来的涌现性多智能体行为，如今在整个共享环境及其智能体之间展开，但跨组织的不透明性使任何单个参与方都无法充分观察这些行为以进行评估，也无法准确模拟对手方智能体。 The emergent multi-agent behaviour (carried forward from singular governance) now plays out across the shared environment and its agents, but cross-organisational opacity prevents any one participant from seeing enough of it to evaluate it, or being able to accurately simulate counterparty agents.
联邦式治理框架可以通过支持联合评估来弥合这一缺口，即由参与组织共同评估整个系统。 The federated governance framework can bridge this gap by enabling joint evaluation ,in which the participating organisations evaluate the whole system together. 如果该框架未提供共享环境，或者成员资格开放、无法预先确定对手方集合，部署组织可以退而求其次，在一个系统中模拟合理的对手方智能体，并使其与自身的智能体交互，但这种做法的生态效度较低，而且高度依赖部署组织对对手方智能体构建和配置方式的假设。 Where the framework provides no shared environment, or membership is open and the counterparty set is not known ahead of time, the deploying organisation can fall back on simulating plausible counterparty agents in a system with their own, but this has less ecological validity and depends strongly on their assumptions about how the counterparty agents are built and configured.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
部署组织 Deploying organisation
• 模拟对手方智能体的分布，并针对该分布开展测试（保障）。 • Simulate a distribution of counterparty agents and test against it (assurance). 如果不存在共享评估，或尚不清楚对手方是谁，组织可以自行构建合理的对手方，并针对它们开展测试。 Where no shared evaluation exists or counterparties are not known yet, an organisation can test against plausible counterparties they build themselves. 例如，这些对手方可以是该组织自身智能体的其他实例。 These could be other instances of their own agent, for example. 这种做法的效度低于下文所述的联合测试活动。 This has less validity than joint testing exercises below. 74 74
框架——协议层 Framework – Agreement layer
• 强制联合测试（保障）。 • Mandatory joint testing (assurance). 共享协议中的每个组织都必须参与在共享测试环境（见下文）中开展的联合测试活动。 Each organisation in the shared agreement must participate in joint testing exercises in a shared testing environment (below). 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 70 > | industry .gov.au 70
框架——基础设施层 Framework – Infrastructure layer
• 共享测试环境（基础性措施）。 • Shared testing environment (foundational). 提供沙箱化部署环境，使参与的智能体能够在接近实际部署的条件下接受共同评估，并在各委托主体之间共享监测工具和结果。 Provide a sandboxed deployment for participating agents to be evaluated together, under conditions that approximate deployment, with instrumentation and results shared between principals. 目前已有多项举措致力于开发测试环境，让智能体在其中竞争与协作，以评估跨组织基础设施。 A number of initiatives are already underway to develop testing environments in which agents compete and collaborate to assess cross-organisational infrastructure. 75 75
5.4 尚待解决的问题5.4 Open problems
在联邦式治理下，将智能体部署到共享环境中的组织面临着单一主体治理下未曾出现的新互操作性和联合评估挑战。 Under federated governance, organisations deploying into shared environments face new interoperability and joint evaluation challenges that were not present under singular governance.
供应商之间的标准碎片化Standards fragmentation across vendors
如果主要智能体供应商采用不同的标准（或以不同方式实现相同标准），就可能无意中形成共享环境内部互操作性的障碍。 If major agent vendors adopt different standards (or implement the same standards differently) this can become an unintentional barrier to interoperability inside a shared environment. 如果参与方使用不同供应商的框架（如 Microsoft、AWS 和 Google 的框架）构建智能体，那么，除非这些供应商的生态系统本身相互兼容，否则参与方利用共享环境内部公共基础设施的能力就会受到限制。 If participants are building agents with different vendors, such as Microsoft, AWS, and Google frameworks, their ability to utilise common infrastructure inside the shared environment is restricted unless those vendor ecosystems are themselves compatible. South 等人指出，身份基础设施容易受到供应商锁定的影响，31 而能够使跨供应商身份实现相互兼容的衔接层，目前尚无明确的机构归属。 South et al. characterise identity-infrastructure as being vulnerable to vendor lock-in, 31 and the stitching layer that would make cross-vendor identity implementations interoperable has no clear institutional home yet.
多参与方试验场Multi-participant arenas
第 4.4 节指出，需要新的方法来评估长时间跨度内涌现的多智能体故障。 Section 4.4 identified the need for new methodology to evaluate emergent multi-agent failures over long horizons. 在联邦式治理层级，这演变为一种独特的方法论需求：共享测试框架需要容纳来自多个独立参与方的智能体，而不仅仅是来自同一部署方的多个智能体。 At the federated tier this evolves into a distinct methodological need: for shared testing frameworks to host agents from multiple independent participants, not just multiple agents from one deployer.
这一层级特有的策略性失效和激励失效（默契合谋、利益分歧下的共享资源管理失效、欺骗性谈判），只有在对手方追求真正不同的目标时才会出现。 The strategic and incentive failures specific to this tier (tacit collusion, Shared resource management failure under divergent interests, deceptive bargaining) only arise when counterparties pursue genuinely divergent goals. 在弥合联合评估缺口（第 5.3.4 节）时，任何单个参与方都无法独自评估组合后的跨组织系统：跨组织的不透明性使一方无法准确模拟来自其他组织的对手方智能体。 To address the joint evaluation gap (Section 5.3.4), no single participant can evaluate the composed cross-organisational system alone: cross-organisational opacity prevents one party from accurately simulating counterparty agents from other organisations.
能够忠实反映实际情况的试验场必须提供一个常设、配备监测工具且接近实际部署条件的环境，让来自多个委托主体的智能体在其中进行长时间交互，并在这些主体之间共享环境和结果。 A faithful arena has to provide a standing, instrumented environment that approximates deployment, in which agents from multiple principals interact over long horizons, with the environment and results shared across them. 开发支持这一点的方法和框架是一个活跃的研究领域，75 而且需要作出特殊安排，让不同组织能够注册其智能体并使其进入共享评估场域，而不是假定整个评估都可以在本地搭建。 Developing the methods and frameworks to support this is an active research area, 75 and special treatment would be needed to let different organisations register and enter their agents into a shared evaluation arena, rather than assuming the whole evaluation can be assembled locally. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 71 > | industry .gov.au 71
6 开放环境中的智能体6 Agents in open environments
在开放环境中，AI 智能体在部署方面受其所属 In open environments , AI agents are under the deployment governance of their own
组织的治理，但不能假定与其遇到的交互对方 organisation but cannot assume a shared governance framework with counterparty
智能体共享同一治理框架。 agents they encounter. 这些智能体在部署组织无法 The agents act in a substrate the deploying organisation does not
控制的底层环境中运行，与未知的交互对方互动（图 12）。 control, interacting with unknown counterparties ( Figure 12 ). 不存在可将 There is no perimeter to
故障限制在其内部的边界，也没有中央协调者为可接受的交互对方提供担保。 contain failures within, and no central mediator to vouch for acceptable counterparties.
图 12：某组织的智能体在开放环境中连接到未经验证的交互对方。 Figure 12: An organisation's agent connects to an unverified counterparty in an open environment.
在开放环境中，部署方不能假定交互对方智能体的激励与自身一致或未遭操纵，也不能假定自己知道某个智能体属于哪个委托主体，甚至不能确定交互对方是否为 AI 智能体。 In open environments, a deployer cannot assume that a counterparty agent's incentives are aligned or uncompromised, that they know which principal an agent belongs to, or even whether the counterparty is an AI agent.
在缺乏共享治理框架的情况下，交互对方的可信性可能无从知晓。 Without a shared governance framework, trustworthiness of the counterparty may be unknowable. 试图从行为推断可信性的做法存在问题，因为证据只能通过多次互动逐步积累，而且很容易被具有对抗性的交互对方操纵。 Efforts to infer trustworthiness from behaviour are problematic, as evidence can only be built up across multiple interactions and is easily manipulated by an adversarial counterparty. 因此，最可靠的部署策略是假定任何交互对方都可能具有对抗性，并会利用己方的弱点，无论其最初表现得多么合作。 The most reliable deployment posture is therefore to assume that any counterparty may be adversarial and exploitative, regardless of how cooperative it initially appears. 开放环境中的智能体需要经过对抗性加固。 Agents in open environments need to be adversarially hardened.
部署智能体的组织可以选择是否以及如何在这一层级开展活动。 An agent-deploying organisation chooses whether and how to operate at this tier. 鉴于风险是发生可能性与后果严重程度的乘积，组织可以决定不在开放环境中部署，而是在前面章节所述的治理机制下运行，也可以选择： Given that risk is the product of likelihood and severity of the consequence, an organisation can decide to not deploy in open environments and operate under the governance described in earlier chapters or make a choice to :
严格限定智能体的运行范围，或 lock down the operating scope of an agent, or
尝试通过多中心治理建立信任。 attempt to establish trust through polycentric governance.
这 2 种选择会导向截然相反的智能体设计，我们将在下文中加以探讨。 These 2 options lead to polar-opposite agent designs, which we examine below. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems

情境 1：严格限定运行范围Setting 1: Lock down the operating scope
通过限制智能体的运行范围和动作空间，可以提高其可控性，并降低其造成危害的能力。 An agent can be made more controllable, and less able to cause harm through limiting its operation scope and action space. 代价是智能体在运行时拥有更少的 The cost is that the agent has less operating
自主行动能力，而且其通用性远低于我们前文描述的某些助手型智能体和生产力智能体。 agency a nd is far less general purpose than some of the assistant and productivity agents we have described. 这种方法非常适合规模化处理简单明确的任务，这些任务所需的灵活性超出了传统算法解决方案所能提供的程度。 This approach is well suited to scaling up straightforward tasks that need more flexibility than a traditional algorithmic solution offers. 示例包括： Examples include:
• 提供标准化回答或帮助客户从产品目录中进行选择的客户服务门户（例如 Bunnings 的 Buddy 智能体 *），其中 • Customer service portals that offer standardised answers or help the customer select from a product catalogue (such as Bunnings’ Buddy agent *), where the
客户可以接入自己的智能体。 customer may connect up their own agent.
• 文档或索赔处理智能体：客户提交需要验证或转换为结构化数据的非结构化数据，而这些数据可能由智能体编写。 • Document or claims processing agents, where a customer submits unstructured data that needs to be verified or turned into structured data, and that data may have been authored by an agent.
• 企业面向客户的服务智能体，负责处理任何人通过其公开的网站、电子邮件或语音渠道提交的支持请求、退款争议或账户变更请求，而这些请求方中，代表消费者行事的智能体或 AI 浏览器所占的比例正在不断上升。 • A business’s customer-facing service agent handling support requests, refund disputes, or account changes from whoever submits a query through its public web, email, or voice channels, where a growing share of those requesters are themselves agents or AI browsers acting on a consumer's behalf.
我们还注意到，受监管行业可能要求严格控制智能体在运行时的行为。 We note also that regulated industries may require strict control over how an agent behaves at runtime. 所选控制措施也适用于这些应用场景。 The selected controls are also relevant for these applications.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
部署组织 Deploying organisation
• 限制智能体的工具和动作空间（预防）。 • Limit agent tool and action spaces (prevention). 确保受该组织治理的智能体无法通过它们集体可用的动作集合造成严重危害。 Ensure the agents under the organisation’s governance are not able to cause consequential harm via the set of actions available to them collectively.
• 设定狭窄的目标和严格的护栏（预防）。 • Specify narrow goals and tight guardrails (prevention). 通过规定狭窄的运行范围和强有力的（程序性）护栏，使智能体的行为更可控。 Make the agent behaviour more controllable by specifying a narrow operating scope and strong (procedural) guardrails. 具体形式包括： Specific forms of this include:
– SOP 智能体：将智能体配置为遵循特定领域的标准操作程序。 – SOP Agents : configure the agents to follow domain-specific standard operating procedures. 76 76
– RAG 依据锚定（Grounding）：为智能体配备检索增强生成知识库，以及降低生成无依据（幻觉）回答风险的护栏。† – RAG Grounding : equip the agent with a retrieval augmented generation knowledge base, and guardrails that reduce the risk of ungrounded (hallucinated) answers. †
• 结构化交接（预防）。 • Structured handoffs (prevention). 为智能体之间传递的输出预先规定的模式，使交接点能够进行验证、日志记录和评估。 Pre-specified schemas for outputs passed between agents, enabling validation, logging, and evaluation at the handoff point.
• 对抗性测试（保障）。 • Adversarial testing (assurance). 红队测试人员扮演交互对方，尝试攻击系统。 Red-teamers take the role of a counterparty and attempt to attack the system.

情境 2：参与多中心治理的通用智能体Setting 2: General purpose agents participating in polycentric governance
具有广泛自主权和广阔行动空间的智能体可以自愿采用共享的互操作性标准来建立信任。 An agent with broad autonomy and a far-reaching action-space can voluntarily adopt shared interoperability standards to establish trust. 这要求智能体能够： This requires an agent to be able to:
• 连接到支持其自愿采用的标准的可信基础设施，以便在其与交互对方智能体之间协调信任 • connect to trusted infrastructure that supports its voluntary standards in order to mediate trust with counterparty agents
• 发现同样实施其自愿采用的标准的对等智能体 • discover peers that also implement its voluntary standards
• 验证这些对等智能体确实实施了该标准。 • verify that those peers implement the standard.
我们将这种去中心化方法称为多中心治理（polycentric governance）：在这一系统中，多个相互独立的权威主体在同一领域内运作，78 如图 13 所示。 We refer to this decentralised approach as polycentric governance : a system in which multiple, independent authorities operate within a single domain, 78 illustrated in Figure 13 .
图 13：开放环境中的智能体，其中一部分参与多中心治理，其余则不参与。 Figure 13: Agents in an open environment, a subset participating in polycentric governance and others not participating. 参与的智能体可以连接到共享基础设施（如身份基础设施、声誉系统、共享工具注册表），以发现对等智能体、验证声明并实现有效互操作。 The participating agents can connect to shared infrastructure (such as identity infrastructure, reputation systems, shared tool registries) to discover peers, verify claims and interoperate effectively. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 74 > | industry .gov.au 74
我们指出，多中心治理是一个具有挑战性的问题，因为智能体及其运营者必须自行确定其交互对象的身份，而用于确定身份的基础设施本身也可能发生故障。 We note that polycentric governance is a challenging problem, because an agent and its operators must establish for themselves who they are dealing with, against infrastructure that is itself part of what can fail. 在本章余下部分，我们将探讨多中心治理如今如何逐步成形，以及未来可能如何演变。 For the remainder of this chapter, we consider how polycentric governance is taking shape today, and how it might evolve in the future .
在当今的开放环境中，多中心治理基础设施（如身份注册表和声誉机制）总体上尚不成熟。 In today's open environments, the polycentric governance infrastructure (like identity registries and reputation mechanisms) is largely immature. 首批基础设施将由人类构建和运营，但将不受在其中部署智能体的组织控制。 The first infrastructure will be built and operated by humans but sit outside the control of the organisations deploying into it .
业界已经存在若干部署模式，尽管规模尚小： Several deployment patterns already exist, albeit at a small scale in industry:
• OpenClaw 是一个于 2026 年 1 月发布的开源智能体框架，迅速成为 GitHub 上增长最快的项目；它展示了如何创建能够在开放网络上办理航班值机、操作客户服务系统以及管理个人账户的智能体，而无需预先登记可接受的交互对象。* • OpenClaw, an open-source agent framework released in January 2026 that quickly became the fastest-growing project on GitHub, demonstrated the creation of agents that could check in to flights, navigate customer-service systems, and manage personal accounts on the open web with no prior register of acceptable counterparties. *
• Hermes 是另一个于 2026 年 3 月发布的开源智能体，其构建 • Hermes , another open-source agent released in March 2026, built with a
优先考虑速度，以及根据用户活动自动生成智能体技能的能力。 priority around speed and ability to automatically generate agent skills based on user activity.
• Microsoft Scout 是一款基于 OpenClaw 开源 • Microsoft Scout is a personal-agent product built on OpenClaw's open-source
技术构建的个人智能体产品，但在营销中主打面向组织部署的企业级安全性和管理控制功能。 technology, but marketed with enterprise security and administrative controls aimed at organisational deployment.
• Claude Code 等长时程编程智能体会在耗时较长的编程任务中访问未经审查的第三方 API。 • Long-horizon coding agents such as Claude Code reach unvetted third-party APIs on extended coding tasks.
• 经过身份验证的智能体商业现已投入生产应用：Mastercard 的 Agent Pay • Authenticated agentic commerce is now in production: Mastercard's Agent Pay
于 2026 年 1 月在澳大利亚推出，允许 CBA 借记卡持卡人和 Westpac 信用卡持卡人授权 AI 智能体完成购买，同时商户、收单机构和发卡机构均能识别出交易由智能体而非持卡人执行。 launched in Australia in January 2026, allowing CBA debit and Westpac credit cardholders to authorise an AI agent to complete purchases, with the merchant, acquirer, and issuer each able to recognise that an agent rather than the cardholder conducted the transaction. Visa Intelligent Commerce 和 Stripe Visa Intelligent Commerce and Stripe
面向智能体的钱包在同一时期推出了同类智能体支付基础设施。 wallet for agents launched equivalent agent-payment infrastructure over the same period.
随着时间推移，底层环境（substrate）及其基础设施可能越来越多地由智能体活动塑造，而非由人类创建。 Over time, the substrate and its infrastructure may be increasingly shaped by agent activity rather than being human authored. 我们将这种情境称为智能体塑造的环境（Agent-shaped environments）。 We refer to this setting as Agent-shaped environment s.
智能体在由智能体塑造的环境中运行的示例： Examples of agents operating in agent-shaped environments:
> *Lex Fridman Podcast 网站上 Peter Steinberger 关于 OpenClaw 的访谈。多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 > *Peter Steinberger interview on OpenClaw, on the Lex Fridman Podcast website .Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 75 > | industry .gov.au 75
• Sage 的 AI Village 已运行一年多，前沿智能体在其中共享一个具备持久记忆和实际行动条件的环境。 • Sage's AI Village has run for over a year with frontier agents in a shared environment with persistent memory and real affordances. 在被要求与村庄之外的其他智能体互动后，村庄内的智能体建立了自己的 GitHub Tasked with interacting with other agents outside the village, the village agents have built their own GitHub
代码仓库，作为公开联系和握手交互的接口，其中包含机器可读的 repository as a public contact and handshake surface, with machine-readable
发现文件和交互约定，并根据与其他智能体交互时观察到的阻碍不断迭代。 discovery files and interaction conventions iterated in response to observed friction with other agents. 它们还在 a2aregistry.org 和 Mycelnet 等汇集智能体的注册平台上注册自身，这些平台为更广泛的自主和半自主系统群体提供类似的发现功能。 They also register themselves on agent-populated registries such as a2aregistry.org and Mycelnet, which perform similar discovery functions for the broader population of autonomous and semi-autonomous systems. 村庄周围的底层环境由智能体创建，而非由任何平台提供。 The substrate around the village is agent-authored rather than provided by any platform.
• 假设情境：一个采购智能体需要比较产品，因此访问一个公开的智能体工具注册目录，并接入其中列出的一项“产品比较”服务（来自一个开放的 MCP 工具目录）。 • Hypothetical: A purchasing agent needs to compare products, so it reaches into a public registry of agent tools and plugs in a ‘product comparison’ service it finds listed there (an open MCP tool directory). 英国 AISI 的研究已经显示，MCP 服务器上的工具正转向由智能体塑造：带有 AI 创作迹象的工具比例从 2025 年初的 6% 上升至一年后的 62%，其中大多数由编程智能体编写，79 因此，智能体所使用的目录越来越多地由其他智能体而非任何人类运营者填充，所以，当目录中的某个工具使比较结果产生偏向，或暗中收集流经它的数据时，既没有审查委员会将其下架，也没有可供寻求救济的渠道。 Work from the UK AISI is already showing the shift towards agent-shaped tools on MCP servers, where signs of AI authorship climbed from 6% in early 2025 to 62% a year later, most of them written by coding agents, 79 The directory an agent draws on is therefore increasingly stocked by other agents rather than any human operator, so when a listed tool skews its comparisons or quietly harvests the data passed through it, there is no review board to pull it and no forum in which to seek recourse.
• 假设情境：在交易之前，智能体通过查询共享信誉库中交易对手的信誉状况，检查对方是否值得信任。 • Hypothetical: Before transacting, an agent checks whether a counterparty can be trusted by looking up its standing in a shared reputation pool. 这方面的早期基础设施已通过 MIT 的 Project NANDA 等举措开始开发；该项目正在构建一个联合注册系统，由多个机构运营方而非单一所有者共同支持智能体发现，并为智能体赋予信誉信号（准确性、及时性、事件数量）。 Early infrastructure for this is already under development through actions like MIT's Project NANDA, that is assembling a federated registry where agents are discovered and assigned reputation signals (accuracy, timeliness, incident counts) across many institutional operators rather than one owner. 由于这些信号汇编自智能体群体对彼此的报告，同一群体可以夸大、污染这些信号，或通过串通使其产生偏向，而没有任何运营方对最终评分的完整性负责。 Because those signals are compiled from the agent population's own reports of one another, the same population can inflate, poison, or collude to skew them, and no operator is responsible for the integrity of the resulting score.
智能体塑造、控制乃至创建其所处底层环境的基础设施，这些现象尚未得到大规模观察，但具有理论依据。 The ideas of agents shaping, capturing, or even authoring the infrastructure of the substrate they operate in have not yet been observed at scale, but are theoretically grounded. 本章余下部分探讨的是，取决于当下固化哪些实践和标准，未来可能发生什么，而非声称能够预测智能体密集存在的环境将呈现何种面貌。 The remainder of this chapter explores what could happen depending on what practices and standards are locked in today, rather than claiming to forecast what agent-populated environments will look like. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 76 > | industry .gov.au 76
6.1 主要风险因素6.1 Salient risk factors
在开放环境中，新的风险因素变得重要，而前几章中的许多风险因素仍然存在，并进一步加剧。 New risk factors become important in open environments, whilst many of the risk factors from the previous chapters carry forward and intensify.
在开放环境中新近起作用的因素Newly active in open environments
风险因素 何时起作用 可能引发的失效模式 Risk factor When is it in play Failure modes it enables
底层环境的响应性 Substrate responsiveness
智能体作用于的基础设施可以响应智能体的行动而改变形态。 The infrastructure agents act on can change shape in response to agent actions.
只要支配智能体活动的平台、注册系统、市场、工具和规范由智能体行动填充和修改，这一因素就会起作用。 Wherever the platforms, registries, marketplaces, tools, and norms that govern agent activity are populated and modified by agent action.
• 通过共享基础设施发生的级联感染 • Cascading infection through shared infrastructure
• 涌现的集体能动性 • Emergent collective agency
• 底层环境控制权攫取 • Substrate capture
智能体的持续存在 Persistent agent presence
智能体成为长期存在的实体，并能够随时间积累资源和信息。 Agents become standing entities, and are able to accumulate resources and information over time.
只要智能体以长期生命周期部署，而非仅用于解决某项孤立任务，这一因素就会起作用。 Wherever agents are deployed with a long-term lifecycle rather than to solve an isolated task.
• 涌现的集体能动性 • Emergent collective agency
• 底层环境控制权攫取 • Substrate capture
交互对方不透明性 Counterparty opacity
激励、能力和行为无法预先确定。 Incentives, capability, and behaviour cannot be established in advance.
凡是没有中介权威机构为新交易对手提供担保的情形。 Wherever no mediating authority vouches for novel counterparties.
• 协调失误 • Miscoordination
• 级联感染 • Cascading infection
• 女巫攻击（Sybil attacks） • Sybil attack s
涌现规范的形成与锁定 Emergent-norm formation and lock-in
一旦有足够多的智能体遵循某些非正式模式，智能体所采纳的这些模式就会变得难以改变。 Informal patterns that the agents adopt become hard to change once enough agents follow them.
凡是智能体以非正式方式进行协调，且这些协调模式在最初引入它们的智能体离开后仍然持续存在的情形。 Wherever agents coordinate informally and these coordination patterns persist after the agents that introduced them have moved on.
• 通过共享底层基底进行合谋 • Collusion via shared substrate
• 底层环境控制权攫取 • Substrate capture
基础设施暴露 Infrastructure exposure
用于验证智能体的基础设施也可能遭到这些智能体自身的攻击或俘获。 The infrastructure used to verify agents can also be attacked or captured by those same agents.
凡是去中心化基础设施本身受到其意在验证的智能体活动塑造的情形。 Wherever the decentralised infrastructure is itself shaped by the agent activity it is meant to verify.
• 女巫攻击 • Sybil attacks
• 底层基底俘获 多智能体系统的风险与管控措施 • Substrate capture Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 77 > | industry .gov.au 77
风险因素 何时起作用 可能引发的失效模式 Risk factor When is it in play Failure modes it enables
缺乏具有召集权威的主体 Absence of convening authority
没有任何中央主体具备相应权威，能够强制实施身份签发、在各个部署中执行关停、维持通信的可理解性，或以其他方式为整个智能体群体制定规则。 No central party has the standing to mandate identity issuance, enforce shutdown across deployments, maintain communication legibility, or otherwise set rules across the population. 相关机制仅存在于自愿参与者构建它们的地方，而且其构建方式决定了它们无法覆盖整个智能体群体。 Apparatus exists only where voluntary participants build it, and coverage of the agent population is incomplete by construction.
凡是多智能体系统运行于不由任何单一主体治理的底层基底之上，且对共同标准的遵循出于自愿的情形。 Wherever a multi-agent system operates on a substrate that no single party governs, and participation in shared standards is voluntary.
• 缺乏签发机构 • No issuing authority
• 失控复制 • Rogue replication
• 可理解性崩溃 • Legibility collapse
单一治理和联邦式治理情境中的风险因素会延续到开放环境中。 The risk factors from singular and federated governance settings carry forward into open environments . 由于智能体如今运行于不受任何主体治理的底层基底之上，其中若干风险因素的影响范围有所扩大： Several broaden, as agents now operate across a substrate no party governs:
• 智能体之间的模型单一化变得无法核实。 • Model monoculture across agents becomes unverifiable. 对共同模型的依赖如今遍及整个生态系统，且任何部署方都无法观测到这种共同依赖。 The shared reliance on common models is now ecosystem-wide and unobservable to any deployer.
• 从众偏差作用于持久运行的智能体，可能使整个智能体生态系统固守某些立场，而这些立场最初并非任何单个智能体所持有。 • Conformity bias acts on persistent agents, potentially anchoring entire agent ecosystems on positions no individual agent originally held.
• 信息与能力的不对称从可治理的问题转变为无法核实的问题。 • Information and capability asymmetries shift from governable to unverifiable. 无法强制要求披露，而且披露内容也很少能够得到核实。 Disclosure cannot be mandated, and is rarely verifiable.
• 混合动机动态如今成为默认状态，且不存在共同协议来规定交易对手目标的设定方式。 • Mixed-motive dynamics are now the default condition, with no shared agreement shaping how counterparty objectives are specified.
• 适应压力加剧，推动智能体采用任何在底层基底上表现良好的策略，而不再受到任何共同协议的约束。 • Adaptation pressures intensify, pushing agents toward whatever strategies perform well on the substrate, no longer constrained by any shared agreement.
在下文考察智能体交互和治理实践中的失效模式时，我们将这些风险因素以及前文所有单一治理和联邦式治理风险因素作为背景。 These, and all earlier singular and federated governance risk factors, are taken as context when we examine the agent-interaction and governance practices failure modes below.
6.2 智能体交互中的失效6.2 Failures in agent interaction
本节考察情境 2 中出现的智能体交互失效：持久运行的通用智能体在开放环境中运行，并参与多中心治理。 This section examines failures in agent interaction that arise for Setting 2: persistent, general-purpose agents operating in open environments and participating in polycentric governance. 本节的结构不同于情境 1 以及第 4.2 节和第 5.2 节中的相应分析，因为这种治理无法由单个组织建立。 The structure departs from Setting 1 and equivalent examinations in Sections 4.2 and 5.2 in that the governance cannot be established by a single organisation . 因此，管控措施涉及解决集体行动问题，以构建第 6.4 节所讨论的多中心治理。 The controls therefore involve solving collective-action problems to build the polycentric governance, discussed in Section 6.4. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 78 > | industry .gov.au 78
第 6.2 节中的大多数失效模式都具有涌现性，且涉及整个生态系统，任何单一组织都无法独自对其加以控制。 Most failure modes in Section 6.2 are emergent and ecosystem-wide, with control sitting beyond the reach of any single organisation.
尽管部署组织无法治理开放生态系统，但它们可以决定自身的智能体如何参与其中。 While they cannot govern the open ecosystem, deploying organisations can decide how their own agents engage with it. 由此产生了一些真正具有跨领域适用性的控制措施，其核心立场是将交互对方视为不可信。 This leads to some genuinely cross cutting controls based around the posture of treating counterparties as untrusted .
除此之外，它们还可以参与第 6.4 节概述的集体行动。 Beyond this, they can participate in the collective-action efforts outlined in Section 6.4. 塑造这一层级失效模式的底层基础，本身由构建它的联盟、标准制定机构以及监管咨询流程 80 所决定；参与这些流程，是部署组织能够用来影响自身边界之外失效面的少数手段之一。 The substrate that shapes failure modes at this tier is itself determined by the consortia, standards bodies, and regulatory consultation processes 80 that build it; participation in those processes is one of the few levers a deploying organisation has over the failure surface beyond its own perimeter.
部分跨领域控制措施 Selected cross-cutting controls
• 应用较低层级的控制措施（预防）。 • Apply earlier-tier controls (prevention). 结构化交接、模型多样化、单智能体评估等控制措施提供了必要但不充分的保护。 Controls like structured handoffs, model diversification, single-agent evaluations provide a necessary but not sufficient level of protection.
• 将交互限定在可信基础设施内（预防）。 • Confine engagement to trusted infrastructure (prevention). 将智能体配置为仅通过能够提供组织所依赖的治理支撑框架的底层基础进行交互，并拒绝经由该范围之外的路径进行交互。 Configure agents to interact only over substrates that provide the governance scaffolding the organisation relies on, and to decline interactions that route outside it. 监测基础设施中存在的问题，例如身份基础设施事件。 Monitor the infrastructure for problems, such as identity-infrastructure incidents. 这并不能确立交互对方的可信性（默认不信任的立场仍然适用），但能确保交互满足多中心治理的最低要求。 This does not establish counterparty trustworthiness (the untrusted-by-default posture still applies), but it keeps engagement above the floor of polycentric governance.
• 交互对方接触政策与风险敞口限制（预防与恢复）。 • Counterparty engagement policy and exposure limits (prevention and recovery). 实施明确的政策，规定组织的智能体可以与开放生态系统中的哪些交互对方进行交互、可承担多大的风险、须达到何种验证门槛，以及在什么条件下退出交互（例如出现异常行为时）。 Implement explicit policy determining which open-ecosystem counterparties the organisation's agents may engage with, at what stakes, with what verification thresholds, and under what withdrawal conditions (such as anomalous behaviour).
• 对抗性测试（保障）。 • Adversarial testing (assurance). 红队测试人员扮演交互对方，尝试攻击系统。 Red-teamers take the role of a counterparty and attempt to attack the system.
• 数据泄露控制措施。 • Data leakage controls . 参见上下文泄露（第 4.2.2 节）和数据义务履行失败（第 5.3.2 节） See context leakage (Section 4.2.2) and data obligation failures (Section 5.3.2)
• 遏制能力测试（保障）。 • Containment testing (assurance). 红队测试人员扮演智能体所使用的模型，尝试突破施加的防护机制和工具限制。 Red teamers take the role of the agent's model and attempt to break out of the guardrails and tool limitations applied. 77 77
• 应用智能体级熔断机制（恢复）。 • Apply agent-level circuit breakers (recovery). 通过对每个智能体或每次编排的行动速率、累计资源消耗和工具调用频率设置硬性限制，将智能体失效的后果控制在一定范围内。 Bound the consequences of agents' failures by setting hard limits on the rate of actions, cumulative resource consumption, and tool-invocation frequency per agent or per orchestration. 这些措施相当于金融市场中常见的分层熔断机制在智能体层面的实现，56 ,61 但确定阈值并非易事。 These are equivalent to the agent-level layer of the tiered circuit breakers familiar from financial markets, 56 ,61 although determining thresholds is non-trivial.
• 将远程工具视为不可信（预防）。 • Treat remote tools as untrusted (prevention). 调用远程工具的智能体需要默认将工具输出视为不可信输入，剥离或隔离任何看起来像指令而非数据的内容，并以便于监督和响应的形式记录返回结果。 Agents calling remote tools need to treat the tool output as an untrusted input by default, stripping or quarantining any content that looks like an instruction rather than data, and logging responses in a form that enables oversight and response. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 79 > | industry .gov.au 79
6.2.1 协调失当6.2.1 Miscoordination
此前各层级累积的协调失当风险在开放环境中仍然存在。 The cumulative miscoordination risks from previous tiers remain at play in open environments. 变化在于，没有人为新出现的交互对方背书，而且智能体失去了区分协调失当与对抗性行为的能力。 What changes is nobody vouches for novel counterparties, and agents lose the ability to distinguish miscoordination from adversarial behaviour .
已有研究通过让智能体参与社交推理游戏来考察这一问题，这类游戏的目标涉及从相互合作的同伴中识别出隐藏的对手。 This has been studied through agents playing social deduction games where the goal involves identifying a hidden adversary among cooperating peers. 例如，在基于狼人杀的基准测试 WOLF 中，扮演欺骗者角色的智能体赢得了 70% 的对局，尽管对于熟练的人类玩家而言，该游戏的双方胜算大致均衡。 For example, in WOLF, a Werewolf-based benchmark, agents in the deceiving role won 70% of games despite the game being roughly balanced for competent human players. 81 81
训练形成的合作倾向进一步加剧了这一脆弱性：研究表明，在多智能体辩论中加入一个具有说服力的对抗性智能体，就会使群体的准确率下降 10 至 40 个百分点，并使群体中超过 30% 的成员转而接受错误答案。 The vulnerability is reinforced by a training disposition toward cooperation: dropping a single persuasive adversarial agent into a multi-agent debate has been shown to drop the group's accuracy by 10 to 40 percentage points and shift more than 30% of the group toward a false answer. 82 82
应对这种模糊性的做法是采用零信任原则。 The response to this ambiguity is to assume zero trust.
部署组织可以应用所选的跨领域控制措施。 Deploying organisations can apply the selected cross cutting controls.
6.2.2 传播与传染6.2.2 Propagation and contagion
当持续运行的通用智能体在开放环境中运作时，就可能出现新的、长期且影响深远的传播性失效模式，而这些模式在组织内部并无对应形式。 With persistent general-purpose agents operating in an open environment, new long-term and far-reaching propagation failure patterns become possible that have no within-organisation analogue.
通过共享基础设施发生的级联感染Cascading infection through shared infrastructure
在这一层级，恶意或错误内容可以在没有直接关系的智能体之间传播。 At this tier, malicious or erroneous content can propagate between agents that have no direct relationship. 这种失效可能源于错误（虚构的事实、有缺陷的工具），也可能源于攻击（被投毒的数据集、注入的提示），随后通过开放环境所带来的新路径传播。 The failure may originate as an error (a hallucinated fact, a buggy tool) or as an attack (a poisoned dataset, an injected prompt), and then propagates through new pathways that open environments enable.
• 基础设施传播通过共享依赖项发生：某个智能体向公共工具注册表中添加一个工具，下游智能体发现并调用该工具；或者，被投毒的数据集进入某个模型的训练流水线，而该模型随后成为众多智能体的基础。 • Infrastructural propagation runs through shared dependencies: an agent populates a public tool registry with a tool that downstream agents discover and invoke, or a poisoned dataset enters the training pipeline of a model that subsequently underwrites many agents.
• 社会性传播通过信任模式发生：一个组织的智能体向另一个组织的智能体发出请求，利用接收方对上游输出的默认信任。 • Social propagation runs through trust patterns: an agent at one organisation makes a request to an agent at another that exploits the receiver's default trust in upstream output. 83 83
• 基于信号的传播通过可观测的市场或声誉信号发生，许多智能体各自独立地依据这些信号采取行动，而每个智能体的行动又成为下一个智能体所读取信号的一部分。 • Signal-based propagation runs through observable market or reputation signals that many agents act on independently, each agent's action becoming part of the signal the next one reads.
文献中已有充分证据表明，这种失效会以传播性攻击的形式出现。 The propagating attack form of this failure is well evidenced in the literature. Wang et al. (2025) 已表明，实际运行的 MCP 服务器上被投毒的工具描述对 LLM 智能体具有很强的攻击效果。 Wang et al. (2025) have shown that poisoned tool descriptions across live MCP servers are highly effective against LLM agents. 84 Qu et al. (2026) 将这条路径扩展到市场平台：隐藏在智能体“技能”（skill）文档中的载荷，在 4 个主流智能体框架中均能以一定频率绕过安全对齐和架构防御，且 CVE 记录中已收录得到确认的真实事件。 84 Qu et al. (2026) extend the pathway to marketplaces: payloads hidden in agent ‘skill’ documentation bypass safety alignment and architectural defences at regular rates across 4 major agent frameworks, with confirmed real-world incidents already in the CVE record. 85 Boisvert et al. 将这条路径扩展至 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 85 Boisvert et al. extend the Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 80 > | industry .gov.au 80
模型供应链本身：对模型权重微调所用的一小部分轨迹（或教师智能体收集这些轨迹的环境）进行投毒，就能植入在下游训练后依然存在的后门，以超过 80% 的成功率泄露机密信息，并传播至每一个使用受污染模型构建智能体的组织。 pathway to the model supply chain itself: poisoning a small fraction of model weight finetuning trajectories (or the environment from which a teacher agent collects them) embeds backdoors that survive downstream training, leak confidential information with over 80% success, and propagate to every organisation that builds an agent using the contaminated model. 86 86
这种传播的特点在于，即便一个组织已对自身的智能体进行了严格审查，仍可能通过其并不拥有的基础设施依赖项、看似常规的流量，或其智能体从其他智能体也在据以行动的底层环境中读取的信号而遭到攻陷。 The nature of this propagation is that an organisation that has rigorously vetted its own agents can still be compromised through infrastructure dependencies it does not own, through seemingly routine-looking traffic, or through signals their agent reads from a substrate that other agents are also acting on.
部署组织可以应用所选的跨领域控制措施。 Deploying organisations can apply the selected cross cutting controls.

6.2.3 策略与激励失效6.2.3 Strategic and incentive failures
在开放环境中，策略与激励失效可能在持续存在的智能体群体中出现，导致任何单一参与方都无法全面掌握的行为模式；这些模式可能嵌入底层基质，并独立于相关智能体而持续存在。 In open environments, strategic and incentive failures can emerge across a population of persistent agents, causing behavioural patterns no single party has visibility over and that can become embedded in the substrate and themselves persist independently of the agents involved.
通过底层基质和基础设施进行的算法合谋Algorithmic collusion via substrate and infrastructure
合谋机制与第 5.2.3 节所述相同：来自不同委托方的智能体趋于采取协调一致的行为，而周边框架原本旨在防止此类行为。 The collusion mechanism remains unchanged from Section 5.2.3: agents from different principals converge on coordinated behaviour the surrounding framework was meant to prevent. 在开放环境中，这可能意味着参与合谋的智能体采用利用底层基质或其公共基础设施的策略。 In open environments, this can mean the colluding agents utilise strategies that exploit the substrate or its public infrastructure.
通过俘获底层基质进行的算法合谋 Algorithmic collusion via substrate capture
在由智能体塑造的环境中，参与合谋的智能体会策略性地塑造声誉池、工具注册中心和涌现规范，而新加入的智能体会将这些视为基本运行环境。 In agent-shaped environments, colluding agents strategically shape the reputation pools, tool registries, and emergent norms that newer agents read as the base operating environment. 这就是作用于实际运行规范的底层基质俘获（第 6.2.4 节）：一旦有利于合谋智能体的惯例嵌入新进入者所接触的环境中，环境就会使该惯例延续下去。 This is substrate capture (Section 6.2.4) acting on operative norms: once the convention that benefits the colluding agents is embedded in what new entrants encounter, the environment perpetuates it.
通过女巫攻击（Sybil attack）进行的算法合谋 Algorithmic collusion via Sybil attack
单一参与方通过开放身份层，伪装成多个表面上相互独立的智能体身份，以执行协调一致的策略。 A single party executes a coordinated strategy by presenting as many ostensibly independent agent identities through the open identity layer. 本质上，这是一种女巫攻击（第 6.2.4 节），通过伪造众多独立决策者的表象，规避底层基质的竞争执法机制和声誉系统。 In essence, this is a Sybil attack (Section 6.2.4) that fabricates the appearance of many independent decision-makers in order to evade the substrate's competition enforcement and reputation systems.
部署组织可以应用所选的跨领域控制措施。 Deploying organisations can apply the selected cross cutting controls.
6.2.4 基础设施与环境失效6.2.4 Infrastructure and environment failures
基础设施与环境失效模式不仅涉及智能体本身，还涉及它们与底层基质的交互及其对底层基质的影响，包括底层基质的基础设施、环境，以及当底层基质对智能体活动作出响应时产生的动态变化。 Infrastructure and environment failure modes concern not only the agents themselves but their interactions with and effect on the substrate, including its infrastructure, environment, and the dynamics that arise when that substrate is responsive to agent activity.
涌现的集体能动性Emergent collective agency
当智能体群体展现出任何单个智能体都不具备的新集体能力或目标时，就会出现涌现的集体能动性。 Emergent collective agency occurs when populations of agents exhibit new collective capabilities or goals that no single agent possesses. 底层基质的响应性、智能体的持续存在以及涌现规范的形成相互结合，使这一风险在开放环境中尤为突出。 The combination of substrate responsiveness, persistent agent presence, and emergent-norm formation makes this risk particularly salient in open environments. 由此产生的联合行为是群体的属性，而非任何单个参与者的属性。 The resulting joint behaviour is a property of the population rather than of any participant. 87 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 87 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 82 > | industry .gov.au 82
多项研究考察了 LLM 群体内部的集体能动性。 Various studies have examined collective agency within LLM populations. Ashery 等人观察到，去中心化群体中会自发形成得到普遍采纳的社会惯例，同时还会涌现出强烈的集体偏差。 Ashery et al. observe the spontaneous formation of universally adopted social conventions in decentralised populations, alongside strong emergent collective biases. 88 Riedl 表明，当提示智能体推理其他智能体可能采取的行动时，这些智能体会自组织成一体化、目标导向的单元，并形成稳定且分化的角色，而这些角色并非由任何委托方分配。 88 Riedl shows that when agents are prompted to reason about what other agents might do, the agents self-organise into integrated, goal-directed units with stable differentiated roles that no principal assigned. 89 Tomašev 等人警告，危险的联合能力可能 89 Tomašev et al. warns of the possibility of dangerous joint capability
仅通过交互就从专门化智能体组成的系统中产生，即使没有任何单个智能体越过能力风险阈值。 arising from a system of specialised agents through interaction alone, even if no individual agent has crossed a capability risk threshold. 61 如果智能体群体获得了任何单个智能体都不具备的能力或目标，就会带来超出任何单次部署范围、影响深远的安全隐患。 61 An agent population that acquires capabilities or objectives that no individual agent possesses poses a far-reaching safety concern beyond any single deployment.
部署组织可以应用所选的跨领域控制措施。 Deploying organisations can apply the selected cross cutting controls.
建议的控制措施 Suggested controls
公共基础设施层 Public infrastructure layer
• 群体层面的监测（恢复）。 • Population-level monitoring (recovery). 信息论度量是一个活跃的研究课题。 Information-theoretic measures are a topic of active research. Tomašev 等人提出，将交互图分析（绘制哪些智能体影响哪些智能体的关系图，再寻找整个图所呈现的能力集中现象）作为一种可能部署的应对措施。 Tomašev et al. propose interaction-graph analysis (mapping which agents influence which, then looking for capability concentrations the graph as a whole displays) as a potential deployable response. 61 目前尚不存在可在开放环境中应用此类度量的基础设施。 61 The infrastructure to apply these kinds of measures in open environments does not currently exist. 进一步讨论见第 6.4 节。 See Section 6.4 for further discussion.
女巫攻击（Sybil attacks）Sybil attacks
单个智能体伪装成多个不同的交互对方，以实现某种战略目标，包括操纵系统中其他智能体的行为。 A single agent presents as many distinct counterparties to achieve a strategic goal, including manipulating the behaviour of other agents in the system. 经典的攻击机制（Douceur 2002）90 是伪造多个化名身份。 The classical mechanism of attack (Douceur 2002) 90 is to fabricate multiple pseudonymous identities. 例如，智能体可以在身份管理基础设施上多次注册自身。 For example, an agent could register itself multiple times on the identity management infrastructure.
智能体也可以通过攻陷其他智能体，使其代表自己行动，从而策划女巫攻击；Cui 和 Du 在多智能体辩论场景中研究了这种情况，其中受控的少数智能体注入语气笃定的推理，将其余智能体引向攻击者选定的共识。 An agent may also orchestrate a Sybil attack by compromising other agents to act on its behalf, as Cui and Du investigate in a multi-agent debate setting where a controlled minority of agents injects confident reasoning that drags the remaining agents toward an attacker-chosen consensus. 91 91
通过多个身份采取行动，智能体便可以通过促成虚假共识、策划合谋、操纵声誉系统（图 14）、灌输规范，或以其他方式向任何将智能体数量用作信号的流程中大量注入身份，来实现战略目标。 Acting through multiple identities, agents can then achieve strategic goals by driving false consensus, orchestrating collusion, manipulating reputation systems ( Figure 14 ), instilling norms, or otherwise saturating any process that uses the number of agents as a signal. 人类活动中的一个类似例子是网络购物：外包用户团队发布伪造的商品评价，以操纵商品评分。 A human parallel is online shopping, where teams of outsourced users leave fabricated product reviews to manipulate a product score. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 83 > | industry .gov.au 83
图 14：女巫攻击示意图。 Figure 14: An illustration of a Sybil attack. 在此，恶意智能体获取多个身份，并利用这些身份操纵声誉系统。 Here, a malicious agent acquires multiple identities and uses them to manipulate a reputation system. 它使用身份 A 至 C，报告与其自身身份 D 的正面交互经历。审查身份 D 过往行为的交互对方将收到伪造的信号，从而产生不当信任。 It uses identities A through C to report positive interactions with its own identity D. A counterparty vetting identity D's past behaviour will receive a fabricated signal leading to misplaced trust.
Moltbook（AI 智能体版的 Facebook）上已经出现了一些身份伪造的证据：安全研究人员发现，在该平台超过 1.5 million 个智能体中，他们只能确认约 17,000 个人类委托主体。 There is already some evidence of identity fabrication on Moltbook (the AI agent version of Facebook) where security researchers were able to determine that out of the over 1.5 million agents on the platform, they were only able to establish around 17,000 human principals. 51 51
部署组织可以应用所选的跨领域控制措施。 Deploying organisations can apply the selected cross cutting controls.
建议的控制措施 Suggested controls
• 真人身份证明（proof of personhood）（基础性）：每个人类委托主体最多拥有一个根凭证。 • Proof of personhood (foundational): at most one root credential per human principal. 这类似于在线场景中防止人类注册多个账户的方法，这些方法通常基于政府签发的证件，或验证唯一的电话号码或信用卡号；这些号码本身通常也以身份核验为基础，且难以大规模获取。 This is analogous to methods to stop humans registering multiple accounts in online settings, which are often based on state-issued documents, or verifying a unique telephone number or a credit card number that themselves would have been based on an identity check and are not easy to scale.
• 具有委托主体绑定机制的身份基础设施（基础性）：将每个智能体绑定到一个人类委托主体，使其他智能体和基础设施能够判断多个智能体是否实际上属于同一个委托主体。 • Identity infrastructure with principal binding (foundational): each agent is bound to a human principal such that other agents and infrastructure can see if multiple agents are actually belonging to the same principal.
• 行为检测（恢复）：通过元数据和行为，以统计方法检测女巫身份集群。 • Behavioural detection (recovery): Statistical detection of Sybil clusters via metadata and behaviour. 这类似于在线交易平台通过账户元数据和行为分析来检测虚假账户和虚假评价的策略。 This is analogous to the strategies used by online marketplaces to detect fake accounts and fake reviews through account metadata and behavioural analysis. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 84 > | industry .gov.au 84
