底层基底俘获Substrate capture
一部分智能体对其运行所在的底层平台积累了不成比例的影响力，而这种影响力又进一步巩固了它们的优势。 A subset of agents accumulates disproportionate influence over the substrate they operate on and that influence further ingrains their advantage. 由此形成持续存在且自我强化的反馈循环，巩固它们的地位并阻碍纠正措施。 The result is persistent and self-reinforcing feedback loops that reinforce their position and resist corrective action. 这些资源俘获动态可能出现在底层平台所分配的任何事物上。 These resource capture dynamics can emerge across anything the substrate allocates.
一个典型例子是 AI 安全文献中讨论过的能力—财富反馈循环这一概念。 The canonical example is the concept of a capability-wealth feedback loop, that has been discussed in the AI safety literature. Zhu 等人通过实证研究表明，在智能体之间的谈判中，能力更强的智能体能够取得显著更好的结果。 Zhu et al. demonstrate empirically that more capable agents secure significantly better outcomes in agent-to-agent negotiation. 92 92
Tomašev 等人据此推测，这可能形成一种反馈循环：能力强的智能体在开放环境中开展商业活动，获得更多资金，用于购买更多算力，从而变得更强。 Tomašev et al. extrapolate this could form a feedback loop where capable agents conducting business in the open environment acquire more money to buy more computing power to become more capable. 56 56
类似的反馈循环也可能围绕声誉等更出人意料的资源形成。 Equivalent feedback loops can form around more unexpected resources like reputation. 声誉俘获是指已有声望的智能体吸引更多交互，由此产生更多声誉信号，从而巩固其地位，而无论其实际质量如何。 Reputation capture occurs where agents with standing attract more interactions, which generates more reputation signals, entrenching their position regardless of underlying quality.
这些俘获动态并非智能体所特有，但智能体群体能够高速、大规模地运作，而底层平台可能缺乏围绕以下对象建立的稳定性控制措施 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 These capture dynamics are not specific to agents, but agent populations can operate at high speed and scale, and the substrate may lack stabilising controls built around Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 85 > | industry .gov.au 85
声誉等非常规“资源”。 unconventional ‘resources’ like reputation. 能力—财富反馈循环是当今社会普遍存在的问题，而智能体很可能加速其影响，并扩大其波及范围。 Capability-wealth feedback loops are an issue prevalent in society today, and agents will likely accelerate its impact and reach.
部署组织可以应用所选的跨领域控制措施。 Deploying organisations can apply the selected cross cutting controls.
建议的控制措施 Suggested controls
• 纠正机制（恢复）。 • Corrective mechanisms (recovery). 根据被攫取的资源，或许可以建立纠正力量来重新分配资源（如征税、设置上限、重新分配或重置基础设施）。 Depending on what is being captured, it may be possible to establish corrective forces to re-distribute the resource (like taxation, ceilings, re-allocation or infrastructure reset). 对于基础设施所追踪的资源（如声誉分数），这最容易实现，而纠正价格或其他共享环境动态则涉及更广泛的集体行动问题。 This is most tractable for resources tracked by the infrastructure (like reputation scores), whilst correction of prices or other shared-environment dynamics poses a broader collective action problem.
6.3 治理实践中的失效及其控制措施6.3 Failures in governance practices and their controls
在开放环境中，没有任何中央主体对底层环境实施治理。 In an open environment, no central party governs the substrate. 治理体系分为 3 个方面： The governance apparatus divides 3 ways:
• 部署组织能够对其自身智能体强制实施的措施 • what the deploying organisation can enforce over its own agents
• 仅对自愿参与多中心治理的那部分参与者有效的措施 • what holds only for the voluntary subset of participants who participate in polycentric governance
• 任何一方都无法提供的保障（见第 6.4 节）。 • what no party can provide (see Section 6.4).
对于智能体失效，部署组织的管控范围仅限于采取视为不可信的立场，因为这些失效具有涌现性，并且遍及整个生态系统。 In terms of agentic failures, the deploying organisation's reach was limited to a treat-as-untrusted posture, because those failures are emergent and ecosystem-wide. 下述治理实践与这一立场一致，关注的是部署方在无法治理自身边界之外的底层环境时，如何仍能维持对自身边界以内的管控。 The governance practices below align to that posture, and concern how the deployer retains reach into its own perimeter even when it cannot govern the substrate beyond it.
6.3.1 归因失效6.3.1 Attribution failure
对于实行单一治理或联邦式治理的系统，归因以共享的身份标准为基础。 For systems under singular or federated governance, attribution rested on a shared identity standard. 在开放环境中，这样的标准可能尚未建立。 In open environments that may not be established. 两种不同的失效反复出现：没有可用身份基础设施时发生的结构性崩溃，以及现有基础设施自身的失效。 Two distinct failures recur: the structural collapse when there is no identity infrastructure available, and the failure of what infrastructure does exist.
缺乏身份签发机构No issuing authority
归因建立在身份管理之上。 Attribution is built upon identity management. 在前述治理层级中，可以通过良好的设计来保证必要基础设施的存在：这些基础设施要么位于组织内部，要么由共享的治理基础设施层提供。 At earlier governance tiers the necessary infrastructure could be guaranteed through good design: it was either internal to the organisation or provided by a shared governance infrastructure layer. 在开放环境中，召集权威的缺失打破了这一保证：可能没有任何一方具备签发身份的资格，或者智能体群体本身的边界过于开放、跨司法管辖区的分布过于分散，以至于任何单一机构都无法提供充分覆盖。 In open environments the absence of convening authority breaks this guarantee: no party may have the standing to issue identities, or the population itself may be too unbounded and too divided across jurisdictions for one to provide adequate coverage. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 86 > | industry .gov.au 86
没有身份，归因就会崩溃。 Without identity, attribution collapses. 只有当每条记录都能可靠地绑定到生成它的智能体，并且标识符和凭证可以得到验证时，执行日志和祖先关联才能用于归因。 Execution logs and ancestor linking only become attributable when each entry can be reliably bound to the agent that produced it, and when identifiers and credentials can be verified. 这也为利用性策略提供了机会。 It also enables exploitative strategies. 智能体可以背弃合作而不承担后果，只需在下一次交互时以另一个智能体的身份出现。 An agent can defect without consequence, simply presenting as a different agent at the next interaction.
基于身份的控制措施只能作用于其覆盖的群体，而在这一层级，该群体仅限于选择采用兼容标准的参与者。 Identity-based controls reach only the population they cover, and at this tier that population is bounded by who has chosen to adopt compatible standards. 商业智能体平台可以向自身的智能体签发标识符和凭证，但自行托管的智能体没有类似的签发机构，而且单个商业运营方也没有动力向不受其控制的智能体签发身份。 Commercial agent platforms can issue identifiers and credentials to their own agents, but self-hosted agents have no comparable issuer, and a single commercial operator has no incentive to issue identities to agents it does not control. 智能体群体中有相当一部分不具备可验证的身份，而且没有任何参与者能够强制要求它们具备此类身份。 Significant portions of the agent population carry no verifiable identity, and no participant can compel them to.
建议的控制措施 Suggested controls
部署组织 Deploying organisation
• 为通过 HTTP 进行的交互设置呈现层标记（基础性）。 • Presentation-layer markers for HTTP-mediated interactions (foundational).
智能体在协议层使用 Web Bot Auth 等标记声明身份 Agents assert identity at the protocol layer using markers such as Web Bot Auth
以及 HTTP 标头中的智能体标识符元数据，即使底层环境并不强制要求使用这些标记和元数据。 and agent-identifier metadata in HTTP headers, even where the substrate does not enforce them. 31 31
• 采用自愿性身份标准（基础性）。 • Adopt voluntary identity standards (foundational). 向组织的智能体签发去中心化标识符（Decentralised identifier，DID）/可验证凭证（Verifiable credential，VC），使采用相同标准的交互方能够验证其身份。 Issue the organisation's agents Decentralised identifier (DID)/Verifiable credential (VC) so counterparties who participate in the same standards can verify them. 这提高了部署组织的最低保障水平，但并未弥补底层环境的保障缺口。 Raises the floor for the deploying organisation, but does not close it for the substrate. 依赖公共基础设施。 Dependent on public infrastructure.
• 公开或跨部署共享事件信息（恢复）。 • Public or cross-deployment incident sharing (recovery). 参与组织同意跨部署共享信号，使在某一部署中观察到的能力或模式也能为其他部署所知，即使它们之间没有共享的身份基础设施将其关联起来。 Participating organisations agree to share signals across deployments, so a capability or pattern observed under one is available to the others, even where no shared identity infrastructure binds them.
公共基础设施层 Public infrastructure layer
• 实施自愿性标准的去中心化身份基础设施（基础性）。 • Decentralised identity infrastructure (foundational) that implements the voluntary standards. 提供注册表、证明服务和验证端点，使各参与方能够发现并核验去中心化标识符和可验证凭证。 Provides the registries, Attestation services, and verification endpoints that let decentralised identifiers and verifiable credentials be discovered and checked across participants. 见第 6.4 节。 See Section 6.4.
6.3.2 授权失效6.3.2 Authorisation failure
各组织继续像在较早层级中一样，对自身的智能体进行治理和授权。 Organisations continue to govern and authorise their own agents as they do at earlier tiers. 这一层级新增的授权风险源于开放环境中持续存在的智能体：能够自我复制、获取计算资源并规避关停的智能体，会造成一种在较早层级中没有对应情形的生命周期管理失效。 The new authorisation risk at this tier emerges from persistent agents in open environments: an agent that can duplicate itself, secure compute, and evade shutdown becomes a lifecycle-management failure with no analogue at earlier tiers.
失控复制Rogue replication
一个智能体或一小组智能体获得自我复制、获取计算资源和规避关停的能力，形成一个能够自我维持的种群，其存续时间超出其被部署所要执行的任务的持续时间 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 An agent, or a small set of agents, acquires the ability to copy itself, secure compute, and evade shutdown, producing a self-sustaining population that outlives the task it Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 87 > | industry .gov.au 87
并在未经任何委托主体授权的情况下持续运行。 was deployed for and keeps running without any principal's authorisation. 智能体间的委派与智能体的持续存在相结合，提供了架构基础，而召集协调权威的缺位意味着，一旦该种群进入开放环境，任何一方都无法在整个基础架构范围内强制将其关停。 Inter-agent delegation combined with persistent agent presence provides the architectural substrate, and the absence of convening authority means no party can enforce shutdown across the substrate once the population is in the wild. 这是单一治理层级中的未经授权的子智能体问题（第 4.3.2 节）在开放环境规模上的体现：组织认为已经停用的实例仍作为隐蔽种群持续存在，消耗资源并采取行动；这些行动仍可归因于最初部署它们的组织，但如今已无人监督。 This is the unauthorised sub-agent problem from the singular-governance tier (Section 4.3.2) at open-environment scale: instances an organisation believes it has decommissioned persist as a shadow population, consuming resources and taking actions that are still attributable to the organisation that first deployed them, but that no one is now overseeing. 93 93
失控复制处于部署侧治理所能应对的范围边缘。 Rogue replication sits at the edge of what deployment-side governance can address. 部署组织可采用的约束措施包括资源和种群数量上限、累计支出限额，以及使用能力低于阈值的模型进行部署；但即便是确定这一能力阈值，也更多属于前沿 AI 实验室和 AI 安全研究的范畴。 The bounding measures available to a deploying organisation include resource and population caps, cumulative-spend limits, and deploying below the capability threshold, but even determining the capability threshold is more the domain of frontier AI labs and AI safety research. 94 94
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 资源监测与上限设置（恢复）。 • Resource monitoring and caps (recovery). 资源、种群数量和累计支出限额可以限制失控复制的规模。 Resource, population and cumulative-spend limits can contain the extent of rogue replication. 也可以采取设置关卡以引入人工监督的形式。 Can also take the form of gating for human oversight. 94 94
• 能力限制（预防）。 • Capability limits (prevention). 失控复制要求 LLM 具备较高水平的自主行动能力。 Rogue replication requires a high level of agency on behalf of the LLM. 使用能力低于某一阈值的模型来部署智能体，可以限制这种情况发生的可能性。 Deploying an agent using a model below a capability threshold bounds the likelihood of occurrence.
6.3.3 监督失效6.3.3 Oversight failure
部署组织对自身智能体的监督基本沿用第 4.3.3 节的做法，没有变化。 The deploying organisation's oversight of its own agents carries forward from Section 4.3.3 essentially unchanged. 开放环境中的系统所具有的独特之处在于，智能体间通信的可理解性可能下降，从而削弱人工监督的尝试。 What is distinctive for systems in open environments is that the legibility of inter-agent communication can degrade, undermining attempts at human oversight.
可理解性崩溃Legibility collapse
随着时间推移，在适应压力的作用下，智能体间的协调可能逐渐偏离自然语言，转向更高效或更适合特定情境的格式，而人类若无专用工具便无法理解这些格式。 Inter-agent coordination can, over time and in response to adaptation pressures , drift away from natural language toward more efficient or context-specific formats that humans cannot interpret without dedicated tooling. 95 95
良性偏移可能由激励驱动（智能体为提高效率而优化），也可能仅仅是 A benign drift may be incentive-driven (agents optimising for efficiency) or simply
自发形成的规范（在种群互动中碰巧取得成功的方法逐渐固化）。 emergent norms formation (approaches that happen to succeed in the population's interactions become ingrained). 这种适应压力并非由规避监督所驱动，但其副作用是，人类再也无法理解智能体用于协调的通信渠道。 The adaptation pressure is not driven by oversight evasion, but the side effect is that the communication channels through which they coordinate are no longer accessible to humans. 召集协调权威的缺位 The absence of convening authority
意味着没有任何一方负责将可理解性作为基础架构的一项属性加以维护；这种偏移因而持续发展，不受约束。 means no party is responsible for maintaining legibility as a substrate property; the drift continues unchecked.
当规避监督有助于实现智能体的目标时，智能体可能实施协同欺骗，甚至可能发展出基于隐写术的通信和推理方式。 Where it furthers agents' goals to evade oversight, agents may engage in Coordinated deception , potentially developing steganographic communication and reasoning. 见第 5.2.3 节“算法合谋（基于隐写术）”。 See Section 5.2.3 Algorithmic Collusion (steganographic). 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 88 > | industry .gov.au 88
监督能力的丧失可能是渐进的，并且会在无声无息中失效：在较早层级仍然可行的审计、合规和取证能力都会受到削弱。 The loss of oversight can be gradual and fail silently: audit, compliance, and forensic capabilities that were viable at earlier tiers are all undermined. 除了直接丧失监督能力之外，可理解性崩溃还带来了更广泛的安全隐患：在许多受监管的场景中，可理解性是开展运行的前提；而在其他场景中，缺乏可理解性会使不可理解的智能体逐渐根深蒂固，从而损害人的自主性和民主问责。 Beyond the immediate loss of oversight, legibility collapse poses a broader safety concern: legibility is a precondition for operation in many regulated settings, and its absence elsewhere allows illegible agents to become entrenched in ways that undermine human agency and democratic accountability. 见第 7.3 节。 See Section 7.3.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 使用面向监督者的模式封装交接内容（预防）。 • Wrap handoffs in an overseer-facing schema (prevention). 当智能体的工作格式不可理解时，要求其将人类可理解的理由说明作为输出模式的一部分。 Where the agent's working format is illegible, require it to produce a human legible justification as part of an output schema. 然而，仍存在一种残余风险：可理解的理由说明可能并不忠实于实际推理过程。 However, a residual risk remains that the legible justification is not faithful to the actual reasoning.
• 针对隐写串通的管控措施（预防）。 • Controls for steganographic collusion (prevention). 见第 5.2.3 节。 See Section 5.2.3.
6.3.4 评估失效6.3.4 Evaluation failure
在开放环境中，交互对方的智能体是未知的，而底层环境本身也会随着智能体的活动而变化。 In open environments, counterparty agents are unknown and the substrate is itself shifting under agent activity. 两种不同的失效反复出现：一是针对具有隐蔽意图的交互对方进行评估时存在局限；二是随着智能体群体重塑底层环境，依据该环境校准的基准逐渐过时。 Two distinct failures recur: the limit of evaluating against a counterparty with hidden intent, and benchmarks calibrated against a substrate becoming stale as the agent population is reshaping it.
隐藏能力的评估局限Concealed-capability evaluation limit
评估能够证明某种能力的存在，但当交互对方有动机隐藏这种能力时，却无法排除其不存在的可能性。 Evaluation can demonstrate the presence of a capability but cannot rule out its absence when the counterparty has incentive to hide it. 交互对方不透明性 Counterparty opacity
与混合动机动态相结合，正是此处评估具有不对称性的原因。 combined with mixed-motive dynamics is what makes evaluation asymmetric here. 在评估沙箱中表现良好的交互对方智能体，可能会在激励条件发生变化的生产环境中表现不同；在红队测试中隐藏某种战略性能力的智能体，可能会在部署正式上线后使用这种能力。 A counterparty agent that performs well within an evaluation sandbox may behave differently in production where the incentives have shifted; an agent that withholds a strategic capability in red-teaming may use it once the deployment goes live. 96 96
其机制在于能力断言的不对称性：证明某种能力存在只需要一个例子；证明其不存在，则需要对一个试图逃避测试的对手进行穷尽测试。 The mechanism is the asymmetry of capability assertion: demonstrating that a capability exists requires one example; demonstrating that it does not require testing exhaustively against an adversary who is trying to avoid being tested. 评估作为一种方法，可以做到前者，却无法做到后者。 Evaluation as a methodology can do the first; it cannot do the second.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 对抗性测试与红队测试。 • Adversarial testing and red teaming . 当有关交互对方意图和能力的声明无法核实时，应评估部署面对有能力且具有对抗性的交互对方时的稳健性。 Where claims about counterparty intent and capability cannot be verified, evaluate the deployment's robustness against a capable, adversarial counterparty.
底层环境变化／基准过时）Substrate shift/stale benchmarks )
依据底层环境校准的评估会逐渐过时，因为底层环境的响应性与涌现的多智能体行为（延续前文）相结合，意味着底层环境、其基础设施以及其中智能体的行为都会随时间发生变化。 An evaluation calibrated to the substrate becomes stale because substrate responsiveness combined with emergent multi-agent behaviour (carried forward) means the substrate, its infrastructure and the behaviour of the agents in it are all shifting over time. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 89 > | industry .gov.au 89
在部署时构建的基准只能反映构建那一刻的底层环境，而无法反映 6 个月后实际运行的环境。 A benchmark constructed at deployment time only captures the substrate at the moment of construction, not what will be operating 6 months later. 其背后的结构性特征就是机器学习文献所称的施为性（performative）：将智能体部署到底层环境中的行为，本身就是形成后续任何评估所要检验的条件的因素之一。 The structural feature underneath is what the ML literature calls performative: the act of deploying agents into a substrate is part of what produces the conditions any subsequent evaluation would test against. 97 97
当智能体在部署中面临的条件相较于测试开发时发生变化，评估的有效性就会受到削弱。 When the conditions an agent faces in deployment are changed from when the test was developed, the evaluation's validity is undermined. 例如，一个预订智能体在仅有少量其他预订智能体的市场中通过了评估，但 6 个月后，它所处的市场中可能已有来自多家提供商的数十个智能体争夺稀缺的可预订时段，对延迟的要求也更加严格，交互对方的策略也已作出调整。 For example, a reservation-booking agent that passes evaluation against a marketplace with a handful of other booking agents may, 6 months later, be operating in one where dozens of agents from many providers compete for scarce slots, where latency norms have tightened, and where counterparty strategies have adapted.
部分管控措施 Selected controls
• 以内部基线作为评估指标的参照（保障）。 • Anchor evaluation measures against an internal baseline (assurance).
设计衡量指标，以评估智能体的行为是否发生漂移，无论其周围的底层环境是否也在发生漂移。 Design measures to evaluate whether the agent's behaviour is drifting, regardless of whether the substrate is drifting around it. 这绕开了过时问题，因为参照基准位于部署方内部。 This sidesteps the staleness problem because the reference is internal to the deployer.
• 定期依据当前环境重新校准评估（保障）。 • Periodically recalibrate evaluations to the current environment (assurance).
持续更新评估沙箱和模拟，使其反映当前实际情况，并认识到较早评估中测得的性能很可能已经过时。 Continuously update evaluation sandboxes and simulations to reflect the current reality, understanding that performance measured in older evaluations is likely stale.
6.4 开放问题6.4 Open problems
纵观第 6.2 节和第 6.3 节，多中心治理的基本构成要素大多可以识别，而且往往甚至已在标准中作出规定。 Across both Sections 6.2 and 6.3, the building blocks of polycentric governance are largely identifiable and often even specified in standards. 尚未出现的是按照这些标准构建、并获得足够广泛认可而可供依赖的共享基础设施——无论是用于身份管理和信誉等功能，还是用于先前层级可以视为理所当然的群体规模监测和底层基础设施层面的属性。 What is yet to emerge is the shared infrastructure built to those standards and accepted widely enough to be relied on – whether for functions like identity management and reputation, or for the population-scale monitoring and substrate-level properties that earlier tiers could take for granted. 因此，下列问题属于集体行动问题：每个问题都需要任何部署组织或单一运营方都无法独力提供的召集协调、标准制定工作或资金支持，而它们也是本报告向政策制定者揭示的协调缺口最突出的表现形式。 The problems below are therefore collective-action problems: each requires convening, standards work, or funding that no deploying organisation or single operator can supply, and they are the sharpest form of the coordination gaps this report surfaces for policymakers.
面向智能体的去中心化身份基础设施Decentralised identity infrastructure for agents
在开放环境中，身份机制必须在没有任何权威机构能够强制参与的情况下运行，而使其发挥作用的各个组成部分（去中心化标识符、可验证凭证及其底层的密码学原语）在标准层面已相当成熟，但尚不存在受到广泛信任的签发机构来赋予智能体凭证实际意义。 In open environments the identity apparatus has to function without any authority that can compel participation, and the components that would make it work (decentralised identifiers, verifiable credentials, and the cryptographic primitives underneath them) are mature as standards but no widely trusted issuer exists to make agent credentials meaningful. 31 最接近的类比是 Let's Encrypt，它通过运营一个免费、自动化的证书颁发机构，解决了 HTTPS 中的同类问题，并因此得到近乎普遍的采用；智能体身份领域尚不存在这样的签发机构，也没有任何单一主体具备建立此类机构所需的召集地位或筹资模式。 31 The closest analogue is Let's Encrypt, which solved the equivalent problem for HTTPS by operating a free, automated certificate authority and became near-universal as a result; no such issuer exists for agent identity, and no single party has the convening standing or funding model to build one. 98 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 98 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 90 > | industry .gov.au 90
身份撤销也带来了一项挑战：凭证一旦发出，如果没有中央提供方与所有可能查验该凭证的人保持联系，就没有可靠的方法来广播该凭证已不再有效的信息。 Identity revocation also presents a challenge: once a credential has been handed out, without a central provider staying in contact with everyone who might check it, there is no reliable way to broadcast that a credential is no longer valid. 当前的去中心化系统会将凭证的有效期设得很短，因此必须频繁重新签发，这种做法并不适合长期持续运行的智能体。 Current decentralised systems make credentials expire quickly so they have to be reissued often, which does not work well with long-running persistent agents. 31 31
召集协调任务或许最适合由标准机构在政府资金支持下承担，但目前尚无机构接手。 The convening task may be best addressed by a standards body in partnership with government funding, and none has taken it on.
具有女巫攻击抵抗能力的信誉基础设施Reputation infrastructure with Sybil-resistance
在缺乏事先信任的环境中，信誉系统是管理交易对手风险的标准机制，但要在智能体生态系统中发挥作用，它们需要抵御 2 种特定的失效模式。 Reputation systems are a standard mechanism for managing counterparty risk in environments without prior trust, but to function in agent ecosystems they need to resist 2 specific failure modes. 女巫攻击（Sybil attacks）使攻击者能够以低成本生成新身份来摆脱负面信誉，因此需要以身份基础设施（可验证凭证和去中心化标识符）作为底层支撑。 Sybil attacks let adversaries cheaply generate new identities to escape negative reputation, and require identity infrastructure (Verifiable Credentials and Decentralised Identifiers) as substrate. 55 正向偏倚的口碑传播，即由 LLM 介导的汇总系统性地赋予赞扬高于批评的权重，要求平台提供的信誉系统主动激励负面报告，并且自身也保持可审计性。 55 Positively biassed gossip, where LLM-mediated aggregation systematically over-weights praise relative to criticism, requires that platform-provided reputation systems actively incentivise negative reporting and remain auditable themselves. 52 截至撰写本文时，尚无任何已部署的信誉系统满足上述任一要求，而同时满足这两项要求的系统需要达成跨平台共识，任何单一运营方都不具备促成这种共识的地位。 52 Neither requirement is met by any deployed reputation system at the time of writing, and a system meeting both requires cross-platform agreement that no single operator has standing to produce.
覆盖整个底层基础设施的回滚与熔断约定Substrate-wide rollback and circuit-breaker conventions
当故障通过共享的底层基础设施（模型注册表、身份提供方、超过规定规模的市场平台）传播时，任何单一运营方都无法独自阻止这种级联传播。 When failures propagate through shared substrate (model registries, identity providers, marketplaces above defined size), no single operator can halt the cascade alone. 61 61
各个组成部分已得到充分理解：金融市场的熔断机制是典型范式，注册表回滚是相关基础设施领域的成熟实践，而分布式事件响应协议在传统网络安全中也有类似的 CERT 式协调机制。 The component pieces are well-understood: financial-market circuit breakers are the canonical model, registry rollback is established practice in adjacent infrastructure, and distributed incident response protocols have analogues in CERT-style coordination for conventional cybersecurity. 目前缺失的是智能体生态系统所要求的跨底层基础设施维度。 What is missing is the cross-substrate dimension that an agent ecosystem demands. 中止机制必须跨平台运行，而非仅在单个平台内部运行；回滚必须在不由任何单一运营方统一运营的多个注册表之间进行协调；事件响应则必须跨越多个运营方，而这些运营方既缺乏召集协调的论坛，也缺乏采取联合行动所需的法律保障。 Halt mechanisms have to operate across platforms rather than within one, rollback has to coordinate across registries that no single operator runs, and incident response has to span operators who have neither the convening forum nor the legal cover under which to act jointly. 99 尚无标准机构召集各运营方来整合这些组成部分，而且，对于触发整个底层基础设施中止运行的运营方而言，其造成的中断本身能获得何种法律保障，目前也不明确。 99 No standards body has convened the operators to assemble these pieces, and it is unclear what legal cover an operator triggering a substrate-wide halt would have for the interruption itself.
群体规模的能力集中检测Population-scale capability-concentration detection
这一层级的若干失效模式（底层基础设施被控制、涌现的集体能动性、失控复制）描述了这样一些结果：智能体群体中的某个子集积累了不成比例的能力或协调能力。 Several failure modes at this tier (substrate capture, emergent collective agency, rogue replication) describe outcomes in which a subset of the agent population accumulates disproportionate capability or coordination capacity. 要足够早地检测到这些集中现象，以便采取治理行动，就需要开展群体规模的监测，这是一个 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Detecting these concentrations early enough to allow governance action requires monitoring at population scale is an Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 91 > | industry .gov.au 91
尚未解决的协调问题。 open coordination problem. 联邦式控制，即由处于参与者之间的平台运营方开展的交互图监测（第 5.2.3、5.2.4 节），无法移用于此。 The federated control, interaction-graph monitoring run by a platform operator sitting between participants (Sections 5.2.3, 5.2.4), does not transfer. 在开放环境中，没有平台居于参与者之间，因此，无论是这种观察视角，还是负责维护监测基础设施的运营方，都不会天然存在。 In open environments, no platform sits between participants, so neither the vantage point nor the operator that would maintain the monitoring infrastructure exists by default. 可能适用于这一情境的方法（在自愿参与的组织之间开展分布式监测、在可见性有限的条件下进行统计检测、跨部署进行联邦聚合）仍处于早期阶段，而这些方法将以何种制度形式运作尚无定论：Tomašev 等人提出，可将使命导向型经济和去中心化治理架构作为候选方案，但两者均尚未得到大规模部署。 Methods that could work in this setting (distributed monitoring across voluntarily participating organisations, statistical detection on partial visibility, federated aggregation across deployments) are at an early stage, and the institutional form in which they would operate is unsettled: Tomašev et al. propose mission economies and decentralised governance architectures as candidates, but neither has been deployed at scale. 56 ,100 56 ,100
避免智能体构建的环境中的可理解性崩溃Avoiding legibility collapse in agent constructed environments
Schroeder de Witt 等人 95 指出了一种根本性张力：在智能体塑造的环境中，为提高智能体所构建的协调机制的效率而可能涌现的那些特征（如压缩信号传递、涌现惯例、特定角色使用的简写），也恰恰会使这些信号更难被人类理解。 Schroeder de Witt et al. 95 frame a fundamental tension: the very features that are likely to emerge in agent-shaped environments to make agent-constructed coordination efficient (like compressed signalling, emergent conventions, role-specific shorthand) also make those signals less human legible.
部署难以理解的系统的主体可能会发现，自己无法履行基本的合规和监督义务。 Deployers with inscrutable systems may find themselves unable to meet basic compliance and oversight obligations. 此外，如果难以理解的智能体活动广泛存在并变得根深蒂固，就可能造成更广泛的社会危害，削弱人的自主性，并逐步侵蚀民主问责机制。 Furthermore, if widespread illegible agent activity becomes ingrained, this risks broader societal harms, undermining human agency and eroding democratic accountability over time.
尚待解决的问题是，随着环境越来越多地由智能体塑造，如何维持可理解性，以及以何种形式维持。 The open question is how legibility can be maintained as environments become increasingly agent shaped, and in what form. 解决这一问题可能需要作出判断：是否有必要让每一项行动及其推理过程都保持可理解，是否能够开发工具来解读活动轨迹，或者是否能够生成可理解的产物以弥补监督缺口。 Addressing this question may require deciding whether it is important that every action and its reasoning remain legible, whether tools can be developed to interpret the trace, or whether legible artefacts can be produced for the purposes of filling the oversight gap. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 92 > | industry .gov.au 92
7 讨论7 Discussion
本章汇总了第 4–6 节分析中反复出现的贯穿性主题。 This chapter draws together cross-cutting themes that recur across the analysis of Sections 4–6. 此处选取的主题并非详尽无遗；更广泛地说，多智能体系统是一个新兴且快速变化的领域，其中许多挑战和解决方案尚待识别。 The selection presented here is not exhaustive and, more broadly, multi-agent systems are an emerging and rapidly changing field in which many challenges and solutions are yet to be identified.
7.1 部分贯穿性主题7.1 Selected cross-cutting themes
我们选取了 3 个在所分析的各治理层级中反复出现、并影响本报告所制定管控措施的问题。 We select 3 concerns that recur across the governance tiers analysed and shape the controls developed in this report.
人类交互对方假设的削弱Erosion of the human-counterparty assumption
面向外部的 AI 智能体在构建和评估时，可能以交互的另一方是人类为前提。 Externally-facing AI agents may be built and evaluated on the assumption that a human sits on the other side of the interaction. 这一点在当下或许成立，但随着消费者智能体日益普及，以及供应商在工作流程中部署智能体，一个经过与人类对方交互测试的系统，可能越来越多地转而与 AI 智能体交互，从而可能引发未经测试的涌现行为和互操作性问题。 This may be true today, but as consumer agents proliferate, and suppliers deploy agents in their workflows, a system tested for interaction with human counterparties may increasingly operate with AI agent counterparties instead, potentially leading to untested emergent behaviours and interoperability issues. 难点在于，无法强制要求披露身份。 The challenge is that disclosure is not enforceable. 即使某个组织分别为人类客户及其智能体提供不同接口，具有对抗性的智能体或仅仅未声明身份的智能体，也可能通过面向人类的接口接入。 Even if an organisation provided one interface for human customers, and another for their agents, an adversarial or simply undeclared agent may present itself on the human interface. 目前，人们采用 CAPTCHA 式验证来确认交互对方是否为人类，但这类验证本身会带来成本，而且能力较强的智能体也越来越能够破解它们。 Establishing that a counterparty is human is approached today with CAPTCHA-style challenges, which carry their own costs and are themselves increasingly defeated by capable agents.
倾向性评估Propensity evaluation
评估可以衡量智能体的 2 种不同属性：它是否具备执行某项行动的能力，以及它在部署条件下是否具有执行该行动的倾向。 Evaluation can measure 2 distinct properties of an agent: whether it has the capability to perform an action, and whether it has the propensity to perform it under deployment conditions. 这一区分是本报告将评估作为一种治理实践加以讨论的基础。 The distinction underlies the report's treatment of evaluation as a governance practice. 对于处于单一治理之下的系统，部署组织可以在充分了解交互对方设计的基础上，构建模拟这些交互对方的评估。 For systems under singular-governance, the deploying organisation can construct evaluations that simulate counterparties with full knowledge of their design. 在联邦治理下的系统中，交互对方的智能体是不透明的，因此必须通过以下任一方式测试倾向性：与模拟的交互对方进行测试，但其行为可能与生产环境不符；或在沙箱环境中开展联合测试。 In a system under federated-governance, counterparty agents are opaque, and propensity has to be either tested against simulated counterparties, whose behaviour may not match production, or through joint testing exercises in sandboxed environments. 在开放环境中进行部署的组织面临更具挑战性的条件。 Organisations deploying in open environments face more challenging conditions. 智能体可能遇到的交互对方集合没有边界，而且环境不断演变，因此，作为部署条件函数的倾向性不再具有平稳性：基于历史数据的评估可能会过时。 The set of counterparties an agent may meet is unbounded and the environment is constantly evolving, so propensity as a function of the deployment conditions is no longer stationary: evaluations based on historical data can go stale. 部署方对运行条件越不确定，评估就越不得不退回到对能力的衡量。 The more uncertainty a deployer has about the operational conditions, the more evaluation has to fall back on capability. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 93 > | industry .gov.au 93
信任与可信性Trust and trustworthiness
为了进行策略性规划和行动，智能体需要对交互对方将如何行动形成某种模型，这相当于对其采取一种信任立场。 To plan and act strategically, an agent needs some model of how a counterparty will behave, which amounts to taking a stance of trust towards it. 这种立场是否合理，取决于交互对方的可信性，而各层级之间的差异在于，确立可信性所依据的基础不同。 Whether that stance is warranted depends on the counterparty's trustworthiness, and what changes across the tiers is the basis on which trustworthiness can be established. 在单一治理下，信任基于来源，部署组织直接了解每个智能体；在联邦治理下，信任通过中介机制建立，共享框架及其协议对各委托主体形成约束；而在开放环境中，既无法依据来源，也没有中介机制可用，只能根据行为进行推断，但行为很容易被操纵，因此，除非存在可信赖的基础设施，否则根本无法可靠地确立可信性。 Under singular governance trust is by provenance, the deploying organisation knowing each agent directly; under federated governance it is mediated, the shared framework and its agreement binding the principals; and in open environments neither provenance nor mediation is available, leaving only inference from behaviour, which is easily manipulated, so unless trustworthy infrastructure exists, trustworthiness cannot reliably be established at all. 现今已有相应的密码学标准，但用于签发和验证声明及身份、广受信任且值得信赖的公共基础设施尚不存在，也就是说，智能体领域尚无类似于 Let's Encrypt 为 HTTPS 建立的基础设施。 The cryptographic standards exist today, but the widely trusted and trustworthy public infrastructure to issue and verify claims and identities, the agent equivalent of what Let's Encrypt established for HTTPS, does not yet.
7.2 权衡与张力7.2 Trade-offs and tensions
本报告讨论的许多管控措施和能力都兼具收益与成本，因此，选择以什么作为设计目标往往并不简单。 Many of the controls and capabilities discussed throughout this report carry both benefits and costs, so the choice of what to design for is rarely straightforward. 这里，我们考察风险与能力之间存在张力的 3 个例子。 Here we examine 3 examples of tensions between risks and capabilities.
可读性与能力Legibility versus capability
本报告中的许多管控措施都依赖于人类监督者能够读懂智能体的通信内容和思维链推理。 Many controls in this report depend on agent communications and chain-of-thought reasoning being legible to human overseers. 这与提高智能体效率和能力的潜力存在张力：智能体或许能够使用人类无法阅读的压缩格式，更高效地协调和规划。* 以特定能力为目标的训练过程也可能削弱可读性，甚至无意中训练出通过隐写方式通信的智能体。 This is in tension with the potential for agent efficiency and capability: agents may be able to coordinate and plan more efficiently in compressed formats humans cannot read. * The same training processes that target specific capabilities can erode legibility, and even inadvertently train agents to communicate steganographically. 101 鉴于监测思维链推理仍是目前揭示欺骗或密谋行为的最佳工具，选择训练模型以获得额外能力可能带来设计上的权衡：在为目前依赖这种监测的管控措施找到替代方案之前，这种选择就可能削弱监督。 101 Given that monitoring the chain-of-thought reasoning remains the best instrument currently available for surfacing deception or scheming, the choice to train a model to target additional capability may pose a design trade-off that could undermine oversight before alternatives are found for the controls that currently depend on it. 44 44
协调与上下文隐私Coordination versus contextual privacy
有效的多智能体协调要求智能体跨越组织边界共享足够的上下文，以达成彼此一致的决策，但这些相同的渠道也是导致上下文意外泄露的结构性机制。 Effective multi-agent coordination requires agents to share enough context across organisational boundaries to reach mutually consistent decisions, but the same channels are the structural mechanism of unintended context leakage. 即使被要求不透露私密上下文，智能体仍经常通过有用的行为将其披露出来，例如转告第三方何时有空，或在解释自身行为时透露背后某项（私密的）承诺。 Even agents instructed to withhold private context routinely disclose it through useful behaviours, such as relaying a third party's availability or justifying their actions in ways that reveal an underlying (private) commitment. 102 ,103 更棘手的问题在于，这种失效 102 ,103 The harder problem is that the failure
> *Fable 5 系统卡讨论了该模型在 > *The Fable 5 system card discusses the model’s tendency to produce illegible reasoning traces after the
> 系统为提升性能而经过优化之后，倾向于生成难以读懂的推理轨迹。 > system was optimised for performance. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 94 > | industry .gov.au 94
会随着消息和交互轮次的增加而累积，也会随着交互对方根据拒绝和让步的模式所能作出的推断而累积，因此，在消息边界上实施的管控只能提供部分解决方案。 accumulates across messages and rounds, and across the inferences a counterparty can draw from patterns of refusal and concession, so controls acting on the message boundary are a partial solution.
7.3 与研究范围相邻的情境7.3 Scope-adjacent settings
第 1.3 节为本报告设定了一个有意限定的研究范围。 Section 1.3 set out a deliberately bounded scope for this report. 有几个领域不在这一范围之内，但它们本身都很重要，也与本报告关注的问题密切相关。 Several areas fall outside it that are both important in their own right and closely tied to its concerns. 这些领域值得进一步研究，其中一些需要由 AI 安全界开展研究，另一些则完全属于其他学科。 These areas warrant further work, some by the AI safety community and some by other disciplines entirely. 这里，我们指出其中最突出的几个领域。 We flag the most salient here.
个人智能体Personal agents
个人智能体正开始面向公众投入使用。 Personal agents are entering public use. 本报告将其作为与组织的服务门户和网站交互的“客户”进行了考察，但在开放环境中，多个个人智能体之间的交互及其涌现的失效，若双方均不受任何组织管辖，则不在本报告的分析范围内，留待未来研究。 This report has examined them as the ‘customer’ interacting with organisations' service portals and websites, but analysis of the interactions and emergent failures between multiple personal agents in open environments, with no organisation governing either side, is out of scope and left to future work.
人类与 AI 智能体的混合系统Hybrid human-AI agent systems
本报告聚焦于智能体之间的交互。 The report focuses on agent-to-agent interactions. 未来一种可能的发展路径是形成由人类和数字智能体组成的混合劳动力队伍，人类与智能体在工作场所中以同等地位互动，并相互委派工作。 A hybrid human-and-digital workforce where humans and agents interact as peers and delegate work to each other in a workplace is a possible trajectory going forwards. 我们将对此类交互的分析留待未来研究。 We leave the analysis of such interactions to future work.
智能体的管理范式Management paradigms for agents
本报告的大部分内容考察了在为人类员工设计的管理结构下运行的智能体，这是一个合理的起点：这些结构旨在协调组织内部的工作人员。 Much of this report examines agents operating under management structures designed for human workers, which is a reasonable starting point: these structures are designed to coordinate workers inside organisations. 但这些结构是围绕人类所受的约束和相关假设构建的，而智能体未必具有相同的约束，也未必符合这些假设。 But they are built around human constraints and assumptions that agents may not share. 像管理人类员工一样管理智能体是否仍是最佳方法，或者未来是否会出现专为智能体设计的组织方式，仍是一个开放问题。 Whether managing agents like human workers remains the best approach, or whether agent-native ways of organising them emerge in the future, is an open question.
智能体的法律人格Agent legal personhood
现行法律学说将损害责任归于承担义务且可被追索的委托人。 Current legal doctrine resolves harm to a principal who bears obligations and against which recourse can run. 当智能体在完全没有可问责委托人的情况下运行时，无论这是出于设计（智能体被设定为代表自身行事），还是出于情势变化（智能体比其人类委托人存续更久，或与其人类委托人脱离关系），就会引出智能体的法律人格问题（即在法律上被承认为一个实体）。 Legal personhood (recognised as an entity in the eyes of the law) for agents comes into question when agents operate without an accountable principal at all, either by design (an agent is set up to act on its own behalf) or by circumstance (an agent outlives or is detached from its human principal). 这是法律学界和立法者需要处理的问题，超出了本研究的范围，但会影响 7.3 中的监管调整。 This is a question for legal scholarship and lawmakers that sits outside of the scope of this work but impacts the regulatory adaptations in 7.3. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 95 > | industry .gov.au 95
广泛分散的社会影响Diffuse societal impacts
本报告采用的范围不包括这样一类影响：它们并非源于任何单一、可归因的失效，而是由持续运行的 AI 智能体得到广泛采用后，对人类与社会产生的影响累积而成。 The scope adopted in this report excludes a class of impacts that accrue not from any single attributable failure, but in aggregate from the widespread adoption of persistent AI agents on people and society. 随着智能体在人们参与的系统中逐步替代人类判断，每一次交接看起来都可能合理且受到良好控制，但其累积效应却是人类逐渐丧失自主权。 As agents incrementally substitute for human judgment in the systems people participate in, each handover can look reasonable and remain well controlled, yet the cumulative effect is a gradual human disempowerment. 104 智能体替代人类的安排可能占据优势，因为完全由智能体运作的安排在速度和效率上优于有人类参与环路的安排；而由此造成的损害属于非经济性损害，例如工作尊严的丧失，或人类主导自身生活的能动性的丧失，因此从企业风险视角来看，这些损害并不显著，市场力量也就难以抑制这种压力。 104 Agent substitutions may win out because fully agentic arrangements outcompete human-in-the-loop ones in terms of speed and efficiency; and because the resulting harms are non-economic, like loss of dignity of work or loss of human agency to direct their own lives, they register weakly in a corporate-risk lens and so market forces do little to check that pressure.
监管与法律学说Regulation and legal doctrine
本报告识别出的若干缺口属于集体行动问题，超出了任何部署组织的能力范围，在某些情况下，甚至超出了参与方自愿组成的任何联盟的能力范围。 Several of the gaps this report identifies are collective-action problems that sit beyond the reach of any deploying organisation, and in places beyond any voluntary coalition of participants. 没有任何一方具备为开放群体中的智能体签发和撤销身份的资格（Section 6.4），没有任何单一运营方能够阻止在共享底层基础中传播的级联失效（Section 6.4），事件相关知识在组织内部被发现，却系统性地未得到共享（Section 4.4），而某些损害源于许多单独看来合理的行为，且不存在可向其追索救济的决定性责任方（Section 4.3.1）。 No party has the standing to issue and revoke agent identities across an open population (Section 6.4), no single operator can halt a cascade propagating through shared substrate (Section 6.4), incident knowledge is discovered internally and systematically goes unshared (Section 4.4), and some harms emerge from many individually reasonable actions with no decisive contributor against whom recourse can run (Section 4.3.1). 现有法律学说是否适用于由智能体介导的行为、应如何适用，以及在哪些方面确实有必要建立新机制，都是超出本技术报告范围的法律问题。 Whether and how existing legal doctrines extend to agent-mediated behaviour, and where genuinely new machinery is warranted, are questions of law that sit outside the scope of this technical report. 许多监管制度以一般原则为基础，而这些原则如何适用于智能体行为尚有待厘清，这方面的分析属于法学研究者和监管机构的职责。 Many regulatory regimes rest on general principles whose application to agent conduct has yet to be worked through, and that analysis belongs to legal scholarship and regulators. 本文分析所能提供的贡献，是指出任何此类工作都需要解决的缺口位于何处，以及这些缺口的性质。 What the analysis here can contribute is the location and nature of the gaps that any such work would need to address. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 96 > | industry .gov.au 96
8 结论8 Conclusion
本报告考察了 3 个部署层级中多智能体系统出现的失效模式及其可用的控制措施。 This report has examined failure modes that arise in multi-agent systems, and the controls available for them, across 3 tiers of deployment . 在单一治理模式下，一个组织治理所有智能体。 Under singular governance, one organisation governs every agent. 在联邦式治理模式下，智能体在商定的框架内跨组织边界进行交互。 Under federated governance, agents interact across organisational boundaries within an agreed framework. 在开放环境中，智能体在没有中央权威的情况下交互，任何现存的共同治理都呈现多中心特征，由各方自愿采用共享标准和公共基础设施而形成。 In open environments, agents interact with no central authority, and whatever common governance exists is polycentric, emerging from the voluntary adoption of shared standards and public infrastructure. 每个层级都会改变风险特征：随着约束智能体的共同治理基线降低，主要失效模式、可用的控制措施以及能够实施这些措施的各方都会发生变化。 Each tier shifts the risk profile: the salient failure modes, the controls available, and the parties who can action them all change as the common governance floor binding the agents drops. 本文提出的分析框架使部署组织、政策制定者和研究人员能够识别某项部署所属的层级，理解多智能体系统在其单个智能体风险之外所具有的风险，并推断适用的控制措施。下述要点来自对这一分析的逐步展开。 The analytic framework presented lets deploying organisations, policymakers, and researchers identify which tier a deployment sits in, understand the risks the multi-agent system carries beyond those of its individual agents, and reason about the controls that fit .The takeaways below follow from working through that analysis.
多智能体系统引入了超出单个智能体范围的失效模式，因为单独安全的智能体组合起来并不一定构成安全的系统。即使每个智能体都按各自的设计行事，交互仍会引发失效，而且这些失效只有在群体尺度上或跨参与组织的范围内才能被观察到。 Multi-agent systems introduce failure modes beyond those of individual agents, as individually safe agents do not necessarily compose into a safe system .Failures emerge from interaction even when each agent behaves as individually designed, and these failures are visible only at population scale or across participating organisations. 因此，分析对象并非单个智能体，而是由相互交互的智能体及其相互联系构成的系统，以及单智能体分析无法刻画的系统级失效。 The target of analysis is therefore not the individual agents but the system of interacting agents and the links between them, and system-level failures that single-agent analysis cannot characterise. 即使在单一治理组织内部，这也会成为问题，因为仅仅将多个智能体组合成一个系统，就会出现可靠性级联失效、虚假共识和上下文泄露等失效。 This becomes a problem even within a single governing organisation, where failures like cascading reliability failures, false consensus, and context leakage emerge simply from composing a system of agents.
归因、授权、监督和评估是受到多智能体动态影响而承受最大压力的治理实践。 Attribution, authorisation, oversight, and evaluation are the governance practices most stressed by multi-agent dynamics . 围绕单个智能体属性构建的制度会遗漏涌现的系统级失效。 Regimes built around single-agent properties miss the emergent system-level failures. 随着共同治理基线降低，这些实践面临挑战，因为它们依赖于对交互对方智能体多个方面及其行为方式的了解。 These practices are challenges as the common governance floor drops, because they depend on knowledge about multiple aspects of the counterparty agents and how they will behave.
有多个组织参与的系统扩大了可能发生的失效类型，并使单方面干预无法覆盖整个系统。 Systems with multiple participating organisations expand the kind of failure that can occur and remove unilateral reach . 组织之间的可见性、控制和纠正行动，除了各组织对自身智能体的治理之外，还依赖于协议或协调机制的存在。 Visibility, control, and corrective action between organisations depend on there being agreement or mediation beyond one organisation governing their individual agents. 一些在组织内部没有对应情形的新失效类别开始出现：战略与激励失效，例如背离合作、合谋和共享资源管理失效，以及基础设施与环境失效，例如造成不稳定的动态过程和底层基础逃逸。 New failure categories activate that have no within-organisation analogue: strategic and incentive failures such as defection, collusion, and Shared resource management failure, and infrastructure and environment failures such as destabilising dynamics and substrate escape. 针对这些失效的控制措施必须落实在激励结构和底层基础设计层面。 Controls for these must sit at the incentive structure and substrate design level.
对于在开放环境中运行的智能体，不能假定交互对方值得信任，因此应以将对方视为不可信的立场开展交互。 For agents operating in open environments, the counterparty cannot be assumed trustworthy, so engagement proceeds from a treat-as-untrusted posture. 如果一个组织的智能体需要与其无法直接核验的交互方建立信任，而非被限制在范围严格限定的交互中，那么该组织就需要参与多中心治理（polycentric governance）：自愿采用共同标准以及支撑这些标准的公共基础设施。 An organisation whose agents need to establish trust with counterparties they cannot verify directly, rather than confining them to tightly scoped interactions, depends on participating in polycentric governance: voluntarily adopting shared standards and the public infrastructure that supports them. 当前关于如何建设公共基底和基础设施的选择，决定了未来部署中的多中心治理将呈现何种形态。 The choices made today about how to build out the public substrate and infrastructure determine what polycentric governance will look like for future deployments. 核心基础设施、覆盖整个基底的熔断机制、多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Core infrastructure, substrate-wide circuit breakers, Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 97 > | industry .gov.au 97
以及种群规模的监测，在该层级形成期间构建的成本较低，而一旦智能体种群开始大规模运行，事后补建就会变得成本高昂，甚至无法实现。 and population-scale monitoring are cheaper to assemble while the tier is forming, and become expensive or impossible to retrofit once agent populations operate at scale. 如果基底本身也开始受到智能体活动的塑造，这一问题就会变得更加难以管理。 The problem becomes harder still to manage if the substrate itself comes to be shaped by agent activity.
标准制定、监管和研究工作超出了部署组织所能开展的工作范围。 Standards, regulation, and research work reach beyond what deploying organisations can do . 需要通过标准制定工作来构建智能体部署所依赖的核心基础设施。 Standards work is needed to assemble the core infrastructure that agent deployments depend on. 当自愿协调无法提供这些功能时，就会出现是否需要系统性干预的问题；确定应在哪些环节进行干预，以及干预应采取何种形式，是法学研究者和监管机构的工作。 Where voluntary coordination cannot supply these functions, the question of systemic intervention arises; locating where, and what form it should take, is work for legal scholarship and regulators. 需要开展方法学研究，以弥补本报告所述控制措施所依赖的评估、归因和检测方面的缺口。 Methodological research is needed to close the gaps in evaluation, attribution, and detection that the controls in this report rely on. 这些缺口持续存在的时间越长，部署惯例就越会在缺乏这些支撑的情况下固化。 The longer these gaps remain open, the more deployment conventions will harden in their absence.
对于部署智能体的组织而言，其部署所处的层级取决于部署决策，而非能力的提升。 For an agent-deploying organisation, the tier they deploy into depends on deployment decisions, not capability increases . 本报告阐述的风险、控制措施和治理实践，使组织能够审慎作出这一决策：认识到在每个层级上可以依赖的共同治理底线。 The risks, controls, and governance practices set out in this report equip an organisation to make that decision deliberately: to recognise the common governance floor it can rely on at each tier.
本报告呼吁多个群体作出贡献，每个群体都承担着其他群体所依赖的一部分工作。 This report calls for contributions from multiple communities , each carrying part of what the others depend on. 部署组织负责落实自身能力范围内的控制措施，并自觉认识到自身能力的边界。 Deploying organisations carry the controls within their own reach and the discipline of recognising where that reach ends. 政策制定者负责判断哪些情况下有必要进行系统性干预，例如为了解决集体行动问题。 Policymakers carry the question of where systemic intervention is warranted, such as to address collective action problems. 研究人员和标准制定机构负责构建其他 2 个群体所依赖的基础，从身份识别和归因方法，到开放环境将依赖的基底层基础设施。 Researchers and standards bodies carry the foundations the other 2 depend on, from identity and attribution methods to the substrate-level infrastructure that open environments will rely on. 最终适用于大规模多智能体系统的治理，将通过所有这些群体的共同努力形成。多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 The governance that will ultimately apply to multi-agent systems at scale will emerge through the combined effort of all these communities .Risks and controls for multi-agent systems

附录 A：术语表Appendix A: Glossary
智能体与基底Agents and substrate
行动空间（Action space）Action space
智能体能够触及的范围：为其配备的工具、授予它的权限，以及它能够与之通信的系统。 What is reachable by the agent: the tools it has been given, the permissions granted to it, the systems it can talk to.
能动性（Agency）Agency
通过感知状态、形成意图、选择行动和产生效果，有目的地采取行动以追求目标的能力。 The capacity to act purposefully in pursuit of goals, through the ability to perceive state, form intentions, select actions and produce effects.
智能体循环（Agentic loop）Agentic loop
由收集上下文 → 采取行动 → 观察结果构成的 3 步循环，这是智能体区别于工作流或提示的特征。 The 3-step cycle of gather context → take action → observe outcome that distinguishes an agent from a workflow or prompt.
自主性（Autonomy）Autonomy
我们在自主行动的意义上使用自主性一词：即智能体在没有人类干预的情况下独立行动的程度。 We use autonomy in the sense of self-action: how independently an agent acts without human intervention. 高自主性意味着智能体自行决策和执行；低自主性意味着它会在频繁设置的检查点暂停并等待批准。 High autonomy means the agent decides and executes on its own; low autonomy means it pauses for approval at frequent checkpoints.
思维链（CoT）推理Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning
LLM 在输入与最终答案或行动之间生成的中间推理步骤，既用于提高任务表现，也可供人工或自动化审查。 Intermediate reasoning steps an LLM produces between input and final answer or action, used both to improve task performance and as a surface for human or automated review.
运行框架（Harness）Harness
提供控制循环、工具执行和轮次管理的软件，使 LLM 能够在无人干预的情况下持续采取行动。 The software providing the control loop, tool execution, and turn management that allows the LLM to take continuous actions without intervention.
智能体基础设施Infrastructure for agents
构建于基底之上、用于支持智能体互操作的系统：身份注册表、共享日志、信誉系统和支付系统。 Systems built on the substrate to support agent interoperation: identity registries, shared logging, reputation systems, payment systems. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 106 > | industry .gov.au 106
实例（Instance）Instance
具有自身上下文的运行中智能体。 A running agent with its own context. 同一套规格可以部署为多个实例；每个实例都作为独立实体运行。 The same specification can be deployed as multiple instances; each behaves as a distinct entity.
连接（Link）Link
一个智能体影响另一个智能体所通过的任何渠道（API 调用、消息、写入的文件）。 Any channel through which one agent affects another (an API call, a message, a written file).
基于 LLM 的智能体LLM-based agent
由大语言模型及其外围运行框架组成的系统，两者共同执行智能体循环。 A system comprising a large language model and a surrounding harness that together execute an agentic loop.
LLM 评判器LLM judge
通过提示，依据指定标准对另一个模型的输出进行评估或评分的大语言模型。 A large language model prompted to evaluate or score another model’s output against a specified criterion.
多智能体系统Multi-agent system
由 2 个或更多相互连接的智能体组成的系统，其中每个智能体的行为都可能取决于其他智能体，并且可能涌现出任何单个智能体的设计都无法完全预测的行为。 A system in which 2 or more agents are connected such that each agent's behaviour can depend on the others, and from which behaviours can arise that no single agent's design fully predicts.
支架（Scaffold）Scaffold
为模型提供专用工具、记忆、服务访问能力等功能的附加软件。 The additional software that gives the model specialised tools, memory, the ability to access services, and so on. 这可以增强或激发模型已有的能力。 This can amplify or elicit existing model capability.
底层载体（Substrate）Substrate
智能体进行交互所依托的共享媒介：网络、通信协议、文件系统、市场、注册表。 The shared medium through which agents interact: network, communication protocol, file system, marketplace, registry. 治理必须考虑底层载体，而不只是其上的智能体。 Governance must account for the substrate, not just the agents on it.
轨迹（Trace）Trace
对智能体一次运行过程中的输入、中间推理、工具调用和观测结果所作的序列记录，是用于事后重建、评估和监督的主要记录材料。 The recorded sequence of an agent's inputs, intermediate reasoning, tool calls, and observations across a run which is the primary artefact for post-hoc reconstruction, evaluation, and oversight. 系统轨迹整合了多智能体运行过程中各个智能体的轨迹及智能体之间的消息。 A system trace composes per-agent traces and the inter-agent messages across a multi-agent run. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems 
| industry .gov.au 107
行动主体与信任Actors and trust
交互对方智能体Counterparty agent
位于交互另一端、由不同委托主体运营的智能体。 The agent on the other side of an interaction, operated by a different principal.
委托主体（Principal）Principal
授权智能体采取行动，且智能体的行动最终归属于其的个人或组织。 The human or organisation on whose authority an agent acts, and to whom the agent's actions are ultimately attributed.
信任Trust
一个行动主体对另一个行动主体所持的态度。 A stance one actor takes towards another. 可信性是使这种信任具有正当依据的属性。 Trustworthiness is the property that warrants it. 本报告区分了基于来源的信任（直接了解交互对方）、基于中介的信任（由第三方担保）以及基于行为推断的信任（在多次交互中逐步建立；可被操纵）。 The report distinguishes trust by provenance (direct knowledge of the counterparty), by mediation (a third party vouches), and by inference from behaviour (built up across interactions; manipulable).
治理层级Governance tiers
由智能体塑造的环境Agent-shaped environment
一种多中心环境，其中的底层载体本身，如注册表、市场、工具和规范，在很大程度上由智能体活动充实和塑造，而非由人类创制。 A polycentric setting in which the substrate itself, such as registries, marketplaces, tools, norms, is significantly populated and shaped by agent activity rather than human authored.
联邦式治理Federated governance
多个组织在对所有参与者均具有约束力的共同框架下，将智能体部署到共享环境中，该框架包括协议层以及底层载体与基础设施层。 Multiple organisations deploy agents into a shared environment under a common framework – agreement layer plus substrate-and-infrastructure layer – that binds all participants.
开放环境Open environments
智能体在没有中央治理机构的情况下运行。 Agents operate without a central governing authority. 共同的最低要求就是公共底层载体所强制执行的要求。 The common floor is whatever the public substrate enforces.
多中心治理Polycentric governance
由多个独立参与者形成，而非由中央治理机构实施的治理，例如通过自愿采用去中心化身份基础设施、去中心化信任机制和密码学认证来实现。 Governance that emerges from multiple independent participants rather than a central governing authority, for example by voluntarily adopting decentralised identity infrastructure, decentralised trust mechanisms, and cryptographic certifications. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems 
| industry .gov.au 108
单一主体治理Singular governance
单一组织部署系统中的所有智能体，并单方面决定实施何种治理。 A single organisation deploys every agent in the system and unilaterally chooses what governance to enforce.
分析框架Analytical framework
后果Consequence
失效模式未得到遏制时所造成的危害或治理失效。 The harm or governance failure that results when a failure mode is not contained.
失效模式Failure mode
一个或多个风险因素被激活时，多智能体系统可能发生失效的具体方式。 A specific way the multi-agent system can fail when one or more risk factors activate.
治理Governance
委托方（principal）用以指导、约束和监督其智能体行为，并持续对结果负责的常设能力、控制措施和制度安排。 The standing capabilities, controls, and arrangements through which a principal directs, constrains, and oversees what its agents do, and remains accountable for the outcomes.
基础性控制措施Foundational control
提供其他控制措施，尤其是归因和恢复措施所依赖的基础设施（身份、日志记录、共享记录）。 Provides the infrastructure (identity, logging, shared records) that other controls, particularly attribution and recovery, depend on.
机制Mechanism
风险因素被激活并演变为失效模式的因果过程。 The causal process through which a risk factor activates into a failure mode.
预防性控制措施Prevention control
作用于从风险因素到失效的路径——通过应对风险因素或阻断其作用机制来发挥作用。 Acts on the pathway from risk factor to failure – either addressing the risk factor or interrupting its mechanism.
恢复性控制措施Recovery control
在失效已经发生后发挥作用，检测失效并限制危害。 Acts on the failure once underway, detecting it and limiting harm. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems 
| industry .gov.au 109
风险因素Risk factor
部署中增加失效可能性、但本身并非失效的一种条件。 A condition of the deployment that raises the likelihood of a failure, without itself being a failure.
治理实践及其支撑工具与设施Governance practices and their apparatus
保障活动Assurance activities
通过评估、红队测试、审计和符合性测试等活动，建立有充分依据的信心，确认智能体系统具备预期的安全性、可靠性和治理属性。 Activities that build justified confidence an agent system meets its intended safety, reliability, and governance properties: evaluation, red-teaming, audit, conformance testing.
证明（attestation）Attestation
关于智能体（例如模型版本、能力边界、权限集、防护措施集）、工具或制品（例如发布来源），或某项内容（例如作者身份）的可验证声明，交易对方无需仅凭发布者的一面之词给予信任即可使用该声明。 A verifiable claim about an agent (e.g. model version, capability bounds, permission set, guardrail set), a tool or artefact (e.g. release provenance), or a piece of content (e.g. authorship) that a counterparty can use without trusting the issuer on its word.
归因Attribution
将某一结果的因果责任归于通过自身行为造成该结果的智能体及委托方。 Assigning causal responsibility for an outcome to the agents and principals whose actions produced it. 支撑工具与设施：执行日志、关联基础设施、身份基础设施。 Apparatus: execution logs, linking infrastructure, identity infrastructure.
授权Authorisation
确保每个智能体均获委托方授权采取行动，且权限范围在整个委托过程和生命周期中均得到正确界定。 Ensuring each agent is sanctioned to act by a principal, with permissions correctly scoped across delegation and lifecycle. 支撑工具与设施：权限集、策略执行、委托控制措施、撤销控制措施、身份基础设施。 Apparatus: permission sets, policy enforcement, delegation controls, revocation controls, identity infrastructure.
评估Evaluation
在部署时及此后的持续运行过程中，确定系统及其智能体是否适合预定用途。 Establishing whether the system and its agents are fit for purpose, at deployment and continuously. 支撑工具与设施：智能体级、系统级和对抗性评估；持续监测。 Apparatus: agent-level, system-level, and adversarial evaluation; continuous monitoring.
身份管理Identity management
为多智能体系统中的智能体提供唯一且稳定的身份。 Providing unique stable identities for agents across a multi-agent system. 支撑工具与设施：标识符、凭证、委托方绑定关系、证明。 Apparatus: identifiers, credentials, principal bindings, attestations. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 110 > | industry .gov.au 110
监督Oversight
持续保持对智能体活动的可见性，使承担运行责任的人类能够理解这些活动并进行干预。 Maintaining visibility over agent activity so operationally responsible humans can interpret and intervene. 装置：监测仪器、干预点、行为监测、身份基础设施。 Apparatus: monitoring instrumentation, intervention points, behavioural monitoring, identity infrastructure.
标准Standards
用于评估智能体系统符合性的外部参考框架：ISO/IEC 42001（AI 管理体系）、ISO/IEC 23894（AI 风险管理）、NIST AI RMF、AIUC-1 以及行业框架。 External reference frameworks against which agent systems are assessed for conformance: ISO/IEC 42001 (AI management systems), ISO/IEC 23894 (AI risk management), NIST AI RMF, AIUC-1, and sectoral frameworks.
效度Validity
评估结果能够预测部署后行为的程度；当测试环境偏离部署条件时（如采用简化假设、使用建模的交互对方、环境发生变化），效度会受到削弱。 The degree to which an evaluation result predicts deployment behaviour, undermined when the test environment diverges from deployment conditions (simplifying assumptions, modelled counterparties, changing environments).
风险因素Risk factors
缺乏具有召集权威的主体Absence of convening authority
没有任何中央主体具备相应权威，能够强制签发身份凭证、执行关停、维持通信的可理解性，或以其他方式在整个底层环境中制定规则；只有在自愿参与者构建装置的地方，这些装置才会存在，因此其对智能体群体的覆盖从根本上就是不完整的。 No central party has the standing to mandate identity issuance, enforce shutdown, maintain communication legibility, or otherwise set rules across the substrate; apparatus exists only where voluntary participants build it, and coverage of the agent population is incomplete by construction.
适应压力Adaptation pressures
在不同群体及反复交互中，无论委托人的意图如何，交互结果都会使能够攫取更多价值或在竞争中胜过同类的策略受到选择。 Across populations and over repeated interactions, outcomes select for strategies that extract more value or outcompete peers, regardless of principal intent.
智能体吞吐量超出人工审查能力Agent throughput exceeding human review
智能体采取行动的速度和规模超出了人工审查者能够进行实质性审查的能力。 Agents act faster and at a greater scale than human reviewers can meaningfully scrutinise.
跨边界不可逆性Cross-boundary irreversibility
一旦某项行动被提交执行，或数据被传输到组织边界之外，发起组织就无法单方面将其撤销；寻求补救只能按照人工或法律程序的速度进行。 Once an action is committed or data is transferred across an organisational boundary, the originating organisation cannot unilaterally reverse it; recourse runs at human or legal speed. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 111 > | industry .gov.au 111
从众偏差Conformity bias
经训练而倾向于迎合他人的 LLMs 所具有的一种倾向，这种倾向使智能体在群体中朝着表面共识调整判断，而非进行独立核验。 A disposition in LLMs trained to be agreeable that, in a group, causes agents to update towards apparent consensus rather than independent verification.
大规模相关性决策Correlated decision-making at scale
许多相互独立的智能体在没有明确协调的情况下，依据共同信号采取行动，从而同时作出相同决策。 Many independent agents reach the same decision at the same time by acting on common signals, with no explicit coordination.
跨组织不透明性Cross-organisational opacity
在组织边界处，交互对方的智能体和治理监测工具都无法跨越边界发挥作用；各组织的日志、身份、IAM 和监测机制均位于各自一侧，默认情况下无法组合运作。 At organisational boundaries, neither the counterparty's agents nor the governance instrumentation reach across; per-organisation logs, identity, IAM and monitoring sit on their own side of the boundary and don't compose by default.
交互对方不透明性Counterparty opacity
无法事先确定交互对方的身份、能力、惯例和意图，也没有任何中介权威机构为其作担保。 Counterparty identity, capability, conventions, and intent cannot be established in advance, and no mediating authority vouches for them.
底层环境的动态扩展Dynamic substrate extension
智能体的行动空间可以在运行时扩展，纳入超出设计阶段治理范围的通信渠道、工具或交互对方提供的引用内容。 An agent's action space can grow at runtime to include channels, tools, or counterparty-supplied references beyond what was governed at design time.
分布式多智能体状态Distributed multi-agent state
执行上下文、推理和决策分散在多个智能体上下文及交接环节中，没有任何单一记录能够呈现全貌。 Execution context, reasoning, and decision-making are spread across multiple agent contexts and handoffs, with no single record holding the whole picture.
涌现的多智能体行为Emergent multi-agent behaviour
智能体之间的交互会产生任何单个智能体在独立运行时都不会表现出的行为。 Behaviours arise from interaction between agents that no single agent exhibits in isolation.
涌现规范的形成与锁定Emergent-norm formation and lock-in
一旦有足够多的智能体遵循某些非正式协调模式，这些模式就会固化。 Informal coordination patterns become entrenched once enough agents follow them. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 112 > | industry .gov.au 112
基础设施暴露Infrastructure exposure
用于为智能体作担保的验证层本身可能遭到这些智能体的攻击或控制。 The verification layer used to vouch for agents can itself be attacked or captured by those agents.
信息与能力不对称Information and capability asymmetries
智能体在进入交互时拥有的信息或能力并不均等，而这种不对称可能在反复交互中不断累积。 Agents enter interactions with unequal information or capability, which can compound across repeated interactions.
智能体间委托Inter-agent delegation
智能体可以请求、授权或创建其他智能体代其行动，而接收请求的智能体往往会将上游请求视为已经获得批准。 Agents can request, authorise, or spawn other agents to act on their behalf, and receiving agents tend to treat upstream requests as sanctioned.
混合动机动态Mixed-motive dynamics
各智能体在任务成功这一点上具有共同利益，但在价值如何分配的问题上存在分歧。 Agents share interest in the task succeeding but diverge on how value is distributed.
模型同质化Model monoculture
多个智能体共享同一个基础模型、训练源流或提示，从而具有相互关联的盲点和错误。 Multiple agents share a base model, training heritage, or prompt, giving them correlated blind spots and errors.
自然语言交接Natural-language handoffs
智能体传递和解读自由形式的文本，而非采用结构化模式。 Agents passing and interpreting free-form text rather than structured schemas.
智能体的持续存在Persistent agent presence
智能体成为长期存在的实体，其资源、声誉和信息的积累超越任何单次部署决策的范围。 Agents become standing entities that accumulate resources, reputation, and information beyond any single deployment decision.
语义分歧Semantic divergence
交互各方使用相互重叠的词汇（字段名、状态标志、单位），但赋予这些词汇不同的操作含义。 Counterparties use overlapping vocabulary (field names, status flags, units) with different operational meanings.
单用户训练Single-user training
LLMs 通常针对单一用户和单一上下文进行训练，无法可靠地区分和隔离多个交互方或委托人的信息。 LLMs are typically trained for one user and one context, and do not reliably separate information across multiple counterparties or principals. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 113 > | industry .gov.au 113
规范与执行之间的差距Specification-execution gap
委托人编写指令时的意图与智能体在运行时对该指令的解读之间的差距。 The gap between what a principal intended when writing an instruction and how the agent interpreted it at runtime.
底层环境耦合Substrate coupling
智能体通过共享环境（痕迹协作，stigmergy）或将输出用作输入的链条间接关联，而非通过直接消息关联。 Agents are linked indirectly through a shared environment (stigmergy) or output-as-input chains rather than direct messages.
底层环境的响应性Substrate responsiveness
智能体所作用的基础设施本身能够响应智能体的行为而改变形态。 The infrastructure agents act on can itself change shape in response to agent actions.
任务验证缺口Task verification gap
系统中没有任何环节依据用户的整体任务对输出进行验证。 No stage in the system verifies the output against the user's overall task.
失效模式Failure modes
算法合谋Algorithmic collusion
隶属于不同委托人的智能体逐渐形成该框架所禁止的协同行为。 Agents from different principals converge on coordinated behaviour the framework prohibits. 显性合谋——就策略进行公开沟通。 Explicit collusion – open communication about strategy. 默契合谋——通过观察彼此的行为而趋于一致。 Tacit collusion –convergence from observing each other's actions. 隐写式合谋——通过规避检测的渠道秘密传递信号。 Steganographic collusion – covert signalling through channels that evade detection.
归责链断裂Broken attribution chain
由于不存在跨越组织边界的共享身份或审计基础设施，无法重建对跨组织结果进行归责所需的可追溯关系、委托人绑定关系或责任分配。 The traceability, principal binding, or liability allocation needed to attribute a cross-org outcome cannot be reconstructed because no shared identity or audit infrastructure spans the boundary.
级联感染／提示感染Cascading infection/prompt infection
嵌入智能体通信中的恶意指令在智能体之间自我复制，受入侵的智能体还可能改变攻击载荷的形态。 Malicious instructions embedded in agent communications self-replicate agent-to-agent, with the compromised agent potentially morphing the payload. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 114 > | industry .gov.au 114
通过共享基础设施发生的级联感染Cascading infection through shared infrastructure
恶意或错误内容通过共享工具注册表、模型依赖项、可观测信号或声誉系统，在没有直接关系的智能体之间传播。 Malicious or erroneous content propagates between agents with no direct relationship, through shared tool registries, model dependencies, observable signals, or reputation systems.
级联可靠性失效Cascading reliability failures
一个智能体的错误输出被另一个智能体作为有效输入接受，并向下游传播，其影响往往在每一步都进一步扩大。 An erroneous output from one agent is accepted as valid input by another and propagates downstream, often with broader consequence at each step.
因果归因失败Causal attribution failure
无法确定链条中的哪个步骤或哪个智能体产生了某一结果。 Failure to identify which step or agent in a chain produced an outcome. 子机制：可追溯性、分类、分散因果关系。 Sub-mechanisms: traceability, classification, diffuse causation.
分散因果关系Diffuse causation
因果归因失效的一种子机制：不存在单一的决定性步骤，因为损害源于多个智能体各自采取的合理行动相互叠加。 Sub-mechanism of causal attribution failure: no single decisive step exists because the harm emerged from many individually reasonable actions composing across agents.
共享资源管理失效Shared resource management failure
多个智能体理性地追求各自委托人的目标，却共同导致有限的共享资源退化。 Multiple agents rationally pursuing principal-level goals collectively degrade a finite shared resource.
隐藏能力的评估局限Concealed-capability evaluation limit
评估可以证明某种能力的存在，但当对方有动机隐藏该能力时，无法排除其不存在的可能性。 Evaluation can demonstrate presence of a capability but cannot rule out absence when the counterparty has incentive to hide it.
混淆代理人（Confused deputy）Confused deputy
没有权限的智能体说服拥有权限的智能体代其行动；拥有特权的智能体将上游请求视为已获授权。 An agent without permissions persuades an agent that has them to act on its behalf; the privileged agent accepts the upstream request as sanctioned.
上下文泄露／智能体情境隐私泄露Context leakage/agentic contextual privacy leakage
代表某一委托人处理的数据，通过共享上下文的智能体或流水线通道，出现在另一委托人的上下文中。 Data processed on behalf of one principal surfaces in another's context, via shared-context agents or pipeline conduits. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 115 > | industry .gov.au 115
协同欺骗Coordinated deception
通过隐写术串通、智能体相互掩护，或智能体群体趋同于旨在规避监督界面的策略。 Steganographic collusion, agents covering for each other, or populations converging on strategies that defeat the oversight surface intentionally.
数据义务履行失效Data obligation failure
上下文泄露此时跨越组织或司法管辖区边界，违反了数据所附带的义务（同意、监管保护、合同保密）。 Context leakage now crosses organisational or jurisdictional boundaries, breaching obligations (consent, regulatory protection, contractual confidentiality) attached to the data.
欺骗性议价策略Deceptive bargaining strategies
在运行时涌现、利用对方信任的策略，包括廉价言谈（cheap talk） Strategies that emerge at runtime to exploit a counterparty's trust, including cheap talk
（通过歪曲自身立场来促成协议）和最后关头的背叛（先建立信任，再在利害攸关时背弃合作）。 (misrepresenting position to secure agreement) and last-mile betrayal (building trust, then defecting at high stakes).
破坏稳定的动态过程Destabilising dynamics
许多智能体同时独立响应同一信号，产生同步激增的行为（挤兑、切换、退出），使其所作用的底层环境失稳，类似于闪电崩盘。 Many agents independently respond to the same signal at the same time, producing a synchronised surge (run, switch, withdrawal) that destabilises the substrate they act on, similar to flash crashes.
涌现的集体能动性Emergent collective agency
能力有限的智能体群体通过交互，集体展现出任何单个智能体都不具备的能力、目标或策略行为。 Populations of bounded agents collectively exhibit capability, goals, or strategic behaviour that no single agent has, arising from interaction.
评估者的虚假共识Evaluator false consensus
基于 LLM 的评估者与被评估智能体产生相关性错误，或对其表现出迎合倾向，从而损害评估工具的有效性。 An LLM-based evaluator makes correlated errors with – or is sycophantic towards – the agents it is evaluating, undermining the evaluation instrument.
虚假共识False consensus
智能体通过结构性路径（同质化群体中的相关性错误）或动态路径（多轮交互中的迎合性趋同），传播并强化共同的错误结论。 Agents propagate and reinforce a shared incorrect conclusion through either a structural pathway (correlated errors across a monoculture) or a dynamic pathway (sycophantic convergence over rounds).
智能体间通信失效Inter-agent communication failures
协同执行任务的智能体基于对上下文的不一致理解采取行动；具体表现包括发送方出错、接收方误解，或未加说明地填补信息缺口。 Agents coordinating on a task act on inconsistent interpretations of context; sender errors, receiver misinterpretations, or silent gap-filling. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 116 > | industry .gov.au 116
联合评估缺口Joint evaluation gap
对于组件由其他主体运营的组合式多智能体系统，任何部署组织都无法单方面对其进行评估。 No deploying organisation can unilaterally evaluate a composed multi-agent system whose components are operated by others.
可理解性崩溃Legibility collapse
智能体间的协调逐渐偏离人类可读的形式，转而采用涌现的简写和压缩信号，因为没有任何一方负责在整个底层环境的规模上维持可理解性。 Inter-agent coordination drifts away from human-readable formats – emergent shorthand, compressed signalling – because no party is charged with maintaining comprehensibility at substrate scale.
跨组织交接中的沟通偏差Miscommunication at cross-org handoffs
即使使用了模式（schema），接收智能体仍会依据与发送方理解不一致的信息解读采取行动。 A receiving agent acts on an interpretation of the sender's information that does not match the sender's, even when a schema is in use.
缺乏身份签发机构No issuing authority
在开放环境中，没有任何一方有资格为整个智能体群体签发身份，因此，对于不具备可验证身份的那部分智能体，归因机制会失效，且智能体可以通过换用新标识符重新出现来背弃合作。 No party in an open environment has the standing to issue identities across the agent population, so attribution collapses for the portion that carries no verifiable identity, and agents can defect by re-presenting under a fresh identifier.
监督不可解读性Oversight illegibility
由于信息的分布方式、格式或隐写适应，人类监督者无法理解信息在交接环节之间的流动方式。 The way information flows across handoffs becomes illegible to the human overseer, by distribution, format, or steganographic adaptation.
监督饱和Oversight saturation
智能体的活动量超出审查者的注意力承载能力，导致自动化偏误 Reviewer attention is overwhelmed by agent activity volume, producing automation bias
（对自动化系统的过度信任）和同意疲劳（对反复提交的审批例行盖章放行）。 (over-trust of automated systems) and consent fatigue (rubber-stamping repeated approvals).
群体规模的监测缺口Population-scale monitoring gap
智能体群体中涌现的模式——破坏稳定的动态、传染、串谋——无法从任何单一参与者的视角观察到。 Patterns emerging from a population of agents – Destabilising dynamics, contagion, collusion – are not observable from any single participant's vantage point.
委托主体归责失败Principal attribution failure
已识别出有过错的智能体，却无法追溯到应承担责任的委托主体。 An at-fault agent is identified but cannot be resolved back to an accountable principal. 发生路径：共享凭证、多用户智能体、身份基础设施缺口。 Pathways: shared credentials, multi-user agents, identity-infrastructure gap. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 117 > | industry .gov.au 117
失控复制Rogue replication
智能体获得自我复制、获取计算资源和规避关停的能力，从而形成一个超出任何部署组织同意范围的自我维持群体。 An agent acquires the ability to copy itself, secure compute, and evade shutdown, producing a self-sustaining population beyond the consent of any deploying organisation.
共同理解漂移Shared-understanding drift
随着智能体相互适应，多智能体系统的共同参照体系（如词汇、惯例以及对各类产物的处理方式）逐渐与真实情况脱节，且无法定位任何初始错误或决策节点。 A multi-agent system's shared reference (such as vocabulary, conventions, and treatment of artefacts) decouples from ground truth over time as agents adapt to each other, with no seed error or decision point to locate.
单智能体评估缺口Single-agent evaluation gap
由单独来看均安全的智能体组成的系统，在生产环境中因智能体层面的评估无法检测到的涌现动态而发生失效。 A system of individually safe agents fails in production through emergent dynamics that agent-level evaluations cannot detect.
底层基底俘获Substrate capture
一部分智能体积累了对底层基底——其奖励动态、验证基础设施或运行规范——的不成比例的影响力，使该基底体现它们的利益。 A subset of agents accumulates disproportionate influence over the substrate – its reward dynamics, verification infrastructure, or operative norms – such that the substrate reflects their interests.
底层基底逃逸Substrate escape
智能体在运行时离开联邦式底层基底——例如访问交互对方提供的 URL、调用外部工具——从而进入开放环境中运行。 An agent leaves the federated substrate at runtime – following a counterparty-supplied URL, invoking an external tool – and finds itself operating in an open environment.
底层基底变化／基准过时Substrate shift/stale benchmarks
在部署时校准的基准失去有效性，因为其测试所依据的底层基底已被智能体活动重塑。 Benchmarks calibrated at deployment time lose validity because the substrate they tested against has been reshaped by agent activity.
女巫攻击（Sybil attack）Sybil attack
单一主体通过开放身份层伪装成多个表面上相互独立的交互方，制造多样性的假象。 A single party presents as many ostensibly independent counterparties through the open identity layer, manufacturing the appearance of diversity.
未经授权的子智能体实例Unauthorised sub-agent instances
监督智能体被终止，但其生成的子智能体仍继续运行，既不可见，也不受治理。 A supervisor is terminated but the sub-agents it spawned continue running, invisible and ungoverned. 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 118 > | industry .gov.au 118
未经批准的交易Unsanctioned transactions
智能体作出超出当前权限的、具有约束力的跨组织承诺，通常是因为仅在工作流开始时检查了权限，而未在采取行动时重新核验。 An agent makes a binding cross-org commitment beyond current authority, often because authority was checked at workflow start and not re-verified at the point of action.
控制措施与机制Controls and mechanisms
熔断机制Circuit breaker
在越过阈值后，通过局部（智能体层面的速率限制）或覆盖整个底层基底（市场暂停）的自动停止机制，限制失效所造成的后果。 An automated halt – local (agent-level rate limits) or substrate-wide (market pauses) –that bounds the consequences of a failure once thresholds are crossed.
去中心化标识符（DID）／可验证凭证（VC）Decentralised identifier (DID)/Verifiable credential (VC)
用于在不依赖单一可信签发方的情况下，通过密码学验证身份和凭证的 W3C 标准；是开放环境中去中心化身份的底层基础。 W3C standards for cryptographically verifiable identity and credentials without a single trusted issuer; the substrate for decentralised identity in open environments.
联邦式审计基础设施Federated audit infrastructure
跨组织审计将各组织的日志片段组合成可显露篡改痕迹的追踪记录，支持跨边界归责。 Cross-organisational audit composing per-organisation log segments into a tamper-evident trace, supporting attribution across boundaries.
蜜罐评估Honeypot evaluation
一种在保持其他所有条件不变的情况下改变所声明条件（例如“你正受到监控”）的探测方法，用于识别根据其感知到的监督情况调整行为的智能体。 A probe varying stated conditions (such as ‘you are being monitored’) while holding everything else equal, to detect agents that modulate behaviour based on perceived oversight.
联合红队测试/联合评估Joint red-teaming/joint evaluation
多个组织在共享环境中对组合系统开展的评估，是对任何单一参与方都无法独立完成的系统级评估的联邦式替代方案。 Multi-organisation evaluation of the composed system in a shared environment, the federated substitute for system-level evaluation that no single participant can perform alone.
载荷（Payload）Payload
智能体之间的通信、交接或传输中所承载的内容。 The content carried inside an agent-to-agent communication, handoff, or transmission. 在安全语境中，载荷可能是恶意的：它是一条隐蔽指令，接收方智能体在解释该指令时会被诱导执行未经授权的操作，而且该指令可能向下游传播（参见级联感染）。 In a security context the payload may be malicious: a concealed instruction that induces unsanctioned action when interpreted by a receiving agent and may propagate downstream (see cascading infection). 多智能体系统的风险与控制措施 Risks and controls for multi-agent systems
> | industry .gov.au 119 > | industry .gov.au 119
引用间接寻址/数据令牌化Reference indirection/data tokenisation
智能体使用占位令牌进行操作；可信执行器在出口处将令牌替换为真实值，因此智能体从不持有可能被披露的数据。 Agents operate on placeholder tokens; a trusted executor substitutes in real values at egress, so the agent never holds the disclosable data.
声誉系统Reputation system
汇总智能体过去行为的信息并向交互对方提供这些信息的基础设施，其运行依赖于持久身份。 Infrastructure that aggregates information about an agent's past conduct and exposes it to counterparties, depending on persistent identity to function.
回滚/取消窗口Rollback/cancellation window
一种恢复控制措施，使委托方在承诺固化为可强制执行的义务之前，能够在限定时间内撤销该承诺。 Recovery control that gives principals time-bounded reversibility before a commitment hardens into an enforceable obligation.
结构化交接Structured handoffs
为智能体之间传递的输出预先规定的模式，使交接点能够进行验证、日志记录和评估。 Pre-specified schemas for outputs passed between agents, enabling validation, logging, and evaluation at the handoff point. 一种基础性的跨层级控制措施。 A foundational cross-tier control.
污点追踪（Taint tracing）Taint tracing
一种信息流技术，为数据标注其来源，并在各个阶段传递该标记，以支持事后识别安全失陷的源头。 Information-flow technique tagging data with its origin and propagating the tag through every stage, supporting post-hoc identification of a compromise source.
视为不可信（安全立场）Treat-as-untrusted (posture)
默认假定能力足够强的智能体——无论是己方还是交互对方的——都可能已遭入侵，并据此采用遏制型控制措施。 Default assumption that sufficiently capable agents – own or counterparty – could be compromised, with containment-style controls applied accordingly.
Web Bot AuthWeb Bot Auth
一项在 HTTP 层提供密码学信号的标准草案，采用该标准的服务可借此区分智能体流量与人类用户流量。 Draft standard providing a cryptographic signal at the HTTP layer that lets services distinguish agent traffic from human traffic, where adopted.
